    77→});
    78→
    79→
    80→// Helper function to generate accessible URLs for GCS files
    81→// Videos use public bucket access, images try signed URLs with fallback
    82→async function getSignedUrl(gcsUrl: string, expiresInMinutes: number = 60): Promise<string> {
    83→    // If it's not a GCS URL, return as-is (e.g., external URLs like Unsplash)
    84→    if (!gcsUrl || !gcsUrl.startsWith('gs://') && !gcsUrl.includes('storage.googleapis.com')) {
    85→        return gcsUrl;
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    let bucketName: string;
    89→    let fileName: string;
    90→
    91→    // Parse GCS URL format: gs://bucket/file or https://storage.googleapis.com/bucket/file
    92→    if (gcsUrl.startsWith('gs://')) {
    93→        const parts = gcsUrl.replace('gs://', '').split('/');
    94→        bucketName = parts[0];
    95→        fileName = parts.slice(1).join('/');
    96→    } else if (gcsUrl.includes('storage.googleapis.com')) {
    97→        const url = new URL(gcsUrl);
    98→        const pathParts = url.pathname.split('/').filter(p => p);
    99→        bucketName = pathParts[0];
   100→        fileName = pathParts.slice(1).join('/');
   101→    } else {
   102→        return gcsUrl;
   103→    }
   104→
   105→    // Both video and image buckets are public - return public URLs
   106→    if (bucketName === VIDEO_BUCKET || bucketName === IMAGE_BUCKET) {
   107→        return `https://storage.googleapis.com/${bucketName}/${fileName}`;
   108→    }
   109→
   110→    // For other buckets, try signed URL with fallback to public URL
   111→    try {
   112→        const [signedUrl] = await storage
   113→            .bucket(bucketName)
   114→            .file(fileName)
   115→            .getSignedUrl({
   116→                version: 'v4',
   117→                action: 'read',
   118→                expires: Date.now() + expiresInMinutes * MILLISECONDS_PER_MINUTE,
   119→            });
   120→        return signedUrl;
   121→    } catch (error: any) {
   122→        // Log the actual error for debugging
   123→        console.warn('GCS signing failed:', fileName, error.message || error);
   124→        // Return public URL as fallback
   125→        return `https://storage.googleapis.com/${bucketName}/${fileName}`;
   126→    }
   127→}
   128→
   129→// Helper to process entities and replace GCS URLs with signed URLs
   130→async function signMediaUrls(entity: any, fields: string[]): Promise<any> {
   131→    const result = { ...entity };
   132→    for (const field of fields) {
   133→        if (result[field] && typeof result[field] === 'string') {
   134→            result[field] = await getSignedUrl(result[field]);
   135→        }
   136→    }
   137→    return result;
   138→}
   139→
   140→// Process array of entities
   141→async function signMediaUrlsArray(entities: any[], fields: string[]): Promise<any[]> {
   142→    return Promise.all(entities.map(entity => signMediaUrls(entity, fields)));
   143→}
   144→
   145→// Multer for file uploads (memory storage for direct GCP upload)
   146→const upload = multer({
   147→    storage: multer.memoryStorage(),
   148→    limits: {
   149→        fileSize: MAX_FILE_UPLOAD_SIZE_BYTES,
   150→    }
   151→});
   152→
   153→// Run database migrations
   154→await runMigrations();
   155→
   156→// Helper to get user ID from JWT token
   157→interface AuthenticatedRequest extends express.Request {
   158→    userId?: number;
   159→    user?: any;
   160→}
   161→
   162→// Middleware for body parsing (for token instroctpection)
   163→app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));
   164→
   165→// Authentication middleware - now imported from auth.middleware.ts
   166→// Supports both cookie-based JWT auth and Bearer token auth
   167→import { authMiddleware } from './middleware/auth.middleware.js';
   168→
   169→// Swagger setup
   170→app.get('/docs/swagger.json', (req, res) => {
   171→    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
   172→    res.send(swaggerSpec);
   173→});
   174→app.use('/docs', swaggerUi.serve);
   175→app.get('/docs', swaggerUi.setup(swaggerSpec, { explorer: true }));
   176→
   177→// Apply auth middleware to all routes
   178→app.use(authMiddleware);
   179→
   180→// Helper function to get user ID safely
   181→const getUserId = (req: AuthenticatedRequest): number | undefined => {
   182→    return req.userId;
   183→};
   184→

   188→setupRoutes(app);

   196→
   197→// Serve static frontend files in production
   198→if (NODE_ENV === 'production') {
   199→    const distPath = path.join(__dirname, '../dist');
   200→
   201→    // Serve static assets
   202→    app.use(express.static(distPath));
   203→
   204→    // SPA fallback - serve index.html for all non-API routes
   205→    // Use a middleware instead of wildcard route for Express 5 compatibility
   206→    app.use((req, res, next) => {
   207→        // Only serve index.html if no other route handled the request
   208→        if (!res.headersSent) {
   209→            res.sendFile(path.join(distPath, 'index.html'));
   210→        } else {
   211→            next();
   212→        }
   213→    });
   214→
   215→    console.log(`📦 Serving static frontend from ${distPath}`);
   216→}
   217→

   221→    console.log(`🌍 Environment: ${NODE_ENV}`);

{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-tic/IQ_PORTAL_MIGRATION_GUIDE.md",
  "content": "# IQ Portal — Migration Guide to kix-infrastructure\n\n**Service**: kon-iq-portal → `kix-usecase-iq-portal`\n**Target Org**: `konecta-ix-workloads`\n**Phase**: P0 (Pilot)\n**Status**: Draft\n\n---\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n1. [Current State Assessment](#1-current-state-assessment)\n2. [Target State](#2-target-state)\n3. [Pre-requisites](#3-pre-requisites)\n4. [Step 1 — Create the New Repository](#step-1--create-the-new-repository)\n5. [Step 2 — Application Code Changes](#step-2--application-code-changes)\n6. [Step 3 — Dockerfile Changes](#step-3--dockerfile-changes)\n7. [Step 4 — Helm Chart](#step-4--helm-chart)\n8. [Step 5 — GitHub Actions CI Workflow](#step-5--github-actions-ci-workflow)\n9. [Step 6 — Secrets Migration](#step-6--secrets-migration)\n10. [Step 7 — GitOps Configuration](#step-7--gitops-configuration)\n11. [Step 8 — Database Migration](#step-8--database-migration)\n12. [Step 9 — Verification & Smoke Tests](#step-9--verification--smoke-tests)\n13. [Step 10 — DNS & Domain Cutover](#step-10--dns--domain-cutover)\n14. [Appendix A — Full Environment Variable Mapping](#appendix-a--full-environment-variable-mapping)\n15. [Appendix B — Rollback Procedure](#appendix-b--rollback-procedure)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Current State Assessment\n\n### Architecture\n\nIQ Portal is a **single-container monolith**: an Express.js (TypeScript) backend serves both the API (`/api/*`) and a pre-generated Nuxt 4 SPA (static files from `/dist`). Everything runs on port **8080** in production.\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Cloud Run (kon-iq-portal)          │\n│                                     │\n│  Express.js :8080                   │\n│  ├── /api/*  → API routes           │\n│  ├── /docs   → Swagger UI           │\n│  └── /*      → Static Nuxt SPA      │\n│                                     │\n│  Cloud SQL (Unix socket)            │\n│  /cloudsql/project:region:instance  │\n└─────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Current Tech Stack\n\n| Component | Detail |\n|-----------|--------|\n| Runtime | Node.js 22 (Alpine) |\n| Frontend | Nuxt 4 (SPA, `ssr: false`, pre-generated static) |\n| Backend | Express 5 (TypeScript, via `tsx`) |\n| Database | PostgreSQL on Cloud SQL (`kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform`) |\n| DB Connection | Unix socket via VPC connector (`DB_HOST=/cloudsql/...`) |\n| Migrations | Custom SQL/TS runner (33 migrations, runs on startup) |\n| Auth | Google OAuth → JWT in HttpOnly cookies |\n| Storage | GCS (3 buckets: images, videos, RAG docs) |\n| AI | Vertex AI (Gemini) + RAG corpus |\n| Async Jobs | Cloud Tasks → kon-rag-ingestion |\n| Email | ChannelHub (external Cognito-auth API) |\n| CI/CD | Cloud Build from Bitbucket |\n| Port | 8080 (production), 3002 (dev) |\n\n### GCP Service Dependencies\n\n| Service | Project | Used For |\n|---------|---------|----------|\n| Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL) | `kd-lab-464110` | Primary database |\n| Cloud Storage | `kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev` | Image/video/RAG file storage |\n| Vertex AI | `kd-lab-464110` | Gemini LLM + RAG retrieval |\n| Cloud Tasks | `kd-lab-464110` | Async RAG document ingestion |\n| Secret Manager | `kd-lab-464110` | JWT secret, DB password, RAG token |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Target State\n\n```\n┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  kix-infrastructure GKE Cluster                                   │\n│                                                                   │\n│  Namespace: dev-api-services (then qa-api-services, prod-...)     │\n│                                                                   │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐      │\n│  │  Pod: kix-usecase-iq-portal                             │      │\n│  │                                                         │      │\n│  │  ┌──────────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────────┐   │      │\n│  │  │  app (Express)   │   │  cloud-sql-proxy sidecar  │   │      │\n│  │  │  :8080           │──▶│  :5432 (TCP)              │   │      │\n│  │  │  /api/* + SPA    │   │  --private-ip             │   │      │\n│  │  └──────────────────┘   └──────────────────────────┘   │      │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘      │\n│      │                                                            │\n│      ▼                                                            │\n│  Service: kix-usecase-iq-portal :8080                             │\n│      │                         │                                  │\n│      ▼                         ▼                                  │\n│  Kong Ingress              GKE Gateway API                        │\n│  /api/iq-portal/*          iq-portal.app.konecta.io               │\n│  (API consumers)           (browser users via IAP)                │\n└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Key Differences from Cloud Run\n\n| Aspect | Cloud Run | kix-infrastructure |\n|--------|-----------|-------------------|\n| DB connection | Unix socket via VPC connector | Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (TCP `localhost:5432`) |\n| Secrets | Secret Manager direct access | External Secrets Operator → K8s Secrets |\n| Scaling | 0-10 instances | HPA 1-5 replicas |\n| Ingress | Direct HTTPS | Dual: Kong (API) + Gateway API + IAP (frontend) |\n| CI/CD | Cloud Build + Bitbucket | GitHub Actions + ArgoCD |\n| Health checks | Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK | K8s liveness/readiness probes |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Pre-requisites\n\nBefore starting, ensure:\n\n- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-workloads` GitHub org (write)\n- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure` GitOps repo (write)\n- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows` (read)\n- [ ] `kix-platform-modules` base Helm chart published to Artifact Registry\n- [ ] ArgoCD ApplicationSet configured with git directory generator\n- [ ] Shared Kong gateway operational in `shared-kong` namespace\n- [ ] GKE Gateway API + IAP configured on frontend LB\n- [ ] External Secrets Operator running in `shared-secrets` namespace\n- [ ] GCP Workload Identity pool configured for GitHub Actions OIDC\n- [ ] Cloud SQL instance accessible from GKE cluster (private IP, same VPC)\n- [ ] GCP service account for IQ Portal workload (`kix-iq-portal@<project>.iam.gserviceaccount.com`) with IAM roles:\n  - `roles/cloudsql.client`\n  - `roles/storage.objectAdmin` (for GCS buckets)\n  - `roles/aiplatform.user` (for Vertex AI)\n  - `roles/cloudtasks.enqueuer` (for Cloud Tasks)\n  - `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor` (for migration 00024)\n\n---\n\n## Step 1 — Create the New Repository\n\n### 1.1 Create repo from template\n\n```bash\ngh repo create konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal \\\n  --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \\\n  --private\n```\n\n### 1.2 Clone and set up\n\n```bash\ngit clone git@github.com:konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal.git\ncd kix-usecase-iq-portal\n```\n\n### 1.3 Copy application code\n\nCopy the entire application source from the current `kon-iq-portal` repo into the new repo root. The final structure should be:\n\n```\nkix-usecase-iq-portal/\n├── backend/              # Express.js API (existing)\n├── src/                  # Nuxt 4 frontend (existing)\n├── public/               # Static assets (existing)\n├── scripts/              # Deployment scripts (existing)\n├── nuxt.config.ts        # (existing)\n├── package.json          # (existing)\n├── package-lock.json     # (existing)\n├── tsconfig*.json        # (existing)\n├── version.txt           # (existing)\n├── Dockerfile            # (modified — see Step 3)\n├── docker-entrypoint.sh  # (existing, no changes needed)\n├── .dockerignore         # (existing or create)\n├── chart/                # (new — Helm chart, see Step 4)\n│   ├── Chart.yaml\n│   └── values.yaml\n├── .github/              # (new — CI workflow, see Step 5)\n│   └── workflows/\n│       └── ci.yaml\n├── CODEOWNERS            # (new)\n└── .env.example          # (existing, for reference only)\n```\n\n### 1.4 Remove Cloud Build config\n\nDelete the `cloudbuild.yaml` file — it is replaced by GitHub Actions.\n\n```bash\nrm cloudbuild.yaml\n```\n\n### 1.5 Add CODEOWNERS\n\n```\n# Default owners\n* @konecta-ix-workloads/iq-portal-team\n```\n\n### 1.6 Add .dockerignore\n\nIf not already present, create `.dockerignore`:\n\n```\nnode_modules\n.nuxt\n.output\ndist\n.git\n.github\nchart\ndocs\n*.md\n.env*\n!.env.example\n```\n\n---\n\n## Step 2 — Application Code Changes\n\n### 2.1 Database connection — switch from Unix socket to TCP localhost\n\nThe Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar exposes the database on `localhost:5432` (TCP), replacing the Unix socket path used on Cloud Run.\n\n**No code changes required.** The existing `database.ts` already supports both modes:\n\n```typescript\n// backend/db/database.ts — already handles both\nconst pool = process.env.DATABASE_URL\n    ? new Pool({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL })\n    : new Pool({\n        host: process.env.DB_HOST || 'localhost',  // was /cloudsql/... on Cloud Run\n        port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT || '5432'),\n        database: process.env.DB_NAME || 'iqplatform',\n        user: process.env.DB_USER || 'konecta',\n        password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD || 'konecta'\n    });\n```\n\nOn kix, the environment variables will be:\n```\nDB_HOST=localhost        # Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar\nDB_PORT=5432\n```\n\nThis is the default — so the app will work without any code change.\n\n### 2.2 Add a root-level health endpoint\n\nThe Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK probes `/health`, but the app only has `/api/health`. Kubernetes probes should hit a lightweight path. Add a simple `/health` alias.\n\n**File: `backend/routes/index.ts`** — add before the existing `/api/health` block:\n\n```typescript\n// ==================== K8S HEALTH PROBES ====================\n// Lightweight endpoint for Kubernetes liveness/readiness probes\n// Separate from /api/health which checks DB + GCP connectivity\napp.get('/health', (req, res) => {\n    res.status(200).json({ status: 'ok' });\n});\n```\n\nThis keeps the existing `/api/health` (with DB + GCP checks) for detailed monitoring, while `/health` serves as a fast liveness probe.\n\n### 2.3 Graceful shutdown (already handled)\n\nThe app already uses `dumb-init` for signal handling. No changes needed — Kubernetes sends `SIGTERM` which `dumb-init` forwards correctly.\n\n### 2.4 MCP Gateway URL update\n\nThe app connects to MCP Gateway at `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` (default `http://localhost:8000`). On kix, this should route through Kong:\n\n```\nMCP_GATEWAY_URL=http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway\n```\n\nThis is an environment variable change only (set in Helm values), no code change.\n\n### 2.5 CORS origins update\n\n`ALLOWED_ORIGINS` needs to include the new domain. This is an environment variable change only:\n\n```\nALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io,https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io\n```\n\n### 2.6 Verify Secret Manager migration dependency\n\nMigration `00024_add_rag_pipeline_token.ts` reads from GCP Secret Manager at startup:\n\n```typescript\n// backend/db/migrations/00024_add_rag_pipeline_token.ts\nconst { SecretManagerServiceClient } = await import('@google-cloud/secret-manager');\n```\n\nThis will work on kix as long as the pod's Workload Identity has `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor`. No code change needed, but the IAM binding is a pre-requisite.\n\n### 2.7 Summary of code changes\n\n| File | Change | Required |\n|------|--------|----------|\n| `backend/routes/index.ts` | Add `/health` endpoint for K8s probes | **Yes** |\n| `cloudbuild.yaml` | Delete | **Yes** |\n| All other source files | No changes | — |\n\nEverything else is handled by environment variable changes in Helm values.\n\n---\n\n## Step 3 — Dockerfile Changes\n\nThe existing Dockerfile is well-structured and mostly suitable for Kubernetes. A few targeted changes:\n\n### 3.1 Pin the base image digest\n\nReplace the mutable tag with a pinned digest for supply chain security.\n\n**Change:**\n```dockerfile\nFROM node:22-alpine AS dependencies\n```\n\n**To:**\n```dockerfile\n# Pin to specific digest — update monthly via Renovate\nFROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<current-digest> AS dependencies\n```\n\nApply the same change to all four `FROM node:22-alpine` lines (dependencies, dev-dependencies, builder, runner).\n\nTo get the current digest:\n```bash\ndocker pull node:22-alpine\ndocker inspect --format='{{index .RepoDigests 0}}' node:22-alpine\n```\n\n### 3.2 Remove the HEALTHCHECK instruction\n\nKubernetes uses its own liveness/readiness probes — the Dockerfile `HEALTHCHECK` is ignored by K8s and adds unnecessary metadata.\n\n**Remove lines 142-157:**\n```dockerfile\n# DELETE THIS BLOCK — K8s probes replace it\nHEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=40s --retries=3 \\\n  CMD node -e \"...\"\n```\n\n### 3.3 Make the port configurable (optional)\n\nThe port is already configurable via the `PORT` env var (default 8080). No change needed.\n\n### 3.4 Summary of Dockerfile changes\n\n| Line(s) | Change | Reason |\n|---------|--------|--------|\n| All `FROM` lines | Pin digest | Supply chain security |\n| 142-157 | Remove `HEALTHCHECK` | K8s probes replace it |\n\nAll other aspects (multi-stage build, non-root user, dumb-init, read-only permissions) are already best practice and should be kept as-is.\n\n---\n\n## Step 4 — Helm Chart\n\nCreate a `chart/` directory in the repo root.\n\n### 4.1 Chart.yaml\n\n```yaml\n# chart/Chart.yaml\napiVersion: v2\nname: kix-usecase-iq-portal\ndescription: Konecta IQ Portal — AI Knowledge Platform\nversion: 0.1.0\ntype: application\n\ndependencies:\n  - name: base-service-chart\n    version: \"~1.0.0\"\n    repository: \"oci://europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/helm-charts\"\n```\n\n### 4.2 values.yaml\n\n```yaml\n# chart/values.yaml\n# Inherits from base-service-chart — only service-specific overrides here\n\nbase-service-chart:\n  nameOverride: kix-usecase-iq-portal\n\n  image:\n    repository: europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/docker/kix-usecase-iq-portal\n    tag: \"latest\"  # overridden by CI pipeline with sha-<commit>\n    pullPolicy: IfNotPresent\n\n  # Single container serving both API + SPA on :8080\n  containers:\n    app:\n      port: 8080\n      env:\n        NODE_ENV: \"production\"\n        PORT: \"8080\"\n        # DB via Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (localhost TCP)\n        DB_HOST: \"localhost\"\n        DB_PORT: \"5432\"\n        DB_NAME: \"iqplatform\"\n        # Non-secret GCP config\n        GCP_PROJECT_ID: \"kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev\"\n        GCP_LOCATION: \"europe-west4\"\n        VERTEX_AI_PROJECT_ID: \"kd-lab-464110\"\n        VERTEX_AI_LOCATION: \"us-central1\"\n        VERTEX_AI_MODEL: \"gemini-2.5-flash\"\n        RAG_PROJECT_ID: \"kd-lab-464110\"\n        RAG_LOCATION: \"europe-west4\"\n        RAG_CORPUS_ID: \"4532873024948404224\"\n        # MCP Gateway via Kong\n        MCP_GATEWAY_URL: \"http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway\"\n        # Cloud Tasks\n        CLOUD_TASKS_PROJECT_ID: \"kd-lab-464110\"\n        CLOUD_TASKS_QUEUE: \"rag-ingestion-poc\"\n        CLOUD_TASKS_LOCATION: \"europe-west1\"\n        # ChannelHub (non-secret)\n        CHANNELHUB_URL: \"https://api.konecta.cloud/one-channel-hub/prd\"\n        CHANNELHUB_LOGIN_URL: \"https://ch-prd.auth.eu-west-1.amazoncognito.com\"\n        # Logging\n        LOG_LEVEL: \"info\"\n      # Secrets injected from External Secrets Operator\n      envFrom:\n        - secretRef:\n            name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets\n      resources:\n        requests:\n          cpu: 250m\n          memory: 256Mi\n        limits:\n          cpu: \"1\"\n          memory: 512Mi\n\n  # Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar\n  cloudSqlProxy:\n    enabled: true\n    instanceConnectionName: \"kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform\"\n    port: 5432\n\n  # Service account with Workload Identity\n  serviceAccount:\n    create: true\n    annotations:\n      iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: kix-iq-portal@kd-ix-platform.iam.gserviceaccount.com\n\n  # K8s probes\n  probes:\n    liveness:\n      httpGet:\n        path: /health\n        port: 8080\n      initialDelaySeconds: 30\n      periodSeconds: 15\n      failureThreshold: 3\n    readiness:\n      httpGet:\n        path: /api/health\n        port: 8080\n      initialDelaySeconds: 40\n      periodSeconds: 10\n      failureThreshold: 3\n    startup:\n      httpGet:\n        path: /health\n        port: 8080\n      initialDelaySeconds: 10\n      periodSeconds: 5\n      failureThreshold: 12  # 60s max startup (migrations may run)\n\n  # HPA\n  autoscaling:\n    enabled: true\n    minReplicas: 1\n    maxReplicas: 5\n    targetCPUUtilization: 70\n    targetMemoryUtilization: 80\n\n  # Dual ingress: Kong for API consumers, Gateway API for browser users\n  services:\n    api:\n      port: 8080\n      ingress:\n        class: kong\n        path: /api/iq-portal\n        stripPath: true\n        annotations:\n          konghq.com/strip-path: \"true\"\n          konghq.com/protocols: \"https\"\n    frontend:\n      port: 8080\n      ingress:\n        class: gateway-api\n        host: iq-portal.app.konecta.io\n        annotations:\n          networking.gke.io/managed-certificates: \"iq-portal-cert\"\n\n  # Pod security\n  podSecurityContext:\n    runAsNonRoot: true\n    runAsUser: 1001\n    fsGroup: 1001\n    seccompProfile:\n      type: RuntimeDefault\n\n  containerSecurityContext:\n    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false\n    readOnlyRootFilesystem: true\n    capabilities:\n      drop: [ALL]\n\n  # Writable tmp for Node.js\n  volumeMounts:\n    - name: tmp\n      mountPath: /tmp\n  volumes:\n    - name: tmp\n      emptyDir:\n        sizeLimit: 100Mi\n\n  # Network policy\n  networkPolicy:\n    enabled: true\n    ingress:\n      - from:\n          - namespaceSelector:\n              matchLabels:\n                name: shared-kong\n          - namespaceSelector:\n              matchLabels:\n                name: shared-gateway\n        ports:\n          - port: 8080\n            protocol: TCP\n```\n\n---\n\n## Step 5 — GitHub Actions CI Workflow\n\n### 5.1 Standard pipeline call\n\nCreate `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`:\n\n```yaml\n# .github/workflows/ci.yaml\nname: CI Pipeline\n\non:\n  push:\n    branches: [main, develop, staging]\n  pull_request:\n    branches: [main]\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n  id-token: write    # OIDC for GCP Workload Identity\n  security-events: write  # Upload SARIF\n\njobs:\n  pipeline:\n    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1\n    with:\n      service-name: kix-usecase-iq-portal\n      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile\n      chart-path: ./chart\n      node-version: \"22\"\n      build-args: |\n        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}\n        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}\n        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}\n      # IQ Portal has no test suite yet — skip test stage\n      skip-tests: true\n    secrets: inherit\n```\n\n### 5.2 Required GitHub repo settings\n\nConfigure in the repo settings:\n\n**Variables** (Settings → Secrets and Variables → Actions → Variables):\n| Variable | Dev | QA | Prod |\n|----------|-----|-----|------|\n| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | `805102662749-...` | `805102662749-...` | `805102662749-...` |\n| `ENVIRONMENT` | `development` | `staging` | `production` |\n\n**Secrets** (inherited from org — no per-repo secrets needed if using shared OIDC):\n- `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` — org-level\n- `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` — org-level\n\n### 5.3 Branch → environment mapping\n\n| Branch | Environment | Namespace |\n|--------|-------------|-----------|\n| `develop` | dev | `dev-api-services` |\n| `staging` | qa | `qa-api-services` |\n| `main` | prod | `prod-api-services` |\n\n---\n\n## Step 6 — Secrets Migration\n\n### 6.1 Identify secrets\n\nCurrent secrets sourced from Secret Manager on Cloud Run:\n\n| Secret | GCP Secret Name | K8s Secret Key |\n|--------|----------------|----------------|\n| Database password | `db-password` | `DB_PASSWORD` |\n| JWT signing key | `koniq-jwt-secret` | `JWT_SECRET` |\n| Google OAuth client ID | `koniq-google-client-id` | `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` |\n| RAG pipeline token | `kon-iq-rag-client-secret` | `RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` |\n| ChannelHub client ID | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` |\n| ChannelHub client secret | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` |\n| ChannelHub connection ID | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID` |\n\n### 6.2 Create GCP Secret Manager entries\n\nIf secrets don't already exist in the kix project's Secret Manager, create them:\n\n```bash\nPROJECT=kd-ix-platform  # or whichever project ESO reads from\n\n# For each secret:\necho -n \"value\" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-db-password \\\n  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-\n\necho -n \"value\" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret \\\n  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-\n\n# ... repeat for each secret\n```\n\n### 6.3 ExternalSecret CRD\n\nCreate `chart/templates/external-secret.yaml` (or include in base chart if templated):\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1\nkind: ExternalSecret\nmetadata:\n  name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets\nspec:\n  refreshInterval: 1h\n  secretStoreRef:\n    name: gcp-secret-store  # Shared SecretStore in namespace\n    kind: SecretStore\n  target:\n    name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets\n    creationPolicy: Owner\n  data:\n    - secretKey: DB_PASSWORD\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-db-password\n    - secretKey: DB_USER\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-db-user\n    - secretKey: JWT_SECRET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret\n    - secretKey: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-google-client-id\n    - secretKey: RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-rag-pipeline-token\n    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-id\n    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-secret\n    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-connection-id\n    - secretKey: GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-image-bucket\n    - secretKey: GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-video-bucket\n    - secretKey: GCS_RAG_BUCKET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-rag-bucket\n```\n\n---\n\n## Step 7 — GitOps Configuration\n\n### 7.1 Add environment values to kix-platform-infrastructure\n\nIn `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`, create:\n\n```\nenvironments/\n├── dev/\n│   └── api-services/\n│       └── kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml\n├── qa/\n│   └── api-services/\n│       └── kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml\n└── prod/\n    └── api-services/\n        └── kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml\n```\n\n### 7.2 Dev environment values\n\n```yaml\n# environments/dev/api-services/kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml\nbase-service-chart:\n  image:\n    tag: \"sha-<initial-commit>\"  # updated by CI pipeline\n\n  containers:\n    app:\n      env:\n        NODE_ENV: \"development\"\n        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT: \"development\"\n        ALLOWED_ORIGINS: \"https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io\"\n        CLOUD_TASKS_HANDLER_URL: \"https://rag-ingestion.dev.konecta.io/poc\"\n        LOG_LEVEL: \"debug\"\n\n  autoscaling:\n    minReplicas: 1\n    maxReplicas: 2\n\n  services:\n    frontend:\n      ingress:\n        host: iq-portal.dev.konecta.io\n```\n\n### 7.3 Prod environment values\n\n```yaml\n# environments/prod/api-services/kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml\nbase-service-chart:\n  image:\n    tag: \"sha-<promoted-commit>\"  # updated by promotion workflow\n\n  containers:\n    app:\n      env:\n        NODE_ENV: \"production\"\n        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT: \"production\"\n        ALLOWED_ORIGINS: \"https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io\"\n        CLOUD_TASKS_HANDLER_URL: \"https://rag-ingestion.app.konecta.io/poc\"\n        LOG_LEVEL: \"info\"\n      resources:\n        requests:\n          cpu: 500m\n          memory: 512Mi\n        limits:\n          cpu: \"2\"\n          memory: 1Gi\n\n  autoscaling:\n    minReplicas: 2\n    maxReplicas: 5\n\n  services:\n    frontend:\n      ingress:\n        host: iq-portal.app.konecta.io\n```\n\n### 7.4 ArgoCD auto-discovers\n\nNo ArgoCD Application manifest needed — the ApplicationSet with git directory generator will automatically detect the new file and create the ArgoCD Application.\n\n---\n\n## Step 8 — Database Migration\n\n### 8.1 Strategy\n\nThe app runs migrations automatically on startup (`runMigrations()` in `backend/index.ts`). This is safe for the initial deployment because:\n\n- The Cloud SQL instance already exists with the schema from Cloud Run\n- Migrations are idempotent (tracked in `schema_migrations` table)\n- No schema changes are part of this migration — same database, same schema\n\n### 8.2 If using a new database\n\nIf creating a fresh Cloud SQL database for kix:\n\n```bash\n# Connect to Cloud SQL\ngcloud sql connect konecta-iqplatform --project=kd-lab-464110 --user=konecta\n\n# Create database\nCREATE DATABASE iqplatform;\n```\n\nThen deploy the app — the 33 migrations will run automatically on first startup, creating the full schema and seed data.\n\n### 8.3 If sharing the existing database\n\nIf pointing kix at the same Cloud SQL instance used by Cloud Run:\n\n1. Verify the `schema_migrations` table is up to date:\n   ```bash\n   # From a machine with Cloud SQL access\n   psql -h <cloud-sql-ip> -U konecta -d iqplatform \\\n     -c \"SELECT * FROM schema_migrations ORDER BY id\"\n   ```\n2. Deploy to kix — migrations will detect all 33 have already run and skip them\n3. Once verified, decommission the Cloud Run service\n\n### 8.4 Cloud SQL Auth Proxy connectivity test\n\nBefore deploying, verify the sidecar can reach Cloud SQL:\n\n```bash\n# Deploy a test pod with cloud-sql-proxy\nkubectl run sql-test --rm -it \\\n  --image=gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.14.3 \\\n  --namespace=dev-api-services \\\n  --serviceaccount=kix-usecase-iq-portal \\\n  -- --private-ip kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform\n```\n\nIn another terminal:\n```bash\nkubectl exec -it sql-test -n dev-api-services -- \\\n  psql -h localhost -U konecta -d iqplatform -c \"SELECT 1\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Step 9 — Verification & Smoke Tests\n\n### 9.1 Deployment checklist\n\nAfter the first CI pipeline runs and ArgoCD syncs:\n\n```bash\nNAMESPACE=dev-api-services\n\n# 1. Pod is running (2/2 = app + cloud-sql-proxy)\nkubectl get pods -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal\n\n# 2. Both containers healthy\nkubectl describe pod -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal | grep -A5 \"Conditions\"\n\n# 3. Liveness probe passing\nkubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app | grep \"running on\"\n\n# 4. Cloud SQL proxy connected\nkubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c cloud-sql-proxy | grep \"ready\"\n\n# 5. Migrations ran\nkubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app | grep \"migration\"\n\n# 6. Service exists\nkubectl get svc -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal\n\n# 7. Kong route active\nkubectl get ingress -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal-api\n\n# 8. Gateway route active\nkubectl get httproute -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal-frontend\n```\n\n### 9.2 Functional smoke tests\n\n```bash\nBASE_URL=\"https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io\"\n\n# Health check (lightweight)\ncurl -s $BASE_URL/health | jq .\n# Expected: {\"status\":\"ok\"}\n\n# Health check (full — DB + GCP)\ncurl -s $BASE_URL/api/health | jq .\n# Expected: {\"status\":\"ok\",\"checks\":{\"database\":\"ok\",\"gcp\":\"ok\"}}\n\n# Version endpoint\ncurl -s $BASE_URL/api/version | jq .\n# Expected: {\"version\":\"0.9.1\",\"commit\":\"<sha>\"}\n\n# Frontend loads (SPA index.html)\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" $BASE_URL/\n# Expected: 200\n\n# Swagger docs\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" $BASE_URL/docs\n# Expected: 200 (or 301 redirect)\n\n# API auth (should return 401 without token)\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" $BASE_URL/api/agents\n# Expected: 401\n```\n\n### 9.3 Kong API route test\n\n```bash\nKONG_URL=\"https://api.konecta.io\"\n\n# Via Kong (API consumers path)\ncurl -s $KONG_URL/api/iq-portal/health | jq .\n# Expected: {\"status\":\"ok\"}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Step 10 — DNS & Domain Cutover\n\nSince nothing is production and services will migrate to new domains, this is straightforward.\n\n### 10.1 New domains\n\n| Path | Domain | Managed By |\n|------|--------|-----------|\n| Frontend (browser) | `iq-portal.app.konecta.io` | GKE Gateway API + IAP |\n| Frontend (dev) | `iq-portal.dev.konecta.io` | GKE Gateway API |\n| API (machine) | `api.konecta.io/api/iq-portal` | Kong |\n\n### 10.2 DNS records\n\n```\niq-portal.app.konecta.io  → A record → Frontend LB external IP\niq-portal.dev.konecta.io  → A record → Frontend LB external IP (dev)\napi.konecta.io            → A record → Kong LB external IP (shared)\n```\n\n### 10.3 Decommission Cloud Run\n\nOnce kix deployment is verified:\n\n```bash\n# Stop Cloud Run service (no traffic)\ngcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \\\n  --region=europe-west1 \\\n  --max-instances=0\n\n# After confidence period (1-2 weeks), delete\ngcloud run services delete kon-iq-portal \\\n  --region=europe-west1\n```\n\n---\n\n## Appendix A — Full Environment Variable Mapping\n\nComplete mapping of every environment variable from Cloud Run to kix Helm values:\n\n| Variable | Source on Cloud Run | Source on kix | Value / Notes |\n|----------|-------------------|--------------|---------------|\n| `NODE_ENV` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `production` |\n| `PORT` | Default (8080) | Helm `env` | `8080` |\n| `ENVIRONMENT` | Cloud Build substitution | Helm `env` | `production` / `development` |\n| `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` | Terraform | Helm `env` | New domain(s) |\n| `DB_HOST` | Terraform (`/cloudsql/...`) | Helm `env` | `localhost` (sidecar) |\n| `DB_PORT` | Default (5432) | Helm `env` | `5432` |\n| `DB_NAME` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `iqplatform` |\n| `DB_USER` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | `konecta` |\n| `DB_PASSWORD` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |\n| `DB_POOL_MIN` | Default (2) | Helm `env` | `2` |\n| `DB_POOL_MAX` | Default (10) | Helm `env` | `10` |\n| `JWT_SECRET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |\n| `JWT_COOKIE_MAX_AGE_DAYS` | Default (7) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `7` |\n| `JWT_EXPIRY` | Default (7d) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `7d` |\n| `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |\n| `DEFAULT_ALLOWED_DOMAIN` | Default | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `konecta.com` |\n| `GCP_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev` |\n| `GCP_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west4` |\n| `VERTEX_AI_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |\n| `VERTEX_AI_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `us-central1` |\n| `VERTEX_AI_MODEL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `gemini-2.5-flash` |\n| `RAG_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |\n| `RAG_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west4` |\n| `RAG_CORPUS_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `4532873024948404224` |\n| `RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |\n| `RAG_SECRET_NAME` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kon-iq-rag-client-secret` |\n| `GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |\n| `GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |\n| `GCS_RAG_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |\n| `MAX_FILE_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB` | Default (100) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `100` |\n| `CLOUD_TASKS_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |\n| `CLOUD_TASKS_QUEUE` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `rag-ingestion-poc` |\n| `CLOUD_TASKS_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west1` |\n| `CLOUD_TASKS_HANDLER_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` (per-env) | New kix ingestion URL |\n| `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` | Default | Helm `env` | `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway` |\n| `CHANNELHUB_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `https://api.konecta.cloud/one-channel-hub/prd` |\n| `CHANNELHUB_LOGIN_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `https://ch-prd.auth.eu-west-1.amazoncognito.com` |\n| `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |\n| `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |\n| `CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |\n| `LOG_LEVEL` | Default (info) | Helm `env` | `info` (prod) / `debug` (dev) |\n| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | Cloud Build arg | CI build arg | `805102662749-...` |\n| `NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT` | Cloud Build arg | CI build arg | `production` / `development` |\n\n**Build-time only** (not needed at runtime):\n| Variable | Source |\n|----------|--------|\n| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | GitHub Actions variable → `--build-arg` |\n| `NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT` | GitHub Actions variable → `--build-arg` |\n| `COMMIT_SHA` | `${{ github.sha }}` → `--build-arg` |\n\n---\n\n## Appendix B — Rollback Procedure\n\n### Automatic (ArgoCD)\n\nArgoCD self-heals. To roll back:\n\n```bash\n# Revert the image tag in the gitops repo\ncd kix-platform-infrastructure\ngit revert HEAD\ngit push\n# ArgoCD syncs the previous image tag automatically\n```\n\n### Manual (kubectl)\n\n```bash\n# Roll back to previous ReplicaSet\nkubectl rollout undo deployment/kix-usecase-iq-portal -n dev-api-services\n\n# Verify\nkubectl rollout status deployment/kix-usecase-iq-portal -n dev-api-services\n```\n\n### Fall back to Cloud Run\n\nIf the entire kix deployment is problematic:\n\n```bash\n# Re-enable Cloud Run\ngcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \\\n  --region=europe-west1 \\\n  --max-instances=10\n\n# Point DNS back (if changed)\n# Update iq-portal.app.konecta.io → Cloud Run URL\n```\n\nSince nothing is production and Cloud Run will remain available during migration, this is low risk.\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. Created `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-tic/IQ_PORTAL_MIGRATION_GUIDE.md` — a 10-step migration guide covering:

**Key findings that shaped the guide:**
- IQ Portal is a **single-container monolith** (Express + static Nuxt SPA on port 8080), not a two-tier app — so it gets a simpler pod pattern than HR Fraud
- It **already uses Cloud SQL** via Unix socket — the only DB change is switching from Unix socket (`/cloudsql/...`) to TCP `localhost:5432` via the sidecar, which requires zero code changes
- **Only one actual code change needed**: adding a `/health` endpoint for K8s probes (the existing `/api/health` does full DB+GCP checks which is too heavy for liveness probes)
- The Dockerfile is already well-hardened (non-root, dumb-init, multi-stage) — only needs digest pinning and removing the `HEALTHCHECK` instruction

**The 10 steps:**
1. Create repo in `konecta-ix-workloads` from template
2. Application code changes (1 file change + delete cloudbuild.yaml)
3. Dockerfile changes (pin digests, remove HEALTHCHECK)
4. Helm chart with Cloud SQL sidecar and dual ingress
5. GitHub Actions CI calling the shared pipeline
6. Secrets migration via External Secrets Operator
7. GitOps values per environment in kix-platform-infrastructure
8. Database migration strategy (auto-migrations on startup)
9. Verification and smoke tests
10. DNS cutover and Cloud Run decommission

Plus two appendices: full env var mapping (36 variables) and rollback procedure.

     1→# Architecture Blueprint: Migration to KIX-Infrastructure Platform
     2→
     3→## Konecta TIC — Cloud Run to Kubernetes (Helm + ArgoCD + GitHub Actions)
     4→
     5→---
     6→
     7→## Table of Contents
     8→
     9→1. [Executive Summary](#1-executive-summary)

    23→
    24→## 1. Executive Summary
    25→
    26→This blueprint defines the architecture for migrating ~24 microservices from GCP Cloud Run to the **kix-infrastructure** Kubernetes platform (CrewAI workflows, infrastructure repos, and config/proxies are out of scope). The migration adopts:
    27→
    28→- **Helm charts** for declarative Kubernetes packaging
    29→- **Secure multi-stage Docker builds** with vulnerability scanning and image signing
    30→- **GitHub Actions** for CI (build, scan, push)
    31→- **ArgoCD** for CD (GitOps-driven deployment across dev/qa/prod)
    32→
    33→**Key principles**: Simple, secure, auditable, repeatable.
    34→
    35→---
    36→
    37→## 2. Current State
    38→
    39→### Infrastructure
    40→- **Compute**: GCP Cloud Run (most services), GKE Autopilot (Kong, LiteLLM, ArgoCD), Cloud Functions (1 bot)
    41→- **Clusters**: GKE Autopilot in eu-west1/eu-west4, private with Cloud NAT
    42→- **CI/CD**: Cloud Build (8 services), GitHub Actions (Terraform only)
    43→- **Registry**: GCP Artifact Registry
    44→- **IaC**: Terraform with modular structure
    45→
    46→### Services In Scope (24 services)
    47→| Category | Count | Stack |
    48→|----------|-------|-------|
    49→| MCP Servers | 14 | Python (FastMCP) |
    50→| Business Apps | 4 | Python (FastAPI) + Node.js (Nuxt 3) |
    51→| Data Pipelines | 3 | Python |
    52→| Bots | 1 | Python (Cloud Functions) |
    53→| Prototypes | 2 | Python |
    54→
    55→**Out of scope**: CrewAI Flows & Tools (12), Infrastructure Repos (4), Config/Proxies (2) — these remain on their current platform or are handled separately.
    56→
    57→### External Dependencies (remain as managed services)
    58→- **Cloud SQL** (PostgreSQL 16+) — accessed via Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (see section 6.9)
    59→- **Firestore** (rate limiting, conversations)
    60→- **Vertex AI** (Gemini, Claude via Vertex, RAG Engine)
    61→- **Cloud Storage** (GCS)
    62→- **Pub/Sub** (async messaging)
    63→- **Google APIs** (Search, Maps, Drive, Chat)
    64→
    65→---
    66→
    67→## 3. Target Architecture
    68→
    69→### High-Level Component Diagram
    70→
    71→```
    72→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    73→│                           INTERNET                                      │
    74→└──────────┬──────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘
    75→           │ HTTPS (API traffic)              │ HTTPS (Frontend traffic)
    76→           │                                  │
    77→┌──────────▼──────────────────┐  ┌────────────▼────────────────────────┐
    78→│  GCP LB + Cloud Armor (WAF) │  │  GCP LB + Gateway API + IAM +       │
    79→│   (API ingress (KONG) {env} │  │  Cloud Armor(Frontend ingress) {env}│
    80→└──────────┬──────────────────┘  └────────────┬────────────────────────┘
    81→           │                                  │
    82→┌──────────▼──────────────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────┐
    83→│              KIX-INFRASTRUCTURE GKE CLUSTER (eu-west1)                  │
    84→│                        kd-ix-eur-{env}-gke                              │
    85→│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
    86→│  │  shared-kong (Kong Gateway)                                      │   │
    87→│  │    ├── routes to {env}-api-services, {env}-mcp-servers           │   │
    88→│  │    └── routes to shared-litellm (LLM Proxy) ← internal only      │   │
    89→│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
    90→│                                                                         │
    91→│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │
    92→│  │  Application Namespaces (per environment × domain)              │    │
    93→│  │                                                                 │    │
    94→│  │  {env}-api-services     → FastAPI backends, business apps,      │    │
    95→│  │                           bots, data pipelines                  │    │
    96→│  │  {env}-mcp-servers      → MCP protocol servers (SSE)            │    │
    97→│  │                                                                 │    │
    98→│  │  {env}-web-apps         → Nuxt 3 frontends  ← Frontend LB       │    │
    99→│  │                           (GKE Gateway API + IAM auth)          │    │
   100→│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
   101→│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
   102→│  │  shared-monitoring: Prometheus / Grafana / Jaeger / OTel         │   │
   103→│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
   104→│                                                                         │
   105→│  ┌───────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐                 │
   106→│  │ shared-litellm│  │ shared-argocd│  │ shared-secrets│                 │
   107→│  │ (behind Kong) │  │ (GitOps CD)  │  │ (ESO + CSI)   │                 │
   108→│  └───────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └───────────────┘                 │
   109→└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   110→         │                    │                    │                    │
   111→         ▼                    ▼                    ▼                    ▼
   112→┌─────────────┐  ┌───────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐
   113→│  Cloud SQL  │  │  Vertex AI / GCS  │  │  GCP Secret Manager │ │  Cloud Storage  │
   114→│ (PostgreSQL)│  │  Pub/Sub / APIs   │  │                     │ │    (Buckets)    │
   115→└─────────────┘  └───────────────────┘  └─────────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘
   116→```
   117→
   118→**Two separate ingress paths**:
   119→- **API path**: Internet → GCP LB + Cloud Armor → Kong Ingress → `{env}-api-services` / `{env}-mcp-servers`
   120→- **Frontend path**: Internet → GCP LB + Cloud Armor + GKE Gateway API + IAM → `{env}-web-apps` (Nuxt frontends)

    32→### Architecture
    33→
    34→IQ Portal is a **single-container monolith**: an Express.js (TypeScript) backend serves both the API (`/api/*`) and a pre-generated Nuxt 4 SPA (static files from `/dist`). Everything runs on port **8080** in production.

    41→│  ├── /api/*  → API routes           │
    42→│  ├── /docs   → Swagger UI           │
    43→│  └── /*      → Static Nuxt SPA      │
    44→│                                     │
    45→│  Cloud SQL (Unix socket)            │
    46→│  /cloudsql/project:region:instance  │
    47→└─────────────────────────────────────┘
    48→```

   122→### Repository Model: Repo-Per-Service Across GitHub Enterprise Orgs
   123→
   124→Services are organized across multiple GitHub Enterprise organizations under **Konecta Digital**. A central **gitops repo** in the platform org holds all Helm value overrides and ArgoCD configuration.
   125→
   126→```
   127→GitHub Enterprise: Konecta Digital
   128→│
   129→├── Org: konecta-ix-platform (Platform Engineering)
   130→│   ├── kix-platform-infrastructure     # GCP infra + ArgoCD apps + GitOps values
   131→│   │   ├── argocd/                     # ApplicationSets
   132→│   │   └── environments/               # Per-env Helm value overrides for ALL services
   133→│   │       ├── dev/{service}.yaml
   134→│   │       ├── qa/{service}.yaml
   135→│   │       └── prod/{service}.yaml
   136→│   ├── kix-platform-modules            # Shared Terraform modules + Helm base chart
   137→│   │   ├── terraform/                  # Existing Terraform modules
   138→│   │   └── helm/base-service-chart/    # Shared Helm library chart (published to OCI)
   139→│   ├── kix-platform-ci-workflows       # Shared reusable GitHub Actions workflows (NEW)
   140→│   │   └── .github/workflows/
   141→│   │       ├── standard-pipeline.yaml
   142→│   │       ├── _build-image.yaml
   143→│   │       ├── _scan-and-sign.yaml
   144→│   │       ├── _push-registry.yaml
   145→│   │       ├── _update-gitops.yaml
   146→│   │       └── _promote.yaml
   147→│   ├── kix-platform-service-template   # Template repo for bootstrapping (NEW)
   148→│   └── kix-platform-docs              # Documentation
   149→│
   150→├── Org: konecta-ix-mcp-servers (MCP Integration Team)
   151→│   ├── kix-mcp-gateway                # Each MCP server has its own repo
   152→│   ├── kix-mcp-ragengine
   153→│   ├── kix-mcp-channelhub
   154→│   ├── kix-mcp-google-search
   155→│   └── kix-mcp-*                      # One per server (~14 repos)
   156→│
   157→├── Org: konecta-ix-apps (Apps Team)
   158→│   ├── kix-app-hrfraud
   159→│   ├── kix-app-hrportal

   177→
   178→**Key design**: All CI workflows are shared from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows`. All GitOps state (what's deployed where) lives in `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`. Individual service repos across all orgs call the shared pipeline and dispatch deployment updates to the central gitops repo.
   179→
   180→### CI/CD Flow
   181→
   182→```
   183→Developer Push → Service Repo (any org) → Standard Pipeline
   184→  (e.g. konecta-ix-mcp-servers/kix-mcp-gateway)
   185→       │
   186→       ▼  Calls reusable workflows from:
   187→       konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows@v1
   188→       ├── Lint + Unit Tests
   189→       ├── Docker Build (BuildKit, multi-stage)
   190→       ├── Trivy Scan + SBOM (Syft)
   191→       ├── Push to Artifact Registry
   192→       └── Cosign Sign (OIDC)
   193→                │
   194→                ▼  Dispatch to:
   195→       konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure
   196→       Update environments/dev/{service}.yaml with new image tag
   197→                │
   198→                ▼
   199→       ArgoCD watches kix-platform-infrastructure
   200→         ├── Dev: Auto-sync
   201→         ├── QA: Manual sync (08:00-18:00 UTC)
   202→         └── Prod: Manual sync (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
   203→```
   204→
   205→---
   206→
   207→## 4. Service Inventory & Tiering
   208→
   209→**No services are currently in production.** Services will migrate directly to new domains on kix-infrastructure — no dual-stack, no DNS cutover, no downtime concerns.
   210→
   211→### Migration Priority
   212→| Priority | Services | Rationale |
   213→|----------|----------|-----------|
   214→| **P0 — Pilot** | kix-usecase-iq-portal (backend + frontend) | Validates the entire platform: both ingress paths, Cloud SQL sidecar, standard pipeline, cross-org dispatch |
   215→| **P1 — Platform core** | Kong, LiteLLM (behind Kong), MCP Gateway | Must be running before other services can function |
   216→| **P2 — MCP Servers** | Remaining 13 MCP servers | Foundation for AI/business workflows |
   217→| **P3 — Business Apps** | kix-app-hrfraud, kix-app-hrportal, kix-usecase-testiq | Remaining user-facing applications |
   218→| **P4 — Data & Demos** | RAG pipelines, bot, prototypes | Lower priority, migrate last |
   219→
   220→---
   221→
   222→## 5. Migration Strategy
   223→
   224→### Phased Approach
   225→
   226→Since nothing is currently in production, services migrate directly to new domains on kix-infrastructure. No dual-stack or traffic cutover needed.
   227→
   228→**Phase 1 — Platform Foundation + Pilot (kix-usecase-iq-portal)**
   229→- Create `kix-platform-ci-workflows` repo in `konecta-ix-platform` with standard pipeline
   230→- Add base Helm chart to `kix-platform-modules/helm/base-service-chart/`, publish to Artifact Registry
   231→- Configure ArgoCD ApplicationSets in `kix-platform-infrastructure`
   232→- Create `kix-platform-service-template` template repo
   233→- Install GitHub App across all orgs for cross-org dispatch
   234→- Configure GitHub OIDC → GCP Workload Identity Pool (per org)
   235→- Deploy Kong + LiteLLM (behind Kong) on kix-infrastructure
   236→- **Pilot: Deploy kix-usecase-iq-portal (backend + frontend)** as the first service through the full pipeline
   237→  - Backend: validates Kong API ingress, Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar, External Secrets, standard pipeline
   238→  - Frontend: validates GKE Gateway API + IAP ingress path, separate LB
   239→  - Exercises both Helm chart profiles (API Service + Frontend)
   240→  - Confirms cross-org workflow dispatch (konecta-ix-workloads → konecta-ix-platform gitops)
   241→- Fix any issues found in base chart, CI workflows, or ArgoCD config before scaling out
   242→
   243→**Phase 2 — MCP Servers (14 services)**
   244→- Deploy kix-mcp-gateway first (dependency for other MCP servers)
   245→- Migrate remaining 13 MCP servers using the validated standard pipeline
   246→- Each service gets a new domain (e.g., `mcp-gateway.dev.konecta.io`)
   247→- Decommission corresponding Cloud Run services as each migrates
   248→
   249→**Phase 3 — Remaining Business Apps (5 services)**
   250→- Migrate kix-app-hrfraud (backend + frontend, Cloud SQL sidecar)
   251→- Migrate kix-app-hrportal, kix-usecase-testiq, kix-demo-prototypes
   252→- Frontend apps deploy behind GKE Gateway API + IAP on new domains
   253→
   254→**Phase 4 — Data Pipelines & Cleanup (4 services)**
   255→- Migrate RAG pipelines (kix-usecase-rag-ingestion, kix-usecase-rag-googledrive)
   256→- Migrate bot (kix-demo-porfolioguru) to K8s Deployment
   257→- Tear down all Cloud Run services
   258→- Remove Cloud Build configurations
   259→
   260→### Stateless Services
   261→- **All application services are stateless** — state lives in Cloud SQL, Firestore, GCS
   262→- Container images contain no data; configuration via ConfigMaps/Secrets
   263→- No persistent volumes needed for application pods
   264→- Temporary storage (`emptyDir`) for: `/tmp`, ML model caches, video processing scratch space
   265→
   266→---
   267→
   268→## 6. Kubernetes & Helm Chart Design
   269→
   270→### 6.1 Helm Chart Strategy
   271→
   272→**Base Chart Library (platform org) + Per-Service Chart (in each service repo) + GitOps Repo (value overrides)**
   273→
   274→Three-layer separation matching the multi-org, repo-per-service model:
   275→
   276→```
   277→Org: konecta-ix-platform
   278→Repo: kix-platform-modules/helm/base-service-chart/
   279→├── Chart.yaml                       # type: library, version: semver
   280→└── templates/                       # Published to Artifact Registry as OCI chart
   281→    ├── _deployment.tpl
   282→    ├── _service.tpl
   283→    ├── _ingress.tpl
   284→    ├── _hpa.tpl
   285→    ├── _pdb.tpl
   286→    ├── _networkpolicy.tpl
   287→    ├── _serviceaccount.tpl
   288→    └── _external-secret.tpl
   289→
   290→Org: konecta-ix-mcp-servers (or any service org)
   291→Repo: kix-mcp-gateway/              # Each service repo
   292→├── src/                             # Application source code
   293→├── Dockerfile
   294→├── chart/                           # Service-specific Helm chart
   295→│   ├── Chart.yaml                   # depends on base-service-chart (OCI reference)
   296→│   ├── values.yaml                  # Service defaults (port, probes, resources)
   297→│   └── templates/                   # Only if service has custom K8s resources
   298→└── .github/workflows/
   299→    └── ci.yaml                      # Calls reusable workflows from kix-platform-ci-workflows
   300→
   301→Org: konecta-ix-platform
   302→Repo: kix-platform-infrastructure/   # ArgoCD watches this repo only
   303→├── argocd/
   304→│   └── applicationset.yaml          # Matrix generator: env × service
   305→└── environments/
   306→    ├── dev/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml     # Overrides: image.tag, replicas, env-specific config
   307→    ├── qa/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
   308→    └── prod/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
   309→```
   310→
   311→**Flow**: Service repo CI builds image → dispatches to `kix-platform-infrastructure` → updates `environments/dev/{service}.yaml` with new tag → ArgoCD syncs.
   312→
   313→**Base chart versioning**: Published to Artifact Registry as OCI Helm chart. Service charts pin to a specific version (`~0.5.0`). Base chart updates are rolled out by bumping the version in each service's `Chart.yaml` (Renovate automates this across all orgs).
   314→
   315→**Cross-org access**: The shared CI workflows in `konecta-ix-platform` are accessible to all other orgs via GitHub Enterprise's internal visibility setting. A GitHub App installed across all orgs handles cross-repo dispatch to the gitops repo.
   316→
   317→**Rationale**: Repo-per-service with org-level boundaries gives teams autonomy and clear ownership. The platform org owns the standards (base chart, CI workflows, gitops state). Service teams own their code and Helm values.
   318→
   319→### 6.2 Namespace Strategy

   320→
   321→**Environment × Domain Boundaries (15 namespaces total)**
   322→
   323→| Namespace Pattern | Contents |
   324→|-------------------|----------|
   325→| `{env}-api-services` | FastAPI backends, business apps, bots, data pipelines |
   326→| `{env}-mcp-servers` | MCP protocol servers |
   327→| `{env}-web-apps` | Nuxt 3 frontends |
   328→| `shared-kong` | Kong API Gateway (routes to APIs, MCP servers, and LiteLLM) |
   329→| `shared-litellm` | LiteLLM proxy (accessible only via Kong, not directly) |
   330→| `shared-argocd` | ArgoCD (existing) |
   331→| `shared-monitoring` | Prometheus, Grafana, Jaeger |
   332→| `shared-devsecops` | SonarQube, Dependency-Track |
   333→| `shared-cert-manager` | TLS certificate management |
   334→
   335→Each namespace gets: ResourceQuotas, LimitRanges, default NetworkPolicies (deny-all ingress), RBAC bindings.
   336→
   337→### 6.3 Resource Profiles
   338→
   339→| Profile | CPU Request | CPU Limit | Mem Request | Mem Limit | Use For |
   340→|---------|------------|-----------|-------------|-----------|---------|
   341→| **API Service** | 100m | 500m | 256Mi | 512Mi | FastAPI APIs, MCP servers, bots, data pipelines |
   342→| **API Heavy** | 250m | 1000m | 512Mi | 1Gi | RAG ingestion, document processing, services with ML deps |
   343→| **Frontend** | 50m | 200m | 128Mi | 256Mi | Nuxt 3 SSR |
   344→
   345→### 6.4 Health Probes
   346→
   347→| Service Type | Liveness | Readiness | Startup |
   348→|-------------|----------|-----------|---------|
   349→| FastAPI | `GET /healthz` 8000 | `GET /readyz` 8000 (checks DB) | N/A |
   350→| MCP Server (SSE) | `GET /healthz` 8000, period=30s | `GET /readyz` 8000, initial=30s | N/A |
   351→| Nuxt 3 | `GET /` 3000 | `GET /_health` 3000 | N/A |
   352→| Heavy services | `GET /healthz` 8000 | `GET /readyz` 8000 | failureThreshold=18 (3min) |
   353→
   354→### 6.5 Autoscaling (HPA)
   355→
   356→| Tier | Min | Max | Target CPU | Scale-Down Window |
   357→|------|-----|-----|------------|-------------------|
   358→| User-facing (frontends, public APIs) | 2 | 20 | 60% | 5 min |
   359→| Internal APIs, MCP servers | 2 | 10 | 75% | 10 min |
   360→| Data pipelines, bots | 1 | 5 | 80% | 15 min |
   361→
   362→Custom metrics via Prometheus Adapter: request rate, Pub/Sub queue depth, active SSE connections.
   363→
   364→### 6.6 Service Mesh
   365→
   366→**Decision: No (initially).** Kong handles ingress traffic management, auth, and rate limiting. Introducing Istio/Linkerd during migration adds unnecessary risk. Revisit post-migration if mTLS between services or advanced traffic management is required.
   367→
   368→### 6.7 Ingress Architecture (Dual Load Balancer)
   369→
   370→Two separate ingress paths with independent load balancers:
   371→
   372→**Path 1 — API / Backend Ingress (Kong)**
   373→```
   374→Internet → GCP External LB + Cloud Armor (WAF)
   375→         → Kong Ingress Controller (shared-kong)
   376→         → {env}-api-services, {env}-mcp-servers
   377→```
   378→- Kong handles API routing, rate limiting, authentication plugins, CORS
   379→- `konghq.com/plugins` annotations per Ingress resource
   380→- Cloud Armor OWASP top-10 WAF rules on the LB
   381→
   382→**Internal — LiteLLM via Kong (all LLM traffic)**
   383→```
   384→Any service pod → Kong (internal route) → shared-litellm (LiteLLM proxy) → Vertex AI / LLM providers
   385→```
   386→- LiteLLM sits behind Kong as an internal-only Kong route (no external exposure)
   387→- **All services** that need LLM access call Kong's internal endpoint (e.g. `kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/llm/...`), which routes to LiteLLM
   388→- Kong applies rate limiting, auth, and observability plugins on LLM traffic
   389→- Benefits: centralized LLM access control, usage metering, consistent auth, audit trail
   390→- NetworkPolicy on `shared-litellm` namespace: allow ingress **only from Kong**, deny all direct access
   391→
   392→**Path 2 — Frontend Ingress (GKE Gateway API + IAP)**
   393→```
   394→Internet → GCP External LB + Cloud Armor (WAF) + GKE Gateway API
   395→         → IAP (Identity-Aware Proxy) / IAM authentication
   396→         → {env}-web-apps (Nuxt 3 frontends)
   397→```
   398→- **GKE Gateway API** (`gateway.networking.k8s.io`): Native GKE ingress using `Gateway` and `HTTPRoute` resources
   399→- **IAP (Identity-Aware Proxy)**: Google-managed identity layer — authenticates users via Google/OIDC before traffic reaches the pod. Configured via `BackendConfig` with IAP enabled.
   400→- **Cloud Armor**: WAF policies applied at the LB level (same OWASP rules as API path)
   401→- **IAM**: Access controlled by IAM policies on the IAP resource — only authorized users/groups can reach the frontend
   402→- No Kong involvement for frontend traffic
   403→
   404→**Shared Configuration**
   405→- **Internal services**: ClusterIP only, accessed via `service.namespace.svc.cluster.local`
   406→- **TLS**: cert-manager + Let's Encrypt for both paths (separate certificates per domain)
   407→- **DNS per environment**:
   408→  - API: `dev.api.konecta.io`, `qa.api.konecta.io`, `api.konecta.io`
   409→  - Frontend: `dev.app.konecta.io`, `qa.app.konecta.io`, `app.konecta.io`
   410→
   411→### 6.8 ArgoCD ApplicationSet Strategy
   412→
   413→**Git Directory Generator on kix-platform-infrastructure**
   414→
   415→ArgoCD watches `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`. Each file in `environments/{env}/` defines an application.
   416→
   417→- ApplicationSet scans `environments/dev/`, `environments/qa/`, `environments/prod/` directories
   418→- Each `{service}.yaml` file contains the Helm source repo URL (across any org), chart path, and environment values
   419→- ArgoCD renders the Helm chart from the **service repo** using values from **kix-platform-infrastructure**
   420→- **Dev**: `syncPolicy.automated` (auto-sync on commit to kix-platform-infrastructure)
   421→- **QA**: `syncPolicy.automated: false` (manual sync, business-hours window)
   422→- **Prod**: `syncPolicy.automated: false` (manual sync, Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
   423→- ArgoCD Projects for RBAC: `dev-project`, `qa-project`, `prod-project`
   424→- Sync waves: infrastructure resources (wave 0) → application deployments (wave 10)
   425→- New services are auto-discovered when their files are added to `environments/` directories
   426→
   427→### 6.9 Cloud SQL Auth Proxy Sidecar
   428→
   429→Services that connect to Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL) use the **Cloud SQL Auth Proxy** as a sidecar container. This is baked into the base Helm chart as an opt-in pattern.
   430→
   431→**Why sidecar (not shared proxy)**:
   432→- Each pod gets its own authenticated connection to Cloud SQL
   433→- Workload Identity per service account — least-privilege IAM per service
   434→- No shared proxy bottleneck; scales with the application pods
   435→- Google-recommended pattern for GKE
   436→
   437→**How it works**:
   438→```
   439→┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
   440→│  Pod                                     │
   441→│  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐  │
   442→│  │  App          │  │  cloud-sql-proxy │  │
   443→│  │  Container    │──│  sidecar         │──│──▶ Cloud SQL (private IP)
   444→│  │  (FastAPI)    │  │  (port 5432)     │  │
   445→│  └──────────────┘  └──────────────────┘  │
   446→│  localhost:5432                           │
   447→└──────────────────────────────────────────┘
   448→```
   449→
   450→- App connects to `localhost:5432` — no connection string changes needed
   451→- Sidecar handles IAM authentication via Workload Identity (no passwords in env vars)
   452→- Private IP connectivity to Cloud SQL (no public IP exposure)
   453→
   454→**Base chart integration** (opt-in via Helm values):
   455→```yaml
   456→# In service values.yaml — only services needing Cloud SQL enable this
   457→cloudSqlProxy:
   458→  enabled: true
   459→  instanceConnectionName: "project:region:instance"
   460→  port: 5432
   461→  resources:
   462→    requests:
   463→      cpu: 50m
   464→      memory: 64Mi
   465→    limits:
   466→      cpu: 200m
   467→      memory: 128Mi
   468→```
   469→
   470→When `cloudSqlProxy.enabled: true`, the base chart template injects:
   471→- A `cloud-sql-proxy` sidecar container (`gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2`)
   472→- `--private-ip` flag (uses VPC-native private IP, not public)
   473→- `--auto-iam-authn` flag (Workload Identity, no password needed)
   474→- The service account annotation for Workload Identity must have `roles/cloudsql.client` IAM role
   475→
   476→**Services requiring Cloud SQL sidecar**:
   477→| Service | Database | Current State |
   478→|---------|----------|---------------|
   479→| kix-usecase-iq-portal | PostgreSQL (IQ portal data) | Already on Cloud SQL |
   480→| kix-app-hrfraud | PostgreSQL (HR fraud data) | Currently containerized PostgreSQL — migrate to Cloud SQL as part of kix move |
   481→
   482→**Connection string pattern** (app-side):
   483→```
   484→DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://user@localhost:5432/dbname
   485→```
   486→No password in the connection string when using `--auto-iam-authn`. The proxy handles authentication via the pod's Workload Identity.
   487→
   488→### 6.10 Multi-Tier App Pattern (Frontend + Backend in Single Pod)
   489→
   490→Two-tiered apps like kix-app-hrfraud and kix-usecase-iq-portal (Nuxt frontend + FastAPI backend) deploy as a **multi-container pod** rather than separate Deployments. This eliminates the inter-service network hops that made Cloud Run deployments slow and complex.
   491→
   492→**Pod layout**:
   493→```
   494→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   495→│  Pod: kix-app-hrfraud                                            │
   496→│                                                                  │
   497→│  ┌──────────────┐   localhost:8000   ┌──────────────────────┐    │
   498→│  │  frontend     │ ─────────────────▶│  backend              │    │
   499→│  │  (Nuxt SSR)   │                   │  (FastAPI)            │    │
   500→│  │  :3000        │                   │  :8000                │    │
   501→│  └──────────────┘                   └──────────┬───────────┘    │
   502→│                                                 │ localhost:5432 │
   503→│                                      ┌──────────▼───────────┐    │
   504→│                                      │  cloud-sql-proxy     │    │
   505→│                                      │  (sidecar)           │────│──▶ Cloud SQL
   506→│                                      │  :5432               │    │
   507→│                                      └──────────────────────┘    │
   508→└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   509→         ▲                    ▲
   510→         │                    │
   511→    Frontend LB          API LB (Kong)
   512→  (Gateway API + IAP)   (internal routes)
   513→```
   514→
   515→**Why single pod (not separate Deployments)**:
   516→- **`localhost` communication**: Frontend calls backend on `localhost:8000` — zero network hop, no DNS resolution, no Service objects needed for the backend
   517→- **Atomic deploys**: Frontend and backend always deploy together — eliminates API version skew during rolling updates
   518→- **Shared lifecycle**: Same release cycle, same repo, same Helm chart — these are tightly coupled components, not independent microservices
   519→- **Simpler networking**: Only the frontend port (3000) and optionally backend port (8000) need to be exposed via K8s Services; inter-container traffic never touches the cluster network
   520→- **Fewer moving parts**: One Deployment, one HPA, one PodDisruptionBudget, one set of health probes
   521→
   522→**Helm values pattern** (opt-in via base chart):
   523→```yaml
   524→# values.yaml for kix-app-hrfraud
   525→containers:
   526→  backend:
   527→    image:
   528→      repository: eu-docker.pkg.dev/project/kix-services/kix-app-hrfraud-backend
   529→      tag: sha-abc1234
   530→    port: 8000
   531→    resources:
   532→      requests: { cpu: 100m, memory: 256Mi }
   533→      limits: { cpu: 500m, memory: 512Mi }
   534→    probes:
   535→      liveness: { path: /healthz, port: 8000 }
   536→      readiness: { path: /readyz, port: 8000 }
   537→    env:
   538→      DATABASE_URL: "postgresql+asyncpg://user@localhost:5432/dbname"
   539→      LITELLM_ENDPOINT: "http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/llm"
   540→
   541→  frontend:
   542→    image:
   543→      repository: eu-docker.pkg.dev/project/kix-services/kix-app-hrfraud-frontend
   544→      tag: sha-def5678
   545→    port: 3000
   546→    resources:
   547→      requests: { cpu: 50m, memory: 128Mi }
   548→      limits: { cpu: 200m, memory: 256Mi }
   549→    probes:
   550→      liveness: { path: /, port: 3000 }
   551→      readiness: { path: /_health, port: 3000 }
   552→    env:
   553→      API_BASE: "http://localhost:8000"    # ← backend via localhost
   554→
   555→cloudSqlProxy:
   556→  enabled: true
   557→  instanceConnectionName: "project:eu-west1:hrfraud-db"
   558→  port: 5432
   559→
   560→# Two K8s Services expose the pod on different ingress paths
   561→services:
   562→  api:
   563→    port: 8000
   564→    targetPort: 8000
   565→    ingress:
   566→      class: kong                           # ← API LB
   567→      path: /api/hrfraud
   568→  frontend:
   569→    port: 3000

   570→    targetPort: 3000

   572→      class: gateway-api                    # ← Frontend LB (Gateway API + IAP)

   575→
   576→**Dual ingress on a single pod**: Two K8s Service objects point at the same pod on different ports. The API Service routes through Kong; the frontend Service routes through the GKE Gateway API + IAP. Both LBs reach the same pod, but through different ports and auth models.
   577→
   578→**CI pipeline for multi-tier apps**: The standard pipeline builds **two images** (backend + frontend) from the same repo. Both image tags are updated atomically in the gitops values file:
   579→```
   580→ci.yaml triggers:
   581→  1. Build backend image → sha-abc1234
   582→  2. Build frontend image → sha-def5678
   583→  3. Dispatch to gitops: update both tags in one commit
   584→  4. ArgoCD syncs → pod rolls with both new images
   585→```
   586→
   587→**When NOT to use this pattern**:
   588→- Services that need independent scaling (e.g., API serving 10x more traffic than its frontend)
   589→- Services with very different resource profiles where one container would waste resources
   590→- Loosely coupled services that release on different cadences
   591→- Services where the frontend is a static SPA (no SSR) — serve from CDN instead
   592→
   593→**Apps using this pattern**:
   594→| App | Containers | Ports |
   595→|-----|-----------|-------|
   596→| kix-usecase-iq-portal | Nuxt frontend (3000) + FastAPI backend (8000) + cloud-sql-proxy (5432) | 3 containers |
   597→| kix-app-hrfraud | Nuxt frontend (3000) + FastAPI backend (8000) + cloud-sql-proxy (5432) | 3 containers |
