   600→
   601→## 7. Secure Docker Builds
   602→
   603→### 7.1 Base Image Policy
   604→
   605→| Stack | Builder Stage | Runtime Stage |
   606→|-------|--------------|---------------|
   607→| Python (standard) | `python:3.x-slim` | `python:3.x-slim` |
   608→| Python (ML: opencv, whisper, ffmpeg) | `python:3.x-slim` + apt deps | `python:3.x-slim` + runtime-only libs |
   609→| Node.js | `node:20-alpine` | `gcr.io/distroless/nodejs20-debian12` |
   610→
   611→- Pin to **full digest** in CI (updated monthly by Renovate/Dependabot)
   612→- Parameterize Python version via `ARG PYTHON_VERSION=3.11`
   613→- **Never use `:latest`**
   614→
   615→### 7.2 Multi-Stage Build Patterns
   616→
   617→**Python services:**
   618→```
   619→Stage 1 "builder": python:3.x-slim → install build deps → pip install --prefix=/install
   620→Stage 2 "runtime": python:3.x-slim → COPY --from=builder /install → non-root USER → ENTRYPOINT
   621→```
   622→
   623→**Node.js services:**
   624→```
   625→Stage 1 "deps": node:20-alpine → npm ci
   626→Stage 2 "builder": COPY source → npm run build → npm prune --omit=dev
   627→Stage 3 "runtime": distroless/nodejs20 → COPY .output → no shell → ENTRYPOINT
   628→```
   629→
   630→### 7.3 Security Hardening
   631→
   632→- **Non-root user** (UID 65532) in all images, enforced by `runAsNonRoot: true` in pod spec
   633→- **Read-only root filesystem** — writable paths via `emptyDir` mounts (`/tmp`, `/app/.cache`)
   634→- **No shell** in Node.js production images (distroless)
   635→- **Drop ALL capabilities** in pod security context
   636→- **No `.env` files** baked into images; all config via K8s secrets/configmaps
   637→- `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1` for Python images
   638→
   639→### 7.4 Dependency Management
   640→
   641→- Python: `pip-compile` or `uv lock` for pinned requirements with hashes
   642→- Node.js: `npm ci` only (never `npm install` in CI)
   643→- Weekly Renovate/Dependabot PRs for dependency updates
   644→
   645→### 7.5 Build Metadata (OCI Labels)
   646→
   647→All images include: `org.opencontainers.image.created`, `.revision` (git SHA), `.version`, `.source`, `.title`, `.vendor` — final stage only.
   648→
   649→### 7.6 Image Size Targets
   650→
   651→| Category | Target | Optimization |
   652→|----------|--------|-------------|
   653→| Python standard | < 200 MB | Multi-stage, no cache, strip pyc |
   654→| Python ML/heavy | < 500 MB | opencv-headless, models as external volumes |
   655→| Node.js (Nuxt) | < 80 MB | Distroless runtime, npm prune |
   656→
   657→---
   658→
   659→## 8. CI/CD Pipeline (GitHub Actions + ArgoCD)
   660→
   661→### 8.1 Standard Pipeline Architecture (Repo-Per-Service)
   662→
   663→Every service repo follows the **same pipeline** by calling reusable workflows from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows`. A service onboards by adding a single `ci.yaml` file.
   664→
   665→**Shared Workflows Repo (`konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/`)**:
   666→
   667→```
   668→kix-platform-ci-workflows/
   669→└── .github/workflows/
   670→    ├── standard-pipeline.yaml      # Main orchestrator (called by every service)
   671→    ├── _lint-and-test.yaml         # Reusable: Language-aware lint + unit tests
   672→    ├── _secret-scan.yaml           # Reusable: Trivy filesystem secret detection
   673→    ├── _sca.yaml                   # Reusable: Dependency-Track SCA analysis
   674→    ├── _sast.yaml                  # Reusable: SonarQube static analysis
   675→    ├── _build-image.yaml           # Reusable: Docker build + BuildKit cache
   676→    ├── _container-scan-sign.yaml   # Reusable: Trivy image scan + SBOM (Syft) + Cosign sign
   677→    ├── _push-registry.yaml         # Reusable: Push to Artifact Registry
   678→    ├── _update-gitops.yaml         # Reusable: Dispatch to kix-platform-infrastructure
   679→    ├── _promote.yaml               # Reusable: Promote image tag between environments
   680→    └── _dast.yaml                  # Reusable: OWASP ZAP baseline scan (post-deploy, pre-prod)
   681→```
   682→
   683→**Standard Service Repo Pipeline (every service copies this)**:
   684→
   685→```yaml
   686→# .github/workflows/ci.yaml (in each service repo — identical across all repos)
   687→name: CI
   688→on:
   689→  push:
   690→    branches: [main]
   691→  pull_request:
   692→    branches: [main]
   693→  workflow_dispatch:
   694→
   695→jobs:
   696→  pipeline:
   697→    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1
   698→    with:
   699→      service-name: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}
   700→      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile
   701→      chart-path: ./chart
   702→      python-version: "3.11"     # or node-version: "20" for Node.js services
   703→    secrets: inherit
   704→```
   705→
   706→**That's it.** Every service repo has one small workflow file. All logic lives in the shared workflows repo. Updates to the pipeline roll out to all services by bumping the `@v1` tag.
   707→
   708→**Cross-org workflow sharing**: GitHub Enterprise allows sharing workflows across organizations. The `kix-platform-ci-workflows` repo is set to **internal** visibility, making it callable from `konecta-ix-mcp-servers`, `konecta-ix-apps`, `konecta-ix-workloads`, etc.
   709→
   710→### 8.2 Standard Pipeline Stages
   711→
   712→Pipeline stages aligned with the **Konecta DevSecOps Handbook**. Failure at any stage stops the pipeline.
   713→
   714→**Code Pipeline (every PR and merge):**
   715→
   716→```
   717→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
   718→│  1. LINT &   │─▶│  2. SECRET   │─▶│  3. SCA      │─▶│  4. SAST     │─▶│  5. BUILD &  │
   719→│     TEST     │  │     SCAN     │  │              │  │              │  │  UNIT TEST   │
   720→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
   721→  PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        merge only
   722→  ├ ruff/eslint     ├ Trivy fs        ├ Syft SBOM       ├ SonarQube       ├ BuildKit
   723→  ├ type checks     │ --scanners      │ generation       │ analysis        ├ multi-stage
   724→  └ pip audit/      │ secret          ├ Dependency-      │ quality gate:   ├ unit tests
   725→    npm audit       └ stops on          Track upload     │ no critical/    └ OCI labels
   726→                      any finding     └ stops on           high issues
   727→                                        critical/high
   728→
   729→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
   730→│  6. CONTAINER│─▶│  7. PUSH &   │─▶│  8. DEPLOY   │
   731→│  SCAN & SIGN │  │  REGISTRY    │  │  (GitOps)    │
   732→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
   733→  merge only        merge only        merge only
   734→  ├ Trivy image     ├ Push to AR      ├ Dispatch to
   735→  │ scan            ├ Tag: sha-xxx    │ gitops repo
   736→  ├ SBOM attach     └ Tag: v1.2.3    ├ Update dev
   737→  │ (Syft)            (on git tag)    │ values.yaml
   738→  └ Cosign sign                       └ ArgoCD syncs
   739→    (OIDC)
   740→```
   741→
   742→**Promotion Pipeline (dev → QA → prod):**
   743→
   744→```
   745→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
   746→│  9. DAST     │─▶│ 10. PROMOTE  │─▶│ 11. MONITOR  │
   747→│  (pre-prod)  │  │              │  │              │
   748→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
   749→  post-deploy       manual trigger    continuous
   750→  ├ OWASP ZAP       ├ Copy image      ├ Prometheus
   751→  │ baseline scan   │ tag to next     ├ Grafana
   752→  │ against QA      │ env values      └ alerts on
   753→  └ blocks prod     └ PR to gitops      anomalies
   754→    promotion if
   755→    findings
   756→```
   757→
   758→**On PR**: Stages 1–4 run (lint, secret scan, SCA, SAST). Fast feedback, no build.
   759→**On merge to main**: Stages 1–8 run. Full build, scan, sign, push, deploy to dev.
   760→**On promotion**: Stage 9 (DAST) runs against QA before prod promotion is allowed.
   761→
   762→### 8.3 Image Tagging Strategy
   763→
   764→| Tag | When | Purpose |
   765→|-----|------|---------|
   766→| `sha-{7-char}` | Every build | Immutable build identifier |
   767→| `v{major}.{minor}.{patch}` | On git tag | Release version |
   768→| `{env}-latest` | On promotion | Environment pointer (mutable) |
   769→
   770→**Rule**: SHA tags are immutable. Never overwrite. Promotion copies the SHA tag reference, not the image.
   771→
   772→### 8.4 Security Gates
   773→
   774→Per the Konecta DevSecOps Handbook, every gate is fail-stop — pipeline halts immediately on critical/high findings.
   775→
   776→| Gate | Tool | Stage | Trigger | Action on Failure |
   777→|------|------|-------|---------|-------------------|
   778→| **Secret Detection** | Trivy (`--scanners secret`) | 2 (pre-build) | Every PR + merge | Stop pipeline, no exceptions |
   779→| **Dependency Vulnerabilities** | Dependency-Track (via Syft SBOM) | 3 (pre-build) | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   780→| **SAST Quality Gate** | SonarQube | 4 (pre-build) | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   781→| **Container Vulnerabilities** | Trivy (image scan) | 6 (post-build) | Every merge | Stop on critical/high |
   782→| **Image Signature** | Cosign (Sigstore/OIDC) | 6 (post-build) | Every merge | Unsigned images blocked |
   783→| **DAST Baseline** | OWASP ZAP | 9 (post-deploy QA) | Before prod promotion | Block promotion on findings |
   784→| **Admission Control** | Kyverno | Deploy-time | Every sync | Reject unsigned images, block `:latest` |
   785→
   786→**Tool details:**
   787→
   788→- **Trivy secret scan**: Scans source filesystem for hardcoded secrets, keys, tokens before any build occurs. Zero tolerance — any finding stops the pipeline.
   789→- **SonarQube**: Hosted instance in `shared-devsecops` namespace. Runs static analysis on every PR. Quality gate: no new critical or high severity issues. Results posted as PR comment via GitHub integration.
   790→- **Dependency-Track**: Hosted instance in `shared-devsecops` namespace. Syft generates CycloneDX SBOMs from source dependencies (stage 3) and from built images (stage 6). Both uploaded to Dependency-Track for continuous vulnerability tracking. Pipeline stops on critical/high.
   791→- **OWASP ZAP**: Runs as a containerized baseline scan against the service's QA endpoint after deployment. Blocks promotion to prod if baseline vulnerabilities found. Integrated into the `_promote.yaml` workflow as a pre-condition.
   792→- **Cosign**: Keyless signing via GitHub OIDC → Sigstore. No long-lived keys.
   793→- **Kyverno**: Cluster admission controller enforces signed images, blocks `:latest` tag, requires Konecta Artifact Registry as source.
   794→
   795→**Vulnerability remediation SLAs** (per DevSecOps Handbook):
   796→
   797→| Severity | Detection → Triage | Triage → Fix | Fix → Deploy |
   798→|----------|-------------------|-------------|-------------|
   799→| Critical | 4 hours | 24 hours | 48 hours |
   800→| High | 24 hours | 7 days | 14 days |
   801→| Medium | 7 days | 30 days | 45 days |
   802→| Low | 30 days | 90 days | Next release |
   803→
   804→### 8.5 GitOps Flow (Cross-Repo)
   805→
   806→```
   807→konecta-ix-mcp-servers/                    konecta-ix-platform/
   808→kix-mcp-gateway                            kix-platform-infrastructure
   809→────────────────────                       ───────────────────────────
   810→1. PR merged to main
   811→2. Pipeline builds sha-abc1234
   812→3. Image pushed to Artifact Registry
   813→4. Pipeline triggers repository_dispatch ──▶ 5. Automated PR or direct commit:
   814→                                                environments/dev/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
   815→                                                  image.tag: sha-abc1234
   816→                                             6. ArgoCD detects change → syncs dev
   817→```
   818→
   819→**Cross-org dispatch**: A GitHub App installed across all orgs (`konecta-ix-platform`, `konecta-ix-mcp-servers`, `konecta-ix-apps`, etc.) enables the `_update-gitops.yaml` workflow to commit to `kix-platform-infrastructure`. The App has write access only to the gitops repo, scoped to minimal permissions.
   820→
   821→### 8.6 Environment Promotion
   822→
   823→| Environment | Trigger | Sync Mode | Change Window |
   824→|-------------|---------|-----------|---------------|
   825→| Dev | Every merge to main | Auto-sync | 24/7 |
   826→| QA | Manual workflow dispatch or PR to kix-platform-infrastructure | Manual sync | 08:00-18:00 UTC |
   827→| Prod | Manual workflow dispatch + approval | Manual sync | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |
   828→
   829→**Promotion workflow** (from `kix-platform-ci-workflows/_promote.yaml`):
   830→
   831→**Dev → QA:**
   832→1. Engineer triggers `promote` workflow on the service repo
   833→2. Workflow copies the image tag from `environments/dev/{service}.yaml` to `environments/qa/{service}.yaml`
   834→3. Creates a PR on kix-platform-infrastructure
   835→4. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to QA
   836→
   837→**QA → Prod (includes DAST gate):**
   838→1. Engineer triggers `promote` workflow targeting prod
   839→2. Workflow runs **OWASP ZAP baseline scan** against the service's QA endpoint (`_dast.yaml`)
   840→3. If ZAP finds baseline vulnerabilities → promotion blocked, findings reported
   841→4. If ZAP passes → workflow copies image tag to `environments/prod/{service}.yaml`
   842→5. Creates a PR on kix-platform-infrastructure (requires approval)
   843→6. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to prod
   844→
   845→### 8.7 Rollback Strategy
   846→
   847→- **Automated** (prod): Prometheus alert on error rate >5% for 3 min → webhook reverts commit in kix-platform-infrastructure → ArgoCD syncs previous image
   848→- **Manual**: Engineer reverts the `environments/prod/{service}.yaml` entry to previous SHA tag via PR to kix-platform-infrastructure
   849→- **RTO target**: <5 min (detection + revert + sync)
   850→- **Git as source of truth**: Every rollback is a visible Git commit in kix-platform-infrastructure
   851→
   852→### 8.8 Secrets Management (CI)
   853→
   854→- **GitHub OIDC** → GCP Workload Identity Pool (no JSON keys, configured per org)
   855→- Dynamic secret fetching from GCP Secret Manager at build time
   856→- Build secrets via `docker build --secret` (never in image layers)
   857→- Token lifetime ~1 hour, auto-refreshing, full audit trail
   858→- **GitHub App** for cross-org dispatch (kix-platform-infrastructure commits), installed across all orgs, scoped to minimal permissions
   859→
   860→### 8.9 Onboarding a New Service
   861→
   862→Adding a new service to the platform:
   863→
   864→1. **Create repo** in the appropriate org from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template`:
   865→   - Standard `Dockerfile` (Python or Node.js variant)
   866→   - Standard `chart/` directory with `Chart.yaml` depending on base chart
   867→   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling shared pipeline
   868→   - Standard `.dockerignore`, `CODEOWNERS`
   869→2. **Add to gitops repo**: Create `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/{service-name}.yaml` in `kix-platform-infrastructure`
   870→3. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** via ApplicationSet git directory generator
   871→4. **First push** triggers the standard pipeline — fully operational
   872→
   873→No pipeline code to write. No Helm templates to create. Just fill in `values.yaml` with service-specific config.
   874→
   875→---
   876→
   877→## 9. Security Architecture
   878→
   879→### 9.1 Container Image Security
   880→
   881→- **Multi-stage builds**: Builder dependencies never ship in runtime image
   882→- **Base image policy**: Pinned digests, updated monthly, slim/distroless only
   883→- **Vulnerability scanning**: Trivy pre-push, CRITICAL/HIGH block deployment
   884→- **Image signing**: Cosign keyless (OIDC) — every image cryptographically signed
   885→- **SBOM**: Generated for every build, stored as OCI annotation
   886→- **Registry**: Artifact Registry with vulnerability scanning enabled, IAM-restricted access
   887→
   888→### 9.2 Supply Chain Security
   889→
   890→- **Dependency pinning**: Hash-verified `requirements.txt` (Python), `package-lock.json` (Node.js)
   891→- **Dependency scanning**: `pip audit` / `npm audit` in CI, Renovate for updates
   892→- **GitHub Actions hardening**:
   893→  - Pin action versions by SHA (not tag)
   894→  - Minimal `GITHUB_TOKEN` permissions per workflow
   895→  - No third-party actions without security review
   896→  - OIDC for all GCP interactions (no stored credentials)
   897→
   898→### 9.3 Runtime Security
   899→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.

  "content": "# CLAUDE.md — AI Assistant Guidelines for kix-infrastructure Migration\n\nThis file provides context for AI assistants working on migrating Konecta TIC services from GCP Cloud Run to the kix-infrastructure Kubernetes platform.\n\n## Reference Documents\n\n| Document | Path | Purpose |\n|----------|------|---------|\n| Architecture Blueprint | `KIX_MIGRATION_BLUEPRINT.md` | Target architecture, Helm design, CI/CD pipeline, security, namespaces, service inventory |\n| IQ Portal Migration Guide | `IQ_PORTAL_MIGRATION_GUIDE.md` | Pilot app migration — use as the template for all subsequent migration guides |\n\n**Read both documents before producing any migration guide or making architectural decisions.** They are the source of truth for all conventions described below.\n\n---\n\n## Platform Overview\n\n~24 microservices migrating from GCP Cloud Run to GKE (kix-infrastructure). Repo-per-service model across 6 GitHub Enterprise orgs. Shared CI workflows, a single GitOps repo, and a base Helm library chart.\n\n### GitHub Enterprise Org Structure\n\n| Org | Purpose | Repo Prefix |\n|-----|---------|-------------|\n| `konecta-ix-platform` | Platform engineering: GitOps, CI workflows, Helm base chart, templates, docs | `kix-platform-*` |\n| `konecta-ix-mcp-servers` | MCP protocol servers | `kix-mcp-*` |\n| `konecta-ix-apps` | Business applications | `kix-app-*` |\n| `konecta-ix-workloads` | Use case workloads | `kix-usecase-*` |\n| `konecta-ix-demos` | Demos and prototypes | `kix-demo-*` |\n| `konecta-ix-services` | Shared platform services (Kong, LiteLLM, monitoring) | `kix-services-*` |\n\n### Key Platform Repos\n\n| Repo | Org | Role |\n|------|-----|------|\n| `kix-platform-infrastructure` | `konecta-ix-platform` | GitOps repo — ArgoCD watches this. Helm values per env per service. |\n| `kix-platform-ci-workflows` | `konecta-ix-platform` | 11 reusable GitHub Actions workflows (standard pipeline) |\n| `kix-platform-modules` | `konecta-ix-platform` | Base Helm library chart + Terraform modules |\n| `kix-platform-service-template` | `konecta-ix-platform` | Template repo for bootstrapping new services |\n\n---\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n### Repo Names\n\nMap from current `kon-` prefix to `kix-` prefix:\n\n| Current Pattern | New Pattern | Example |\n|----------------|-------------|---------|\n| `kon-mcp-{name}` | `kix-mcp-{name}` | `kon-mcp-gateway` → `kix-mcp-gateway` |\n| `kon-app-{name}` | `kix-app-{name}` | `kon-app-hrfraud` → `kix-app-hrfraud` |\n| `kon-iq-portal` | `kix-usecase-iq-portal` | Workload, goes to `konecta-ix-workloads` |\n| `kon-testiq` | `kix-usecase-testiq` | Workload |\n| `kon-bot-{name}` | `kix-demo-{name}` | `kon-bot-porfolioguru` → `kix-demo-porfolioguru` |\n| `kon-flow-{name}` | Out of scope | CrewAI flows excluded |\n| `kon-crewai-{name}` | Out of scope | CrewAI tools excluded |\n\n### Domain Names\n\n| Path | Pattern |\n|------|---------|\n| API (via Kong) | `api.konecta.io/api/{service-name}` |\n| Frontend (via Gateway API + IAP) | `{service-name}.app.konecta.io` |\n| Frontend (dev) | `{service-name}.dev.konecta.io` |\n\n### Namespace Mapping\n\nServices go into `{env}-{domain}` namespaces:\n\n| Domain | Namespace | Services |\n|--------|-----------|----------|\n| `api-services` | `{env}-api-services` | FastAPI backends, Express.js backends, bots, data pipelines |\n| `mcp-servers` | `{env}-mcp-servers` | MCP protocol servers |\n| `web-apps` | `{env}-web-apps` | Standalone Nuxt frontends (no backend) |\n\nEnvironments: `dev`, `qa`, `prod`.\n\n---\n\n## Service Architecture Patterns\n\nWhen creating a migration guide for a service, first determine which pattern it follows:\n\n### Pattern 1 — Single Container (API or monolith)\n\n**Use when**: Service is a single process (Python FastAPI, Express.js monolith, etc.)\n**Example**: Most MCP servers, IQ Portal (Express serves both API + static SPA)\n\n```\nPod:\n  ┌─────────────┐   ┌──────────────────┐\n  │  app         │   │  cloud-sql-proxy │  (optional)\n  │  :8080/:8000 │   │  :5432           │\n  └─────────────┘   └──────────────────┘\n```\n\n- One container serves everything\n- Single K8s Service, dual ingress if the service has both API and browser users\n- Cloud SQL sidecar if needed (opt-in)\n- IQ Portal follows this pattern — Express.js serves API on `/api/*` and static Nuxt SPA on `/*`, all on port 8080\n\n### Pattern 2 — Multi-Container Pod (Frontend + Backend)\n\n**Use when**: Service has separate frontend (Nuxt SSR) and backend (FastAPI) processes that are tightly coupled\n**Example**: HR Fraud (Nuxt 3 SSR + FastAPI + cloud-sql-proxy)\n\n```\nPod:\n  ┌──────────────┐   ┌──────────────┐   ┌──────────────────┐\n  │  frontend     │   │  backend      │   │  cloud-sql-proxy │\n  │  (Nuxt SSR)   │   │  (FastAPI)    │   │  :5432           │\n  │  :3000        │──▶│  :8000        │──▶│                  │\n  └──────────────┘   └──────────────┘   └──────────────────┘\n     localhost:8000       localhost:5432\n```\n\n- Frontend calls backend on `localhost:8000` (zero network hop)\n- Two K8s Services on the same pod: one for Kong (API), one for Gateway API (frontend)\n- Atomic deploys — frontend and backend always update together\n- Two Docker images built from same repo, both tags updated in one gitops commit\n\n### Pattern 3 — Standalone Frontend\n\n**Use when**: Nuxt/Vue SPA with no backend process (API served by another service)\n**Example**: HR Portal (frontend-only, calls external APIs)\n\n```\nPod:\n  ┌──────────────┐\n  │  frontend     │\n  │  (Nuxt)       │\n  │  :3000        │\n  └──────────────┘\n```\n\n- Single container, single Service\n- Gateway API + IAP ingress only (no Kong)\n- Namespace: `{env}-web-apps`\n\n### Pattern 4 — Python MCP Server\n\n**Use when**: MCP protocol server (most common service type, ~14 services)\n**Example**: `kon-mcp-gateway`, `kon-mcp-ragengine`, etc.\n\n```\nPod:\n  ┌──────────────┐\n  │  app          │\n  │  (FastMCP)    │\n  │  :8000        │\n  └──────────────┘\n```\n\n- Single container, port 8000\n- Kong ingress only (no browser frontend)\n- Namespace: `{env}-mcp-servers`\n- Lightweight resources (100m/256Mi)\n- Most have no database — those that do get Cloud SQL sidecar\n\n---\n\n## Standard Migration Guide Structure\n\nWhen asked to create a migration guide for a service, follow this structure (derived from the IQ Portal guide):\n\n### Investigation Phase\n\nBefore writing, explore the service directory thoroughly:\n\n1. **Directory structure** — full tree\n2. **Dockerfile(s)** — base images, stages, security posture\n3. **docker-compose.yml / cloudbuild.yaml** — current orchestration and CI\n4. **Backend stack** — framework, language version, config pattern, database connection\n5. **Frontend** — framework, build output (SSR vs SPA vs static), API base URL config\n6. **Environment variables** — `.env.example`, `config.py`/`constants.ts`, all `process.env`/`os.environ` refs\n7. **Database** — migration system (Alembic, custom SQL, Prisma), connection method (Cloud SQL socket, local Postgres, Firestore)\n8. **GCP service dependencies** — Cloud Storage, Vertex AI, Pub/Sub, Cloud Tasks, Secret Manager, Firestore\n9. **Health endpoints** — path, what they check, suitability for K8s probes\n10. **Existing CI/CD** — Cloud Build, GitHub Actions, Bitbucket triggers\n\n### Guide Sections (10 steps)\n\n| Step | Title | Content |\n|------|-------|---------|\n| Header | Service metadata | Service name mapping, target org, phase, status |\n| 1 | Current State Assessment | Architecture diagram, tech stack table, GCP dependencies table |\n| 2 | Target State | Pod diagram with containers, ingress paths, key differences table |\n| 3 | Pre-requisites | Checklist of platform, IAM, and infrastructure requirements |\n| Step 1 | Create New Repository | Create from template, copy source, remove Cloud Build config, add CODEOWNERS + .dockerignore |\n| Step 2 | Application Code Changes | **Only changes required for K8s** — health endpoints, connection string adjustments, env var updates. List every file that changes and why. Most services need minimal/zero code changes. |\n| Step 3 | Dockerfile Changes | Pin base image digests, remove Docker HEALTHCHECK (K8s probes replace it), other security hardening |\n| Step 4 | Helm Chart | `chart/Chart.yaml` + `chart/values.yaml` using base chart dependency. Include: containers, probes, resources, cloudSqlProxy, serviceAccount, ingress, networkPolicy, security contexts, volumes |\n| Step 5 | GitHub Actions CI | `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling the shared standard pipeline. Build args, env-specific variables, branch→env mapping |\n| Step 6 | Secrets Migration | Map every secret from Cloud Run / Secret Manager to ExternalSecret CRD keys |\n| Step 7 | GitOps Configuration | Environment values files in `kix-platform-infrastructure` for dev/qa/prod |\n| Step 8 | Database Migration | Strategy for schema (reuse existing DB vs fresh), migration runner behaviour, connectivity test |\n| Step 9 | Verification & Smoke Tests | kubectl commands to verify pod health, probe output, service/ingress. curl commands for functional validation. |\n| Step 10 | DNS & Domain Cutover | New domain records, Cloud Run decommission steps |\n| Appendix A | Full Environment Variable Mapping | Complete table: every env var, its source on Cloud Run, source on kix, value |\n| Appendix B | Rollback Procedure | ArgoCD revert, kubectl rollout undo, Cloud Run fallback |\n\n---\n\n## Key Architectural Decisions\n\nReference these when making decisions in migration guides:\n\n### Database Connectivity\n- **Cloud Run**: Unix socket via VPC connector (`DB_HOST=/cloudsql/project:region:instance`)\n- **kix**: Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar, TCP on `localhost:5432`\n- Most apps support both — check the DB connection code for `DATABASE_URL` or individual `DB_HOST`/`DB_PORT` vars\n- If the app currently hardcodes a Unix socket path, that's a code change. If it uses `DB_HOST` env var, it's just a config change.\n\n### LiteLLM / LLM Access\n- All LLM traffic routes through Kong: `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/llm`\n- If a service uses `LITELLM_ENDPOINT`, `LITELLM_BASE_URL`, or similar — change to the Kong internal URL\n- NetworkPolicy enforces Kong-only access to `shared-litellm` namespace\n\n### MCP Gateway Access\n- Services calling MCP Gateway use: `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway`\n- Replace any direct MCP Gateway URLs (`http://localhost:8000` or Cloud Run URLs)\n\n### Secrets\n- **Never in Helm values or Git.** All secrets go through External Secrets Operator → GCP Secret Manager → K8s Secrets\n- Non-secret config (GCP project IDs, region, model names, bucket names, URLs) can go in Helm `env` block\n- Secrets are mounted via `envFrom.secretRef` pointing to an ExternalSecret-managed K8s Secret\n\n### Health Probes\n- **Liveness**: Lightweight, app-internal only (is the process alive?). Path: `/health` or `/healthz`. No external dependency checks.\n- **Readiness**: Can include dependency checks (DB, GCP). Path: `/api/health` or `/readyz`. Failing readiness removes pod from service — acceptable for warm-up.\n- **Startup**: Use generous `failureThreshold` if the app runs migrations on boot or has slow initialization.\n\n### Ingress\n- **API-only services** (MCP servers, backend APIs): Kong ingress only\n- **Frontend-only services** (standalone Nuxt): Gateway API + IAP ingress only\n- **Services with both API and browser users**: Dual ingress — both Kong and Gateway API routes pointing to the same pod (different or same port)\n\n### Port Conventions\n| Stack | Default Port | Notes |\n|-------|-------------|-------|\n| Python FastAPI/FastMCP | 8000 | Standard across all Python services |\n| Node.js Express (production) | 8080 | Cloud Run convention, kept for consistency |\n| Nuxt SSR | 3000 | Default Nuxt port |\n\n---\n\n## Dockerfile Conventions\n\n### Python Services\n```dockerfile\nFROM python:3.11-slim@sha256:<digest> AS builder\nWORKDIR /app\nCOPY requirements.txt .\nRUN pip install --no-cache-dir --prefix=/install -r requirements.txt\n\nFROM python:3.11-slim@sha256:<digest> AS runtime\nCOPY --from=builder /install /usr/local\nCOPY . .\nRUN adduser --disabled-password --uid 65532 appuser\nUSER appuser\nENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1\nEXPOSE 8000\nCMD [\"uvicorn\", \"app.main:app\", \"--host\", \"0.0.0.0\", \"--port\", \"8000\"]\n```\n\n### Node.js Services\n```dockerfile\nFROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest> AS dependencies\nWORKDIR /app\nCOPY package*.json ./\nRUN npm ci --omit=dev\n\nFROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest> AS builder\nWORKDIR /app\nCOPY package*.json ./\nRUN npm ci\nCOPY . .\nRUN npm run build\n\nFROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest> AS runner\nRUN addgroup -g 1001 -S nodejs && adduser -S nodejs -u 1001\nCOPY --from=dependencies /app/node_modules /app/node_modules\nCOPY --from=builder /app/.output /app/.output\nUSER nodejs\nEXPOSE 8080\nCMD [\"node\", \".output/server/index.mjs\"]\n```\n\n### Required Changes for K8s (all Dockerfiles)\n1. **Pin base image digests** — replace `python:3.11-slim` with `python:3.11-slim@sha256:<digest>`\n2. **Remove HEALTHCHECK instruction** — K8s probes replace it\n3. **Ensure non-root user** — `USER` instruction must be present\n4. **No secrets in layers** — use `--secret` mount or env vars at runtime\n\n---\n\n## CI/CD Conventions\n\n### Standard ci.yaml (every repo)\n```yaml\nname: CI\non:\n  push:\n    branches: [main, develop, staging]\n  pull_request:\n    branches: [main]\n\njobs:\n  pipeline:\n    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1\n    with:\n      service-name: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}\n      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile\n      chart-path: ./chart\n      python-version: \"3.11\"     # OR node-version: \"22\"\n    secrets: inherit\n```\n\n### Build Args for Frontends\nIf the frontend embeds config at build time (e.g., `VITE_*` variables):\n```yaml\n    with:\n      build-args: |\n        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}\n        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}\n        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}\n```\n\n### Branch → Environment Mapping\n| Branch | Environment | Namespace suffix |\n|--------|-------------|-----------------|\n| `develop` | dev | `dev-*` |\n| `staging` | qa | `qa-*` |\n| `main` | prod | `prod-*` |\n\n---\n\n## Helm Chart Conventions\n\n### chart/Chart.yaml\n```yaml\napiVersion: v2\nname: <service-name>           # e.g., kix-mcp-gateway\ndescription: <one-liner>\nversion: 0.1.0\ntype: application\ndependencies:\n  - name: base-service-chart\n    version: \"~1.0.0\"\n    repository: \"oci://europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/helm-charts\"\n```\n\n### chart/values.yaml Structure\nAll values nested under `base-service-chart:` key:\n\n```yaml\nbase-service-chart:\n  nameOverride: <service-name>\n  image:\n    repository: europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/docker/<service-name>\n    tag: \"latest\"  # overridden by CI\n  containers:\n    app:\n      port: <8000|8080|3000>\n      env: { ... }               # Non-secret config\n      envFrom:\n        - secretRef:\n            name: <service-name>-secrets\n      resources:\n        requests: { cpu: ..., memory: ... }\n        limits: { cpu: ..., memory: ... }\n  cloudSqlProxy:                  # Only if service uses Cloud SQL\n    enabled: true/false\n    instanceConnectionName: \"project:region:instance\"\n    port: 5432\n  serviceAccount:\n    create: true\n    annotations:\n      iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: <sa>@<project>.iam.gserviceaccount.com\n  probes:\n    liveness: { httpGet: { path: /health, port: <port> } }\n    readiness: { httpGet: { path: /readyz, port: <port> } }\n  autoscaling:\n    enabled: true\n    minReplicas: 1\n    maxReplicas: 5\n  services:\n    api:                          # Kong ingress\n      port: <port>\n      ingress:\n        class: kong\n        path: /api/<service-name>\n    frontend:                     # Gateway API ingress (only if browser-facing)\n      port: <port>\n      ingress:\n        class: gateway-api\n        host: <service>.app.konecta.io\n  podSecurityContext:\n    runAsNonRoot: true\n    seccompProfile: { type: RuntimeDefault }\n  containerSecurityContext:\n    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false\n    readOnlyRootFilesystem: true\n    capabilities: { drop: [ALL] }\n  networkPolicy:\n    enabled: true\n```\n\n---\n\n## GitOps Values Structure\n\nIn `kix-platform-infrastructure/environments/{env}/{domain}/{service}.yaml`:\n\n```yaml\nbase-service-chart:\n  image:\n    tag: \"sha-abc1234\"           # Updated by CI pipeline\n  containers:\n    app:\n      env:\n        # Environment-specific overrides only\n        LOG_LEVEL: \"debug\"       # dev\n        ALLOWED_ORIGINS: \"https://<service>.dev.konecta.io\"\n  autoscaling:\n    minReplicas: 1               # dev: 1, prod: 2\n```\n\n---\n\n## Service Inventory (In Scope)\n\n| Current Repo | New Repo | (Python) | No | P3 |\n| `kon-demo-prototypes` | `kix-demo-prototypes` | demos | api-services | Single container | No | P3 |\n\n### Out of Scope\n- **CrewAI Flows & Tools** (12 repos): `kon-flow-*`, `kon-crewai-*`, `kon-crew-*`\n- **Infrastructure Repos** (4): `kon-gcp-platform`, `kon-argocd-*`, `kon-tic-apps-terraform`\n- **Config/Proxies** (2): `kon-litellm`, `kon-argocd-platform`\n\n---\n\n## Common Investigation Patterns\n\n### Finding Database Usage\n```\n# Check for Cloud SQL connections\ngrep -r \"cloudsql\\|DB_HOST\\|DATABASE_URL\\|SQLALCHEMY\\|asyncpg\\|pg\\b\" backend/ --include=\"*.py\" --include=\"*.ts\"\n\n# Check for Firestore\ngrep -r \"firestore\\|Firestore\" backend/ --include=\"*.py\" --include=\"*.ts\"\n\n# Check for migration system\nls backend/alembic/ 2>/dev/null    # Python (Alembic)\nls backend/db/migrations/ 2>/dev/null  # Custom SQL migrations\n```\n\n### Finding GCP Dependencies\n```\n# Python\ngrep -r \"google.cloud\\|google-cloud\\|vertexai\\|aiplatform\" requirements.txt pyproject.toml\n\n# Node.js\ngrep -r \"@google-cloud\" package.json\n```\n\n### Finding Health Endpoints\n```\ngrep -r \"health\\|healthz\\|readyz\" backend/ --include=\"*.py\" --include=\"*.ts\" -l\n```\n\n### Finding All Environment Variables\n```\n# Python\ngrep -roh \"os\\.environ\\[.*?\\]\\|os\\.getenv(.*?)\" backend/ --include=\"*.py\" | sort -u\n\n# Node.js\ngrep -roh \"process\\.env\\.\\w+\" backend/ --include=\"*.ts\" | sort -u\n```\n\n---\n\n## Important Reminders\n\n1. **Read the service's CLAUDE.md first** if one exists (e.g., `kon-app-hrfraud/CLAUDE.md`). It contains service-specific conventions.\n2. **Minimal code changes.** The goal is to deploy the same application on K8s. Don't refactor, improve, or add features. Only change what's necessary for K8s compatibility.\n3. **No secrets in Git.** Every secret must go through ExternalSecret CRDs → GCP Secret Manager.\n4. **Nothing is production.** No downtime concerns. Services migrate to new domains.\n5. **IQ Portal is the reference.** When in doubt, check how it was handled in `IQ_PORTAL_MIGRATION_GUIDE.md`.\n6. **The blueprint is the source of truth** for architecture, pipeline, security, and namespace decisions. Don't contradict it.\n7. **Each service's cloudbuild.yaml gets deleted.** Replaced by `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling the shared pipeline.\n8. **Docker HEALTHCHECK instructions get removed.** K8s probes replace them.\n9. **Base image digests get pinned.** No mutable tags (`:latest`, `:3.11-slim`).\n10. **LiteLLM always goes through Kong.** Never set a direct LiteLLM URL.\n"
}

   600→
   601→## 7. Secure Docker Builds
   602→
   603→### 7.1 Base Image Policy
   604→
   605→| Stack | Builder Stage | Runtime Stage |
   606→|-------|--------------|---------------|
   607→| Python (standard) | `python:3.x-slim` | `python:3.x-slim` |
   608→| Python (ML: opencv, whisper, ffmpeg) | `python:3.x-slim` + apt deps | `python:3.x-slim` + runtime-only libs |
   609→| Node.js | `node:20-alpine` | `gcr.io/distroless/nodejs20-debian12` |
   610→
   611→- Pin to **full digest** in CI (updated monthly by Renovate/Dependabot)
   612→- Parameterize Python version via `ARG PYTHON_VERSION=3.11`
   613→- **Never use `:latest`**
   614→
   615→### 7.2 Multi-Stage Build Patterns
   616→
   617→**Python services:**
   618→```
   619→Stage 1 "builder": python:3.x-slim → install build deps → pip install --prefix=/install
   620→Stage 2 "runtime": python:3.x-slim → COPY --from=builder /install → non-root USER → ENTRYPOINT
   621→```
   622→
   623→**Node.js services:**
   624→```
   625→Stage 1 "deps": node:20-alpine → npm ci
   626→Stage 2 "builder": COPY source → npm run build → npm prune --omit=dev
   627→Stage 3 "runtime": distroless/nodejs20 → COPY .output → no shell → ENTRYPOINT
   628→```
   629→
   630→### 7.3 Security Hardening
   631→
   632→- **Non-root user** (UID 65532) in all images, enforced by `runAsNonRoot: true` in pod spec
   633→- **Read-only root filesystem** — writable paths via `emptyDir` mounts (`/tmp`, `/app/.cache`)
   634→- **No shell** in Node.js production images (distroless)
   635→- **Drop ALL capabilities** in pod security context
   636→- **No `.env` files** baked into images; all config via K8s secrets/configmaps
   637→- `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1` for Python images
   638→
   639→### 7.4 Dependency Management
   640→
   641→- Python: `pip-compile` or `uv lock` for pinned requirements with hashes
   642→- Node.js: `npm ci` only (never `npm install` in CI)
   643→- Weekly Renovate/Dependabot PRs for dependency updates
   644→
   645→### 7.5 Build Metadata (OCI Labels)
   646→
   647→All images include: `org.opencontainers.image.created`, `.revision` (git SHA), `.version`, `.source`, `.title`, `.vendor` — final stage only.
   648→
   649→### 7.6 Image Size Targets
   650→
   651→| Category | Target | Optimization |
   652→|----------|--------|-------------|
   653→| Python standard | < 200 MB | Multi-stage, no cache, strip pyc |
   654→| Python ML/heavy | < 500 MB | opencv-headless, models as external volumes |
   655→| Node.js (Nuxt) | < 80 MB | Distroless runtime, npm prune |
   656→
   657→---
   658→
   659→## 8. CI/CD Pipeline (GitHub Actions + ArgoCD)
   660→
   661→### 8.1 Standard Pipeline Architecture (Repo-Per-Service)
   662→
   663→Every service repo follows the **same pipeline** by calling reusable workflows from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows`. A service onboards by adding a single `ci.yaml` file.
   664→
   665→**Shared Workflows Repo (`konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/`)**:
   666→
   667→```
   668→kix-platform-ci-workflows/
   669→└── .github/workflows/
   670→    ├── standard-pipeline.yaml      # Main orchestrator (called by every service)
   671→    ├── _lint-and-test.yaml         # Reusable: Language-aware lint + unit tests
   672→    ├── _secret-scan.yaml           # Reusable: Trivy filesystem secret detection
   673→    ├── _sca.yaml                   # Reusable: Dependency-Track SCA analysis
   674→    ├── _sast.yaml                  # Reusable: SonarQube static analysis
   675→    ├── _build-image.yaml           # Reusable: Docker build + BuildKit cache
   676→    ├── _container-scan-sign.yaml   # Reusable: Trivy image scan + SBOM (Syft) + Cosign sign
   677→    ├── _push-registry.yaml         # Reusable: Push to Artifact Registry
   678→    ├── _update-gitops.yaml         # Reusable: Dispatch to kix-platform-infrastructure
   679→    ├── _promote.yaml               # Reusable: Promote image tag between environments
   680→    └── _dast.yaml                  # Reusable: OWASP ZAP baseline scan (post-deploy, pre-prod)
   681→```
   682→
   683→**Standard Service Repo Pipeline (every service copies this)**:
   684→
   685→```yaml
   686→# .github/workflows/ci.yaml (in each service repo — identical across all repos)
   687→name: CI
   688→on:
   689→  push:
   690→    branches: [main]
   691→  pull_request:
   692→    branches: [main]
   693→  workflow_dispatch:
   694→
   695→jobs:
   696→  pipeline:
   697→    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1
   698→    with:
   699→      service-name: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}
   700→      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile
   701→      chart-path: ./chart
   702→      python-version: "3.11"     # or node-version: "20" for Node.js services
   703→    secrets: inherit
   704→```
   705→
   706→**That's it.** Every service repo has one small workflow file. All logic lives in the shared workflows repo. Updates to the pipeline roll out to all services by bumping the `@v1` tag.
   707→
   708→**Cross-org workflow sharing**: GitHub Enterprise allows sharing workflows across organizations. The `kix-platform-ci-workflows` repo is set to **internal** visibility, making it callable from `konecta-ix-mcp-servers`, `konecta-ix-apps`, `konecta-ix-workloads`, etc.
   709→
   710→### 8.2 Standard Pipeline Stages
   711→
   712→Pipeline stages aligned with the **Konecta DevSecOps Handbook**. Failure at any stage stops the pipeline.
   713→
   714→**Code Pipeline (every PR and merge):**
   715→
   716→```
   717→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
   718→│  1. LINT &   │─▶│  2. SECRET   │─▶│  3. SCA      │─▶│  4. SAST     │─▶│  5. BUILD &  │
   719→│     TEST     │  │     SCAN     │  │              │  │              │  │  UNIT TEST   │
   720→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
   721→  PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        merge only
   722→  ├ ruff/eslint     ├ Trivy fs        ├ Syft SBOM       ├ SonarQube       ├ BuildKit
   723→  ├ type checks     │ --scanners      │ generation       │ analysis        ├ multi-stage
   724→  └ pip audit/      │ secret          ├ Dependency-      │ quality gate:   ├ unit tests
   725→    npm audit       └ stops on          Track upload     │ no critical/    └ OCI labels
   726→                      any finding     └ stops on           high issues
   727→                                        critical/high
   728→
   729→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
   730→│  6. CONTAINER│─▶│  7. PUSH &   │─▶│  8. DEPLOY   │
   731→│  SCAN & SIGN │  │  REGISTRY    │  │  (GitOps)    │
   732→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
   733→  merge only        merge only        merge only
   734→  ├ Trivy image     ├ Push to AR      ├ Dispatch to
   735→  │ scan            ├ Tag: sha-xxx    │ gitops repo
   736→  ├ SBOM attach     └ Tag: v1.2.3    ├ Update dev
   737→  │ (Syft)            (on git tag)    │ values.yaml
   738→  └ Cosign sign                       └ ArgoCD syncs
   739→    (OIDC)
   740→```
   741→
   742→**Promotion Pipeline (dev → QA → prod):**
   743→
   744→```
   745→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
   746→│  9. DAST     │─▶│ 10. PROMOTE  │─▶│ 11. MONITOR  │
   747→│  (pre-prod)  │  │              │  │              │
   748→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
   749→  post-deploy       manual trigger    continuous
   750→  ├ OWASP ZAP       ├ Copy image      ├ Prometheus
   751→  │ baseline scan   │ tag to next     ├ Grafana
   752→  │ against QA      │ env values      └ alerts on
   753→  └ blocks prod     └ PR to gitops      anomalies
   754→    promotion if
   755→    findings
   756→```
   757→
   758→**On PR**: Stages 1–4 run (lint, secret scan, SCA, SAST). Fast feedback, no build.
   759→**On merge to main**: Stages 1–8 run. Full build, scan, sign, push, deploy to dev.
   760→**On promotion**: Stage 9 (DAST) runs against QA before prod promotion is allowed.
   761→
   762→### 8.3 Image Tagging Strategy
   763→
   764→| Tag | When | Purpose |
   765→|-----|------|---------|
   766→| `sha-{7-char}` | Every build | Immutable build identifier |
   767→| `v{major}.{minor}.{patch}` | On git tag | Release version |
   768→| `{env}-latest` | On promotion | Environment pointer (mutable) |
   769→
   770→**Rule**: SHA tags are immutable. Never overwrite. Promotion copies the SHA tag reference, not the image.
   771→
   772→### 8.4 Security Gates
   773→
   774→Per the Konecta DevSecOps Handbook, every gate is fail-stop — pipeline halts immediately on critical/high findings.
   775→
   776→| Gate | Tool | Stage | Trigger | Action on Failure |
   777→|------|------|-------|---------|-------------------|
   778→| **Secret Detection** | Trivy (`--scanners secret`) | 2 (pre-build) | Every PR + merge | Stop pipeline, no exceptions |
   779→| **Dependency Vulnerabilities** | Dependency-Track (via Syft SBOM) | 3 (pre-build) | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   780→| **SAST Quality Gate** | SonarQube | 4 (pre-build) | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   781→| **Container Vulnerabilities** | Trivy (image scan) | 6 (post-build) | Every merge | Stop on critical/high |
   782→| **Image Signature** | Cosign (Sigstore/OIDC) | 6 (post-build) | Every merge | Unsigned images blocked |
   783→| **DAST Baseline** | OWASP ZAP | 9 (post-deploy QA) | Before prod promotion | Block promotion on findings |
   784→| **Admission Control** | Kyverno | Deploy-time | Every sync | Reject unsigned images, block `:latest` |
   785→
   786→**Tool details:**
   787→
   788→- **Trivy secret scan**: Scans source filesystem for hardcoded secrets, keys, tokens before any build occurs. Zero tolerance — any finding stops the pipeline.
   789→- **SonarQube**: Hosted instance in `shared-devsecops` namespace. Runs static analysis on every PR. Quality gate: no new critical or high severity issues. Results posted as PR comment via GitHub integration.
   790→- **Dependency-Track**: Hosted instance in `shared-devsecops` namespace. Syft generates CycloneDX SBOMs from source dependencies (stage 3) and from built images (stage 6). Both uploaded to Dependency-Track for continuous vulnerability tracking. Pipeline stops on critical/high.
   791→- **OWASP ZAP**: Runs as a containerized baseline scan against the service's QA endpoint after deployment. Blocks promotion to prod if baseline vulnerabilities found. Integrated into the `_promote.yaml` workflow as a pre-condition.
   792→- **Cosign**: Keyless signing via GitHub OIDC → Sigstore. No long-lived keys.
   793→- **Kyverno**: Cluster admission controller enforces signed images, blocks `:latest` tag, requires Konecta Artifact Registry as source.
   794→
   795→**Vulnerability remediation SLAs** (per DevSecOps Handbook):
   796→
   797→| Severity | Detection → Triage | Triage → Fix | Fix → Deploy |
   798→|----------|-------------------|-------------|-------------|
   799→| Critical | 4 hours | 24 hours | 48 hours |
   800→| High | 24 hours | 7 days | 14 days |
   801→| Medium | 7 days | 30 days | 45 days |
   802→| Low | 30 days | 90 days | Next release |
   803→
   804→### 8.5 GitOps Flow (Cross-Repo)
   805→
   806→```
   807→konecta-ix-mcp-servers/                    konecta-ix-platform/
   808→kix-mcp-gateway                            kix-platform-infrastructure
   809→────────────────────                       ───────────────────────────
   810→1. PR merged to main
   811→2. Pipeline builds sha-abc1234
   812→3. Image pushed to Artifact Registry
   813→4. Pipeline triggers repository_dispatch ──▶ 5. Automated PR or direct commit:
   814→                                                environments/dev/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
   815→                                                  image.tag: sha-abc1234
   816→                                             6. ArgoCD detects change → syncs dev
   817→```
   818→
   819→**Cross-org dispatch**: A GitHub App installed across all orgs (`konecta-ix-platform`, `konecta-ix-mcp-servers`, `konecta-ix-apps`, etc.) enables the `_update-gitops.yaml` workflow to commit to `kix-platform-infrastructure`. The App has write access only to the gitops repo, scoped to minimal permissions.
   820→
   821→### 8.6 Environment Promotion
   822→
   823→| Environment | Trigger | Sync Mode | Change Window |
   824→|-------------|---------|-----------|---------------|
   825→| Dev | Every merge to main | Auto-sync | 24/7 |
   826→| QA | Manual workflow dispatch or PR to kix-platform-infrastructure | Manual sync | 08:00-18:00 UTC |
   827→| Prod | Manual workflow dispatch + approval | Manual sync | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |
   828→
   829→**Promotion workflow** (from `kix-platform-ci-workflows/_promote.yaml`):
   830→
   831→**Dev → QA:**
   832→1. Engineer triggers `promote` workflow on the service repo
   833→2. Workflow copies the image tag from `environments/dev/{service}.yaml` to `environments/qa/{service}.yaml`
   834→3. Creates a PR on kix-platform-infrastructure
   835→4. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to QA
   836→
   837→**QA → Prod (includes DAST gate):**
   838→1. Engineer triggers `promote` workflow targeting prod
   839→2. Workflow runs **OWASP ZAP baseline scan** against the service's QA endpoint (`_dast.yaml`)
   840→3. If ZAP finds baseline vulnerabilities → promotion blocked, findings reported
   841→4. If ZAP passes → workflow copies image tag to `environments/prod/{service}.yaml`
   842→5. Creates a PR on kix-platform-infrastructure (requires approval)
   843→6. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to prod
   844→
   845→### 8.7 Rollback Strategy
   846→
   847→- **Automated** (prod): Prometheus alert on error rate >5% for 3 min → webhook reverts commit in kix-platform-infrastructure → ArgoCD syncs previous image
   848→- **Manual**: Engineer reverts the `environments/prod/{service}.yaml` entry to previous SHA tag via PR to kix-platform-infrastructure
   849→- **RTO target**: <5 min (detection + revert + sync)
   850→- **Git as source of truth**: Every rollback is a visible Git commit in kix-platform-infrastructure
   851→
   852→### 8.8 Secrets Management (CI)
   853→
   854→- **GitHub OIDC** → GCP Workload Identity Pool (no JSON keys, configured per org)
   855→- Dynamic secret fetching from GCP Secret Manager at build time
   856→- Build secrets via `docker build --secret` (never in image layers)
   857→- Token lifetime ~1 hour, auto-refreshing, full audit trail
   858→- **GitHub App** for cross-org dispatch (kix-platform-infrastructure commits), installed across all orgs, scoped to minimal permissions
   859→
   860→### 8.9 Onboarding a New Service
   861→
   862→Adding a new service to the platform:
   863→
   864→1. **Create repo** in the appropriate org from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template`:
   865→   - Standard `Dockerfile` (Python or Node.js variant)
   866→   - Standard `chart/` directory with `Chart.yaml` depending on base chart
   867→   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling shared pipeline
   868→   - Standard `.dockerignore`, `CODEOWNERS`
   869→2. **Add to gitops repo**: Create `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/{service-name}.yaml` in `kix-platform-infrastructure`
   870→3. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** via ApplicationSet git directory generator
   871→4. **First push** triggers the standard pipeline — fully operational
   872→
   873→No pipeline code to write. No Helm templates to create. Just fill in `values.yaml` with service-specific config.
   874→
   875→---
   876→
   877→## 9. Security Architecture
   878→
   879→### 9.1 Container Image Security
   880→
   881→- **Multi-stage builds**: Builder dependencies never ship in runtime image
   882→- **Base image policy**: Pinned digests, updated monthly, slim/distroless only
   883→- **Vulnerability scanning**: Trivy pre-push, CRITICAL/HIGH block deployment
   884→- **Image signing**: Cosign keyless (OIDC) — every image cryptographically signed
   885→- **SBOM**: Generated for every build, stored as OCI annotation
   886→- **Registry**: Artifact Registry with vulnerability scanning enabled, IAM-restricted access
   887→
   888→### 9.2 Supply Chain Security
   889→
   890→- **Dependency pinning**: Hash-verified `requirements.txt` (Python), `package-lock.json` (Node.js)
   891→- **Dependency scanning**: `pip audit` / `npm audit` in CI, Renovate for updates
   892→- **GitHub Actions hardening**:
   893→  - Pin action versions by SHA (not tag)
   894→  - Minimal `GITHUB_TOKEN` permissions per workflow
   895→  - No third-party actions without security review
   896→  - OIDC for all GCP interactions (no stored credentials)
   897→
   898→### 9.3 Runtime Security
   899→
   900→- **Pod Security Standards**: `restricted` profile enforced via namespace labels
   901→  - `runAsNonRoot: true`
   902→  - `readOnlyRootFilesystem: true`
   903→  - `allowPrivilegeEscalation: false`
   904→  - `capabilities.drop: [ALL]`
   905→  - `seccompProfile.type: RuntimeDefault`
   906→- **No privileged containers**. No `hostNetwork`, `hostPID`, `hostIPC`.
   907→- Writable paths only via explicit `emptyDir` volume mounts
   908→
   909→### 9.4 Secrets Management (Runtime)
   910→
   911→**External Secrets Operator (ESO) + GCP Secret Manager**
   912→
   913→- ESO installed in `shared-secrets` namespace
   914→- `SecretStore` per environment, authenticated via Workload Identity
   915→- `ExternalSecret` CRDs in each service Helm chart pull secrets from GCP
   916→- Secrets never stored in Git or Helm values files
   917→- Automatic rotation support via ESO refresh interval
   918→
   919→**Workload Identity for GCP API access**:
   920→```yaml
   921→serviceAccount:
   922→  annotations:
   923→    iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: {service}@{project}.iam.gserviceaccount.com
   924→```
   925→No service account JSON keys. Ever.
   926→
   927→### 9.5 Network Security
   928→
   929→- **Dual load balancer isolation**: API traffic (Kong) and frontend traffic (Gateway API + IAP) on separate LBs — blast radius is contained per path
   930→- **IAP (Identity-Aware Proxy)** on frontend LB: Users authenticate via Google/OIDC before reaching Nuxt frontends. Access controlled by IAM policies on the IAP resource.
   931→- **Cloud Armor WAF** on both load balancers (OWASP top-10 rules, DDoS protection)
   932→- **Default deny ingress** on all application namespaces
   933→- **Explicit NetworkPolicies** per service: allow only expected sources
   934→- **GCP health check CIDRs** allowed (35.191.0.0/16, 130.211.0.0/22)
   935→- **Private cluster**: No public node IPs, egress via Cloud NAT
   936→- **DNS policies**: Restrict external DNS resolution where possible
   937→
   938→### 9.6 RBAC & Namespace Isolation
   939→
   940→- **Service accounts per namespace** with least-privilege Roles
   941→- **ArgoCD Projects** restrict which namespaces each team can deploy to
   942→- **Namespace ResourceQuotas** prevent resource exhaustion attacks
   943→- **Kyverno policies**:
   944→  - Enforce signed images (prod)
   945→  - Block `latest` tag
   946→  - Require resource limits
   947→  - Enforce label standards
   948→
   949→### 9.7 Admission Control
   950→
   951→- **Kyverno** (preferred over OPA/Gatekeeper for readability):
   952→  - Verify Cosign image signatures
   953→  - Enforce pod security standards
   954→  - Block privileged containers
   955→  - Require Konecta Artifact Registry as image source
   956→  - Enforce annotation/label standards
   957→
   958→---
   959→
   960→## 10. Observability
   961→
   962→### Stack
   963→| Component | Tool | Purpose |
   964→|-----------|------|---------|
   965→| Metrics | Prometheus | Scrape K8s + application metrics |
   966→| Dashboards | Grafana | Visualization, alerting |
   967→| Tracing | Jaeger | Distributed traces (OpenTelemetry) |
   968→| Logging | GCP Cloud Logging | Centralized log aggregation |
   969→| Instrumentation | OpenTelemetry SDK | App-level traces, metrics, logs |
   970→| Collection | OTel Collector (DaemonSet) | Receive, process, export telemetry |
   971→
   972→### Application Integration
   973→- All services emit OpenTelemetry traces/metrics (already instrumented)
   974→- OTel Collector as DaemonSet in `shared-monitoring` receives and exports
   975→- Prometheus scrapes `/metrics` endpoints via PodMonitor/ServiceMonitor CRDs
   976→- Grafana dashboards per service tier (AI workloads, APIs, frontends)
   977→
   978→### Key Alerts
   979→- Pod CrashLoopBackOff > 2 restarts
   980→- Error rate > 5% for 3 min (triggers rollback)
   981→- P95 latency > 2x baseline
   982→- HPA at max replicas for > 10 min
   983→- Certificate expiry < 14 days
   984→- ArgoCD sync failure
   985→
   986→---
   987→
   988→## 11. Cost & Operational Considerations
   989→
   990→### Cloud Run vs GKE Cost Model
   991→
   992→| Factor | Cloud Run | GKE (Target) |
   993→|--------|-----------|---------------|
   994→| Billing | Per-request (scale to zero) | Reserved capacity (always-on) |
   995→| Idle cost | $0 | Node pool minimum |
   996→| Burst cost | High (cold starts + per-invocation) | Lower (pre-provisioned pods) |
   997→| Predictability | Variable | Predictable |
   998→
   999→**Mitigation for idle cost**: GKE Autopilot scales node pools down. Low-traffic Tier 3 services can run with 1 replica (HPA min=1). Consider `minReplicas: 0` with KEDA for truly idle services.
  1000→
  1001→### Operational Overhead
  1002→- **Helm chart maintenance**: Base chart library reduces per-service overhead
  1003→- **ArgoCD operations**: Self-healing reduces manual intervention
  1004→- **Monitoring**: Prometheus + Grafana replaces fragmented Cloud Run metrics
  1005→- **On-call**: Standardized runbooks across all services (same platform, same patterns)
  1006→
  1007→### Disaster Recovery
  1008→- **Multi-region**: Secondary GKE cluster in eu-west4 (already provisioned)
  1009→- **Failover**: DNS-based failover to secondary cluster
  1010→- **Data**: Cloud SQL cross-region replicas, GCS multi-regional buckets
  1011→- **RPO**: Near-zero (managed services handle replication)
  1012→- **RTO**: <15 min (ArgoCD can deploy to secondary cluster from same Git state)
  1013→
  1014→---
  1015→
  1016→## 12. Risk Register
  1017→
  1018→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
  1019→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
  1020→| Resource sizing incorrect for K8s | Medium | Medium | Start with generous limits, tune based on metrics after migration |
  1021→| Secret exposure during migration | Low | Critical | External Secrets Operator, no secrets in Git, Workload Identity only |
  1022→| ArgoCD sync storms (24 services) | Low | Medium | Sync waves, rate limiting, progressive rollout |
  1023→| Team unfamiliarity with Helm/K8s | Medium | Medium | Pilot phase in Phase 1, runbook documentation, training |
  1024→| Kong ingress misconfiguration | Low | High | Test routes per service before decommissioning Cloud Run |
  1025→| Shared workflow drift | Low | Medium | Versioned `@v1` tag, Renovate bumps, changelog for breaking changes |
  1026→| LiteLLM routing via Kong | Low | Medium | NetworkPolicy enforces Kong-only access; test LLM routes early in Phase 1 |
  1027→
  1028→---

     1→# Architecture Blueprint: Migration to KIX-Infrastructure Platform
     2→
     3→## Konecta TIC — Cloud Run to Kubernetes (Helm + ArgoCD + GitHub Actions)
     4→
     5→---
     6→
     7→## Table of Contents
     8→
     9→1. [Executive Summary](#1-executive-summary)
    10→2. [Current State](#2-current-state)
    11→3. [Target Architecture](#3-target-architecture)
    12→4. [Service Inventory & Tiering](#4-service-inventory--tiering)
    13→5. [Migration Strategy](#5-migration-strategy)
    14→6. [Kubernetes & Helm Chart Design](#6-kubernetes--helm-chart-design)
    15→7. [Secure Docker Builds](#7-secure-docker-builds)
