    21→
    22→---
    23→
    24→## 1. Executive Summary
    25→
    26→This blueprint defines the architecture for migrating ~24 microservices from GCP Cloud Run to the **kix-infrastructure** Kubernetes platform (CrewAI workflows, infrastructure repos, and config/proxies are out of scope). The migration adopts:
    27→
    28→- **Helm charts** for declarative Kubernetes packaging
    29→- **Secure multi-stage Docker builds** with vulnerability scanning and image signing
    30→- **GitHub Actions** for CI (build, scan, push)
    31→- **ArgoCD** for CD (GitOps-driven deployment across dev/qa/prod)
    32→
    33→**Key principles**: Simple, secure, auditable, repeatable.
    34→
    35→---
    36→
    37→## 2. Current State
    38→
    39→### Infrastructure
    40→- **Compute**: GCP Cloud Run (most services), GKE Autopilot (Kong, LiteLLM, ArgoCD), Cloud Functions (1 bot)
    41→- **Clusters**: GKE Autopilot in eu-west1/eu-west4, private with Cloud NAT
    42→- **CI/CD**: Cloud Build (8 services), GitHub Actions (Terraform only)
    43→- **Registry**: GCP Artifact Registry
    44→- **IaC**: Terraform with modular structure
    45→
    46→### Services In Scope (24 services)
    47→| Category | Count | Stack |
    48→|----------|-------|-------|
    49→| MCP Servers | 14 | Python (FastMCP) |
    50→| Business Apps | 4 | Python (FastAPI) + Node.js (Nuxt 3) |
    51→| Data Pipelines | 3 | Python |
    52→| Bots | 1 | Python (Cloud Functions) |
    53→| Prototypes | 2 | Python |
    54→
    55→**Out of scope**: CrewAI Flows & Tools (12), Infrastructure Repos (4), Config/Proxies (2) — these remain on their current platform or are handled separately.
    56→
    57→### External Dependencies (remain as managed services)
    58→- **Cloud SQL** (PostgreSQL 16+) — accessed via Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (see section 6.9)
    59→- **Firestore** (rate limiting, conversations)
    60→- **Vertex AI** (Gemini, Claude via Vertex, RAG Engine)
    61→- **Cloud Storage** (GCS)
    62→- **Pub/Sub** (async messaging)
    63→- **Google APIs** (Search, Maps, Drive, Chat)
    64→
    65→---
    66→
    67→## 3. Target Architecture
    68→
    69→### High-Level Component Diagram
    70→
    71→```
    72→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    73→│                           INTERNET                                      │
    74→└──────────┬──────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘
    75→           │ HTTPS (API traffic)              │ HTTPS (Frontend traffic)
    76→           │                                  │
    77→┌──────────▼──────────────────┐  ┌────────────▼────────────────────────┐
    78→│  GCP LB + Cloud Armor (WAF) │  │  GCP LB + Gateway API + IAM +       │
    79→│   (API ingress (KONG) {env} │  │  Cloud Armor(Frontend ingress) {env}│
    80→└──────────┬──────────────────┘  └────────────┬────────────────────────┘
    81→           │                                  │
    82→┌──────────▼──────────────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────┐
    83→│              KIX-INFRASTRUCTURE GKE CLUSTER (eu-west1)                  │
    84→│                        kd-ix-eur-{env}-gke                              │
    85→│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
    86→│  │  shared-kong (Kong Gateway)                                      │   │
    87→│  │    ├── routes to {env}-api-services, {env}-mcp-servers           │   │
    88→│  │    └── routes to shared-litellm (LLM Proxy) ← internal only      │   │
    89→│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
    90→│                                                                         │
    91→│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │
    92→│  │  Application Namespaces (per environment × domain)              │    │
    93→│  │                                                                 │    │
    94→│  │  {env}-api-services     → FastAPI backends, business apps,      │    │
    95→│  │                           bots, data pipelines                  │    │
    96→│  │  {env}-mcp-servers      → MCP protocol servers (SSE)            │    │
    97→│  │                                                                 │    │
    98→│  │  {env}-web-apps         → Nuxt 3 frontends  ← Frontend LB       │    │
    99→│  │                           (GKE Gateway API + IAM auth)          │    │
   100→│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
   101→│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
   102→│  │  shared-monitoring: Prometheus / Grafana / Jaeger / OTel         │   │
   103→│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
   104→│                                                                         │
   105→│  ┌───────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐                 │
   106→│  │ shared-litellm│  │ shared-argocd│  │ shared-secrets│                 │
   107→│  │ (behind Kong) │  │ (GitOps CD)  │  │ (ESO + CSI)   │                 │
   108→│  └───────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └───────────────┘                 │
   109→└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   110→         │                    │                    │                    │
   111→         ▼                    ▼                    ▼                    ▼
   112→┌─────────────┐  ┌───────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐
   113→│  Cloud SQL  │  │  Vertex AI / GCS  │  │  GCP Secret Manager │ │  Cloud Storage  │
   114→│ (PostgreSQL)│  │  Pub/Sub / APIs   │  │                     │ │    (Buckets)    │
   115→└─────────────┘  └───────────────────┘  └─────────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘
   116→```
   117→
   118→**Two separate ingress paths**:
   119→- **API path**: Internet → GCP LB + Cloud Armor → Kong Ingress → `{env}-api-services` / `{env}-mcp-servers`
   120→- **Frontend path**: Internet → GCP LB + Cloud Armor + GKE Gateway API + IAM → `{env}-web-apps` (Nuxt frontends)
   121→
   122→### Repository Model: Repo-Per-Service Across GitHub Enterprise Orgs
   123→
   124→Services are organized across multiple GitHub Enterprise organizations under **Konecta Digital**. A central **gitops repo** in the platform org holds all Helm value overrides and ArgoCD configuration.
   125→
   126→```
   127→GitHub Enterprise: Konecta Digital
   128→│
   129→├── Org: konecta-ix-platform (Platform Engineering)
   130→│   ├── kix-platform-infrastructure     # GCP infra + ArgoCD apps + GitOps values
   131→│   │   ├── argocd/                     # ApplicationSets
   132→│   │   └── environments/               # Per-env Helm value overrides for ALL services
   133→│   │       ├── dev/{service}.yaml
   134→│   │       ├── qa/{service}.yaml
   135→│   │       └── prod/{service}.yaml
   136→│   ├── kix-platform-modules            # Shared Terraform modules + Helm base chart
   137→│   │   ├── terraform/                  # Existing Terraform modules
   138→│   │   └── helm/base-service-chart/    # Shared Helm library chart (published to OCI)
   139→│   ├── kix-platform-ci-workflows       # Shared reusable GitHub Actions workflows (NEW)
   140→│   │   └── .github/workflows/
   141→│   │       ├── standard-pipeline.yaml
   142→│   │       ├── _build-image.yaml
   143→│   │       ├── _scan-and-sign.yaml
   144→│   │       ├── _push-registry.yaml
   145→│   │       ├── _update-gitops.yaml
   146→│   │       └── _promote.yaml
   147→│   ├── kix-platform-service-template   # Template repo for bootstrapping (NEW)
   148→│   └── kix-platform-docs              # Documentation
   149→│
   150→├── Org: konecta-ix-mcp-servers (MCP Integration Team)
   151→│   ├── kix-mcp-gateway                # Each MCP server has its own repo
   152→│   ├── kix-mcp-ragengine
   153→│   ├── kix-mcp-channelhub
   154→│   ├── kix-mcp-google-search
   155→│   └── kix-mcp-*                      # One per server (~14 repos)
   156→│
   157→├── Org: konecta-ix-apps (Apps Team)
   158→│   ├── kix-app-hrfraud
   159→│   ├── kix-app-hrportal
   160→│   └── kix-app-*                      # One per app
   161→│
   162→├── Org: konecta-ix-workloads (Use Case Teams)
   163→│   ├── kix-usecase-iq-portal
   164→│   ├── kix-usecase-testiq
   165→│   └── kix-usecase-*                  # One per use case
   166→│
   167→├── Org: konecta-ix-demos (Demo Team)
   168→│   ├── kix-demo-porfolioguru          # Bot/demo apps
   169→│   └── kix-demo-*
   170→│
   171→└── Org: konecta-ix-services (Platform Services — managed by platform team)
   172→    ├── kix-services-kong
   173→    ├── kix-services-litellm
   174→    ├── kix-services-monitoring
   175→    └── kix-services-backstage
   176→```
   177→
   178→**Key design**: All CI workflows are shared from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows`. All GitOps state (what's deployed where) lives in `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`. Individual service repos across all orgs call the shared pipeline and dispatch deployment updates to the central gitops repo.
   179→
   180→### CI/CD Flow
   181→
   182→```
   183→Developer Push → Service Repo (any org) → Standard Pipeline
   184→  (e.g. konecta-ix-mcp-servers/kix-mcp-gateway)
   185→       │
   186→       ▼  Calls reusable workflows from:
   187→       konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows@v1
   188→       ├── Lint + Unit Tests
   189→       ├── Docker Build (BuildKit, multi-stage)
   190→       ├── Trivy Scan + SBOM (Syft)
   191→       ├── Push to Artifact Registry
   192→       └── Cosign Sign (OIDC)
   193→                │
   194→                ▼  Dispatch to:
   195→       konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure
   196→       Update environments/dev/{service}.yaml with new image tag
   197→                │
   198→                ▼
   199→       ArgoCD watches kix-platform-infrastructure
   200→         ├── Dev: Auto-sync
   201→         ├── QA: Manual sync (08:00-18:00 UTC)
   202→         └── Prod: Manual sync (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
   203→```
   204→
   205→---
   206→
   207→## 4. Service Inventory & Tiering
   208→
   209→**No services are currently in production.** Services will migrate directly to new domains on kix-infrastructure — no dual-stack, no DNS cutover, no downtime concerns.
   210→
   211→### Migration Priority
   212→| Priority | Services | Rationale |
   213→|----------|----------|-----------|
   214→| **P0 — Pilot** | kix-usecase-iq-portal (backend + frontend) | Validates the entire platform: both ingress paths, Cloud SQL sidecar, standard pipeline, cross-org dispatch |
   215→| **P1 — Platform core** | Kong, LiteLLM (behind Kong), MCP Gateway | Must be running before other services can function |
   216→| **P2 — MCP Servers** | Remaining 13 MCP servers | Foundation for AI/business workflows |
   217→| **P3 — Business Apps** | kix-app-hrfraud, kix-app-hrportal, kix-usecase-testiq | Remaining user-facing applications |
   218→| **P4 — Data & Demos** | RAG pipelines, bot, prototypes | Lower priority, migrate last |
   219→
   220→---
   221→
   222→## 5. Migration Strategy
   223→
   224→### Phased Approach
   225→
   226→Since nothing is currently in production, services migrate directly to new domains on kix-infrastructure. No dual-stack or traffic cutover needed.
   227→
   228→**Phase 1 — Platform Foundation + Pilot (kix-usecase-iq-portal)**
   229→- Create `kix-platform-ci-workflows` repo in `konecta-ix-platform` with standard pipeline
   230→- Add base Helm chart to `kix-platform-modules/helm/base-service-chart/`, publish to Artifact Registry
   231→- Configure ArgoCD ApplicationSets in `kix-platform-infrastructure`
   232→- Create `kix-platform-service-template` template repo
   233→- Install GitHub App across all orgs for cross-org dispatch
   234→- Configure GitHub OIDC → GCP Workload Identity Pool (per org)
   235→- Deploy Kong + LiteLLM (behind Kong) on kix-infrastructure
   236→- **Pilot: Deploy kix-usecase-iq-portal (backend + frontend)** as the first service through the full pipeline
   237→  - Backend: validates Kong API ingress, Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar, External Secrets, standard pipeline
   238→  - Frontend: validates GKE Gateway API + IAP ingress path, separate LB
   239→  - Exercises both Helm chart profiles (API Service + Frontend)
   240→  - Confirms cross-org workflow dispatch (konecta-ix-workloads → konecta-ix-platform gitops)
   241→- Fix any issues found in base chart, CI workflows, or ArgoCD config before scaling out
   242→
   243→**Phase 2 — MCP Servers (14 services)**
   244→- Deploy kix-mcp-gateway first (dependency for other MCP servers)
   245→- Migrate remaining 13 MCP servers using the validated standard pipeline
   246→- Each service gets a new domain (e.g., `mcp-gateway.dev.konecta.io`)
   247→- Decommission corresponding Cloud Run services as each migrates
   248→
   249→**Phase 3 — Remaining Business Apps (5 services)**
   250→- Migrate kix-app-hrfraud (backend + frontend, Cloud SQL sidecar)
   251→- Migrate kix-app-hrportal, kix-usecase-testiq, kix-demo-prototypes
   252→- Frontend apps deploy behind GKE Gateway API + IAP on new domains
   253→
   254→**Phase 4 — Data Pipelines & Cleanup (4 services)**
   255→- Migrate RAG pipelines (kix-usecase-rag-ingestion, kix-usecase-rag-googledrive)
   256→- Migrate bot (kix-demo-porfolioguru) to K8s Deployment
   257→- Tear down all Cloud Run services
   258→- Remove Cloud Build configurations
   259→
   260→### Stateless Services
   261→- **All application services are stateless** — state lives in Cloud SQL, Firestore, GCS
   262→- Container images contain no data; configuration via ConfigMaps/Secrets
   263→- No persistent volumes needed for application pods
   264→- Temporary storage (`emptyDir`) for: `/tmp`, ML model caches, video processing scratch space
   265→
   266→---
   267→
   268→## 6. Kubernetes & Helm Chart Design
   269→
   270→### 6.1 Helm Chart Strategy
   271→
   272→**Base Chart Library (platform org) + Per-Service Chart (in each service repo) + GitOps Repo (value overrides)**
   273→
   274→Three-layer separation matching the multi-org, repo-per-service model:
   275→
   276→```
   277→Org: konecta-ix-platform
   278→Repo: kix-platform-modules/helm/base-service-chart/
   279→├── Chart.yaml                       # type: library, version: semver
   280→└── templates/                       # Published to Artifact Registry as OCI chart
   281→    ├── _deployment.tpl
   282→    ├── _service.tpl
   283→    ├── _ingress.tpl
   284→    ├── _hpa.tpl
   285→    ├── _pdb.tpl
   286→    ├── _networkpolicy.tpl
   287→    ├── _serviceaccount.tpl
   288→    └── _external-secret.tpl
   289→
   290→Org: konecta-ix-mcp-servers (or any service org)
   291→Repo: kix-mcp-gateway/              # Each service repo
   292→├── src/                             # Application source code
   293→├── Dockerfile
   294→├── chart/                           # Service-specific Helm chart
   295→│   ├── Chart.yaml                   # depends on base-service-chart (OCI reference)
   296→│   ├── values.yaml                  # Service defaults (port, probes, resources)
   297→│   └── templates/                   # Only if service has custom K8s resources
   298→└── .github/workflows/
   299→    └── ci.yaml                      # Calls reusable workflows from kix-platform-ci-workflows
   300→
   301→Org: konecta-ix-platform
   302→Repo: kix-platform-infrastructure/   # ArgoCD watches this repo only
   303→├── argocd/
   304→│   └── applicationset.yaml          # Matrix generator: env × service
   305→└── environments/
   306→    ├── dev/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml     # Overrides: image.tag, replicas, env-specific config
   307→    ├── qa/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
   308→    └── prod/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
   309→```
   310→
   311→**Flow**: Service repo CI builds image → dispatches to `kix-platform-infrastructure` → updates `environments/dev/{service}.yaml` with new tag → ArgoCD syncs.
   312→
   313→**Base chart versioning**: Published to Artifact Registry as OCI Helm chart. Service charts pin to a specific version (`~0.5.0`). Base chart updates are rolled out by bumping the version in each service's `Chart.yaml` (Renovate automates this across all orgs).
   314→
   315→**Cross-org access**: The shared CI workflows in `konecta-ix-platform` are accessible to all other orgs via GitHub Enterprise's internal visibility setting. A GitHub App installed across all orgs handles cross-repo dispatch to the gitops repo.
   316→
   317→**Rationale**: Repo-per-service with org-level boundaries gives teams autonomy and clear ownership. The platform org owns the standards (base chart, CI workflows, gitops state). Service teams own their code and Helm values.
   318→
   319→### 6.2 Namespace Strategy
   320→
   321→**Environment × Domain Boundaries (15 namespaces total)**
   322→
   323→| Namespace Pattern | Contents |
   324→|-------------------|----------|
   325→| `{env}-api-services` | FastAPI backends, business apps, bots, data pipelines |
   326→| `{env}-mcp-servers` | MCP protocol servers |
   327→| `{env}-web-apps` | Nuxt 3 frontends |
   328→| `shared-kong` | Kong API Gateway (routes to APIs, MCP servers, and LiteLLM) |
   329→| `shared-litellm` | LiteLLM proxy (accessible only via Kong, not directly) |
   330→| `shared-argocd` | ArgoCD (existing) |
   331→| `shared-monitoring` | Prometheus, Grafana, Jaeger |
   332→| `shared-devsecops` | SonarQube, Dependency-Track |
   333→| `shared-cert-manager` | TLS certificate management |
   334→
   335→Each namespace gets: ResourceQuotas, LimitRanges, default NetworkPolicies (deny-all ingress), RBAC bindings.
   336→
   337→### 6.3 Resource Profiles
   338→
   339→| Profile | CPU Request | CPU Limit | Mem Request | Mem Limit | Use For |
   340→|---------|------------|-----------|-------------|-----------|---------|
   341→| **API Service** | 100m | 500m | 256Mi | 512Mi | FastAPI APIs, MCP servers, bots, data pipelines |
   342→| **API Heavy** | 250m | 1000m | 512Mi | 1Gi | RAG ingestion, document processing, services with ML deps |
   343→| **Frontend** | 50m | 200m | 128Mi | 256Mi | Nuxt 3 SSR |
   344→
   345→### 6.4 Health Probes
   346→
   347→| Service Type | Liveness | Readiness | Startup |
   348→|-------------|----------|-----------|---------|
   349→| FastAPI | `GET /healthz` 8000 | `GET /readyz` 8000 (checks DB) | N/A |
   350→| MCP Server (SSE) | `GET /healthz` 8000, period=30s | `GET /readyz` 8000, initial=30s | N/A |
   351→| Nuxt 3 | `GET /` 3000 | `GET /_health` 3000 | N/A |
   352→| Heavy services | `GET /healthz` 8000 | `GET /readyz` 8000 | failureThreshold=18 (3min) |
   353→
   354→### 6.5 Autoscaling (HPA)
   355→
   356→| Tier | Min | Max | Target CPU | Scale-Down Window |
   357→|------|-----|-----|------------|-------------------|
   358→| User-facing (frontends, public APIs) | 2 | 20 | 60% | 5 min |
   359→| Internal APIs, MCP servers | 2 | 10 | 75% | 10 min |
   360→| Data pipelines, bots | 1 | 5 | 80% | 15 min |
   361→
   362→Custom metrics via Prometheus Adapter: request rate, Pub/Sub queue depth, active SSE connections.
   363→
   364→### 6.6 Service Mesh
   365→
   366→**Decision: No (initially).** Kong handles ingress traffic management, auth, and rate limiting. Introducing Istio/Linkerd during migration adds unnecessary risk. Revisit post-migration if mTLS between services or advanced traffic management is required.
   367→
   368→### 6.7 Ingress Architecture (Dual Load Balancer)
   369→
   370→Two separate ingress paths with independent load balancers:
   371→
   372→**Path 1 — API / Backend Ingress (Kong)**
   373→```
   374→Internet → GCP External LB + Cloud Armor (WAF)
   375→         → Kong Ingress Controller (shared-kong)
   376→         → {env}-api-services, {env}-mcp-servers
   377→```
   378→- Kong handles API routing, rate limiting, authentication plugins, CORS
   379→- `konghq.com/plugins` annotations per Ingress resource
   380→- Cloud Armor OWASP top-10 WAF rules on the LB
   381→
   382→**Internal — LiteLLM via Kong (all LLM traffic)**
   383→```
   384→Any service pod → Kong (internal route) → shared-litellm (LiteLLM proxy) → Vertex AI / LLM providers
   385→```
   386→- LiteLLM sits behind Kong as an internal-only Kong route (no external exposure)
   387→- **All services** that need LLM access call Kong's internal endpoint (e.g. `kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/llm/...`), which routes to LiteLLM
   388→- Kong applies rate limiting, auth, and observability plugins on LLM traffic
   389→- Benefits: centralized LLM access control, usage metering, consistent auth, audit trail
   390→- NetworkPolicy on `shared-litellm` namespace: allow ingress **only from Kong**, deny all direct access
   391→
   392→**Path 2 — Frontend Ingress (GKE Gateway API + IAP)**
   393→```
   394→Internet → GCP External LB + Cloud Armor (WAF) + GKE Gateway API
   395→         → IAP (Identity-Aware Proxy) / IAM authentication
   396→         → {env}-web-apps (Nuxt 3 frontends)
   397→```
   398→- **GKE Gateway API** (`gateway.networking.k8s.io`): Native GKE ingress using `Gateway` and `HTTPRoute` resources
   399→- **IAP (Identity-Aware Proxy)**: Google-managed identity layer — authenticates users via Google/OIDC before traffic reaches the pod. Configured via `BackendConfig` with IAP enabled.
   400→- **Cloud Armor**: WAF policies applied at the LB level (same OWASP rules as API path)
   401→- **IAM**: Access controlled by IAM policies on the IAP resource — only authorized users/groups can reach the frontend
   402→- No Kong involvement for frontend traffic
   403→
   404→**Shared Configuration**
   405→- **Internal services**: ClusterIP only, accessed via `service.namespace.svc.cluster.local`
   406→- **TLS**: cert-manager + Let's Encrypt for both paths (separate certificates per domain)
   407→- **DNS per environment**:
   408→  - API: `dev.api.konecta.io`, `qa.api.konecta.io`, `api.konecta.io`
   409→  - Frontend: `dev.app.konecta.io`, `qa.app.konecta.io`, `app.konecta.io`
   410→
   411→### 6.8 ArgoCD ApplicationSet Strategy
   412→
   413→**Git Directory Generator on kix-platform-infrastructure**
   414→
   415→ArgoCD watches `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`. Each file in `environments/{env}/` defines an application.
   416→
   417→- ApplicationSet scans `environments/dev/`, `environments/qa/`, `environments/prod/` directories
   418→- Each `{service}.yaml` file contains the Helm source repo URL (across any org), chart path, and environment values
   419→- ArgoCD renders the Helm chart from the **service repo** using values from **kix-platform-infrastructure**
   420→- **Dev**: `syncPolicy.automated` (auto-sync on commit to kix-platform-infrastructure)
   421→- **QA**: `syncPolicy.automated: false` (manual sync, business-hours window)
   422→- **Prod**: `syncPolicy.automated: false` (manual sync, Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
   423→- ArgoCD Projects for RBAC: `dev-project`, `qa-project`, `prod-project`
   424→- Sync waves: infrastructure resources (wave 0) → application deployments (wave 10)
   425→- New services are auto-discovered when their files are added to `environments/` directories
   426→
   427→### 6.9 Cloud SQL Auth Proxy Sidecar
   428→
   429→Services that connect to Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL) use the **Cloud SQL Auth Proxy** as a sidecar container. This is baked into the base Helm chart as an opt-in pattern.
   430→
   431→**Why sidecar (not shared proxy)**:
   432→- Each pod gets its own authenticated connection to Cloud SQL
   433→- Workload Identity per service account — least-privilege IAM per service
   434→- No shared proxy bottleneck; scales with the application pods
   435→- Google-recommended pattern for GKE
   436→
   437→**How it works**:
   438→```
   439→┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
   440→│  Pod                                     │
   441→│  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐  │
   442→│  │  App          │  │  cloud-sql-proxy │  │
   443→│  │  Container    │──│  sidecar         │──│──▶ Cloud SQL (private IP)
   444→│  │  (FastAPI)    │  │  (port 5432)     │  │
   445→│  └──────────────┘  └──────────────────┘  │
   446→│  localhost:5432                           │
   447→└──────────────────────────────────────────┘
   448→```
   449→
   450→- App connects to `localhost:5432` — no connection string changes needed
   451→- Sidecar handles IAM authentication via Workload Identity (no passwords in env vars)
   452→- Private IP connectivity to Cloud SQL (no public IP exposure)
   453→
   454→**Base chart integration** (opt-in via Helm values):
   455→```yaml
   456→# In service values.yaml — only services needing Cloud SQL enable this
   457→cloudSqlProxy:
   458→  enabled: true
   459→  instanceConnectionName: "project:region:instance"
   460→  port: 5432
   461→  resources:
   462→    requests:
   463→      cpu: 50m
   464→      memory: 64Mi
   465→    limits:
   466→      cpu: 200m
   467→      memory: 128Mi
   468→```
   469→
   470→When `cloudSqlProxy.enabled: true`, the base chart template injects:
   471→- A `cloud-sql-proxy` sidecar container (`gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2`)
   472→- `--private-ip` flag (uses VPC-native private IP, not public)
   473→- `--auto-iam-authn` flag (Workload Identity, no password needed)
   474→- The service account annotation for Workload Identity must have `roles/cloudsql.client` IAM role
   475→
   476→**Services requiring Cloud SQL sidecar**:
   477→| Service | Database | Current State |
   478→|---------|----------|---------------|
   479→| kix-usecase-iq-portal | PostgreSQL (IQ portal data) | Already on Cloud SQL |
   480→| kix-app-hrfraud | PostgreSQL (HR fraud data) | Currently containerized PostgreSQL — migrate to Cloud SQL as part of kix move |
   481→
   482→**Connection string pattern** (app-side):
   483→```
   484→DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://user@localhost:5432/dbname
   485→```
   486→No password in the connection string when using `--auto-iam-authn`. The proxy handles authentication via the pod's Workload Identity.
   487→
   488→### 6.10 Multi-Tier App Pattern (Frontend + Backend in Single Pod)
   489→
   490→Two-tiered apps like kix-app-hrfraud and kix-usecase-iq-portal (Nuxt frontend + FastAPI backend) deploy as a **multi-container pod** rather than separate Deployments. This eliminates the inter-service network hops that made Cloud Run deployments slow and complex.
   491→
   492→**Pod layout**:
   493→```
   494→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   495→│  Pod: kix-app-hrfraud                                            │
   496→│                                                                  │
   497→│  ┌──────────────┐   localhost:8000   ┌──────────────────────┐    │
   498→│  │  frontend     │ ─────────────────▶│  backend              │    │
   499→│  │  (Nuxt SSR)   │                   │  (FastAPI)            │    │
   500→│  │  :3000        │                   │  :8000                │    │
   501→│  └──────────────┘                   └──────────┬───────────┘    │
   502→│                                                 │ localhost:5432 │
   503→│                                      ┌──────────▼───────────┐    │
   504→│                                      │  cloud-sql-proxy     │    │
   505→│                                      │  (sidecar)           │────│──▶ Cloud SQL
   506→│                                      │  :5432               │    │
   507→│                                      └──────────────────────┘    │
   508→└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   509→         ▲                    ▲
   510→         │                    │
   511→    Frontend LB          API LB (Kong)
   512→  (Gateway API + IAP)   (internal routes)
   513→```
   514→
   515→**Why single pod (not separate Deployments)**:
   516→- **`localhost` communication**: Frontend calls backend on `localhost:8000` — zero network hop, no DNS resolution, no Service objects needed for the backend
   517→- **Atomic deploys**: Frontend and backend always deploy together — eliminates API version skew during rolling updates
   518→- **Shared lifecycle**: Same release cycle, same repo, same Helm chart — these are tightly coupled components, not independent microservices
   519→- **Simpler networking**: Only the frontend port (3000) and optionally backend port (8000) need to be exposed via K8s Services; inter-container traffic never touches the cluster network
   520→- **Fewer moving parts**: One Deployment, one HPA, one PodDisruptionBudget, one set of health probes
   521→
   522→**Helm values pattern** (opt-in via base chart):
   523→```yaml
   524→# values.yaml for kix-app-hrfraud
   525→containers:
   526→  backend:
   527→    image:
   528→      repository: eu-docker.pkg.dev/project/kix-services/kix-app-hrfraud-backend
   529→      tag: sha-abc1234
   530→    port: 8000
   531→    resources:
   532→      requests: { cpu: 100m, memory: 256Mi }
   533→      limits: { cpu: 500m, memory: 512Mi }
   534→    probes:
   535→      liveness: { path: /healthz, port: 8000 }
   536→      readiness: { path: /readyz, port: 8000 }
   537→    env:
   538→      DATABASE_URL: "postgresql+asyncpg://user@localhost:5432/dbname"
   539→      LITELLM_ENDPOINT: "http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/llm"
   540→
   541→  frontend:
   542→    image:
   543→      repository: eu-docker.pkg.dev/project/kix-services/kix-app-hrfraud-frontend
   544→      tag: sha-def5678
   545→    port: 3000
   546→    resources:
   547→      requests: { cpu: 50m, memory: 128Mi }
   548→      limits: { cpu: 200m, memory: 256Mi }
   549→    probes:
   550→      liveness: { path: /, port: 3000 }
   551→      readiness: { path: /_health, port: 3000 }
   552→    env:
   553→      API_BASE: "http://localhost:8000"    # ← backend via localhost
   554→
   555→cloudSqlProxy:
   556→  enabled: true
   557→  instanceConnectionName: "project:eu-west1:hrfraud-db"
   558→  port: 5432
   559→
   560→# Two K8s Services expose the pod on different ingress paths
   561→services:
   562→  api:
   563→    port: 8000
   564→    targetPort: 8000
   565→    ingress:
   566→      class: kong                           # ← API LB
   567→      path: /api/hrfraud
   568→  frontend:
   569→    port: 3000
   570→    targetPort: 3000
   571→    ingress:
   572→      class: gateway-api                    # ← Frontend LB (Gateway API + IAP)
   573→      host: hrfraud.app.konecta.io
   574→```
   575→
   576→**Dual ingress on a single pod**: Two K8s Service objects point at the same pod on different ports. The API Service routes through Kong; the frontend Service routes through the GKE Gateway API + IAP. Both LBs reach the same pod, but through different ports and auth models.
   577→
   578→**CI pipeline for multi-tier apps**: The standard pipeline builds **two images** (backend + frontend) from the same repo. Both image tags are updated atomically in the gitops values file:
   579→```
   580→ci.yaml triggers:
   581→  1. Build backend image → sha-abc1234
   582→  2. Build frontend image → sha-def5678
   583→  3. Dispatch to gitops: update both tags in one commit
   584→  4. ArgoCD syncs → pod rolls with both new images
   585→```
   586→
   587→**When NOT to use this pattern**:
   588→- Services that need independent scaling (e.g., API serving 10x more traffic than its frontend)
   589→- Services with very different resource profiles where one container would waste resources
   590→- Loosely coupled services that release on different cadences
   591→- Services where the frontend is a static SPA (no SSR) — serve from CDN instead
   592→
   593→**Apps using this pattern**:
   594→| App | Containers | Ports |
   595→|-----|-----------|-------|
   596→| kix-usecase-iq-portal | Nuxt frontend (3000) + FastAPI backend (8000) + cloud-sql-proxy (5432) | 3 containers |
   597→| kix-app-hrfraud | Nuxt frontend (3000) + FastAPI backend (8000) + cloud-sql-proxy (5432) | 3 containers |
   598→
   599→---
   600→

     1→# IQ Portal — Migration Guide to kix-infrastructure
     2→
     3→**Service**: kon-iq-portal → `kix-usecase-iq-portal`
     4→**Target Org**: `konecta-ix-workloads`
     5→**Phase**: P0 (Pilot)
     6→**Status**: Draft
     7→
     8→---
     9→
    10→## Table of Contents
    11→
    12→1. [Current State Assessment](#1-current-state-assessment)
    13→2. [Target State](#2-target-state)

    16→5. [Step 2 — Application Code Changes](#step-2--application-code-changes)
    17→6. [Step 3 — Dockerfile Changes](#step-3--dockerfile-changes)
    18→7. [Step 4 — Helm Chart](#step-4--helm-chart)
    19→8. [Step 5 — GitHub Actions CI Workflow](#step-5--github-actions-ci-workflow)
    20→9. [Step 6 — Secrets Migration](#step-6--secrets-migration)
    21→10. [Step 7 — GitOps Configuration](#step-7--gitops-configuration)
    22→11. [Step 8 — Database Migration](#step-8--database-migration)
    23→12. [Step 9 — Verification & Smoke Tests](#step-9--verification--smoke-tests)
    24→13. [Step 10 — DNS & Domain Cutover](#step-10--dns--domain-cutover)
    25→14. [Appendix A — Full Environment Variable Mapping](#appendix-a--full-environment-variable-mapping)
    26→15. [Appendix B — Rollback Procedure](#appendix-b--rollback-procedure)
    27→
    28→---
    29→
    30→## 1. Current State Assessment
    31→
    32→### Architecture
    33→
    34→IQ Portal is a **single-container monolith**: an Express.js (TypeScript) backend serves both the API (`/api/*`) and a pre-generated Nuxt 4 SPA (static files from `/dist`). Everything runs on port **8080** in production.
    35→
    36→```
    37→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
    38→│  Cloud Run (kon-iq-portal)          │
    39→│                                     │
    40→│  Express.js :8080                   │
    41→│  ├── /api/*  → API routes           │
    42→│  ├── /docs   → Swagger UI           │
    43→│  └── /*      → Static Nuxt SPA      │
    44→│                                     │
    45→│  Cloud SQL (Unix socket)            │
    46→│  /cloudsql/project:region:instance  │
    47→└─────────────────────────────────────┘
    48→```
    49→
    50→### Current Tech Stack
    51→
    52→| Component | Detail |
    53→|-----------|--------|
    54→| Runtime | Node.js 22 (Alpine) |
    55→| Frontend | Nuxt 4 (SPA, `ssr: false`, pre-generated static) |
    56→| Backend | Express 5 (TypeScript, via `tsx`) |
    57→| Database | PostgreSQL on Cloud SQL (`kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform`) |
    58→| DB Connection | Unix socket via VPC connector (`DB_HOST=/cloudsql/...`) |
    59→| Migrations | Custom SQL/TS runner (33 migrations, runs on startup) |
    60→| Auth | Google OAuth → JWT in HttpOnly cookies |
    61→| Storage | GCS (3 buckets: images, videos, RAG docs) |
    62→| AI | Vertex AI (Gemini) + RAG corpus |
    63→| Async Jobs | Cloud Tasks → kon-rag-ingestion |
    64→| Email | ChannelHub (external Cognito-auth API) |
    65→| CI/CD | Cloud Build from Bitbucket |
    66→| Port | 8080 (production), 3002 (dev) |
    67→
    68→### GCP Service Dependencies
    69→
    70→| Service | Project | Used For |
    71→|---------|---------|----------|
    72→| Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL) | `kd-lab-464110` | Primary database |
    73→| Cloud Storage | `kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev` | Image/video/RAG file storage |
    74→| Vertex AI | `kd-lab-464110` | Gemini LLM + RAG retrieval |
    75→| Cloud Tasks | `kd-lab-464110` | Async RAG document ingestion |
    76→| Secret Manager | `kd-lab-464110` | JWT secret, DB password, RAG token |
    77→
    78→---
    79→
    80→## 2. Target State
    81→
    82→```
    83→┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    84→│  kix-infrastructure GKE Cluster                                   │
    85→│                                                                   │
    86→│  Namespace: dev-api-services (then qa-api-services, prod-...)     │
    87→│                                                                   │
    88→│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐      │
    89→│  │  Pod: kix-usecase-iq-portal                             │      │
    90→│  │                                                         │      │
    91→│  │  ┌──────────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────────┐   │      │
    92→│  │  │  app (Express)   │   │  cloud-sql-proxy sidecar  │   │      │
    93→│  │  │  :8080           │──▶│  :5432 (TCP)              │   │      │
    94→│  │  │  /api/* + SPA    │   │  --private-ip             │   │      │
    95→│  │  └──────────────────┘   └──────────────────────────┘   │      │
    96→│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘      │
    97→│      │                                                            │
    98→│      ▼                                                            │
    99→│  Service: kix-usecase-iq-portal :8080                             │
   100→│      │                         │                                  │
   101→│      ▼                         ▼                                  │
   102→│  Kong Ingress              GKE Gateway API                        │
   103→│  /api/iq-portal/*          iq-portal.app.konecta.io               │
   104→│  (API consumers)           (browser users via IAP)                │
   105→└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   106→```
   107→
   108→### Key Differences from Cloud Run
   109→
   110→| Aspect | Cloud Run | kix-infrastructure |
   111→|--------|-----------|-------------------|
   112→| DB connection | Unix socket via VPC connector | Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (TCP `localhost:5432`) |
   113→| Secrets | Secret Manager direct access | External Secrets Operator → K8s Secrets |
   114→| Scaling | 0-10 instances | HPA 1-5 replicas |
   115→| Ingress | Direct HTTPS | Dual: Kong (API) + Gateway API + IAP (frontend) |
   116→| CI/CD | Cloud Build + Bitbucket | GitHub Actions + ArgoCD |
   117→| Health checks | Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK | K8s liveness/readiness probes |
   118→
   119→---
   120→
   121→## 3. Pre-requisites
   122→
   123→Before starting, ensure:
   124→
   125→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-workloads` GitHub org (write)
   126→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure` GitOps repo (write)
   127→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows` (read)
   128→- [ ] `kix-platform-modules` base Helm chart published to Artifact Registry
   129→- [ ] ArgoCD ApplicationSet configured with git directory generator
   130→- [ ] Shared Kong gateway operational in `shared-kong` namespace
   131→- [ ] GKE Gateway API + IAP configured on frontend LB
   132→- [ ] External Secrets Operator running in `shared-secrets` namespace
   133→- [ ] GCP Workload Identity pool configured for GitHub Actions OIDC
   134→- [ ] Cloud SQL instance accessible from GKE cluster (private IP, same VPC)
   135→- [ ] GCP service account for IQ Portal workload (`kix-iq-portal@<project>.iam.gserviceaccount.com`) with IAM roles:
   136→  - `roles/cloudsql.client`
   137→  - `roles/storage.objectAdmin` (for GCS buckets)
   138→  - `roles/aiplatform.user` (for Vertex AI)
   139→  - `roles/cloudtasks.enqueuer` (for Cloud Tasks)
   140→  - `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor` (for migration 00024)
   141→

   144→## Step 1 — Create the New Repository
   145→
   146→### 1.1 Create repo from template

   149→gh repo create konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal \

   153→
   154→### 1.2 Clone and set up

   160→
   161→### 1.3 Copy application code
   162→

   164→
   165→```
   166→kix-usecase-iq-portal/
   167→├── backend/              # Express.js API (existing)
   168→├── src/                  # Nuxt 4 frontend (existing)
   169→├── public/               # Static assets (existing)
   170→├── scripts/              # Deployment scripts (existing)
   171→├── nuxt.config.ts        # (existing)
   172→├── package.json          # (existing)
   173→├── package-lock.json     # (existing)
   174→├── tsconfig*.json        # (existing)
   175→├── version.txt           # (existing)
   176→├── Dockerfile            # (modified — see Step 3)
   177→├── docker-entrypoint.sh  # (existing, no changes needed)
   178→├── .dockerignore         # (existing or create)
   179→├── chart/                # (new — Helm chart, see Step 4)
   180→│   ├── Chart.yaml
   181→│   └── values.yaml

   189→### 1.4 Remove Cloud Build config

   222→---
   223→
   224→## Step 2 — Application Code Changes
   225→
   226→### 2.1 Database connection — switch from Unix socket to TCP localhost
   227→
   228→The Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar exposes the database on `localhost:5432` (TCP), replacing the Unix socket path used on Cloud Run.
   229→
   230→**No code changes required.** The existing `database.ts` already supports both modes:
   231→
   232→```typescript
   233→// backend/db/database.ts — already handles both
   234→const pool = process.env.DATABASE_URL
   235→    ? new Pool({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL })
   236→    : new Pool({
   237→        host: process.env.DB_HOST || 'localhost',  // was /cloudsql/... on Cloud Run
   238→        port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT || '5432'),
   239→        database: process.env.DB_NAME || 'iqplatform',
   240→        user: process.env.DB_USER || 'konecta',
   241→        password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD || 'konecta'
   242→    });
   243→```
   244→
   245→On kix, the environment variables will be:
   246→```
   247→DB_HOST=localhost        # Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar
   248→DB_PORT=5432
   249→```
   250→
   251→This is the default — so the app will work without any code change.
   252→
   253→### 2.2 Add a root-level health endpoint
   254→
   255→The Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK probes `/health`, but the app only has `/api/health`. Kubernetes probes should hit a lightweight path. Add a simple `/health` alias.
   256→
   257→**File: `backend/routes/index.ts`** — add before the existing `/api/health` block:
   258→
   259→```typescript
   260→// ==================== K8S HEALTH PROBES ====================
   261→// Lightweight endpoint for Kubernetes liveness/readiness probes
   262→// Separate from /api/health which checks DB + GCP connectivity
   263→app.get('/health', (req, res) => {
   264→    res.status(200).json({ status: 'ok' });
   265→});
   266→```
   267→
   268→This keeps the existing `/api/health` (with DB + GCP checks) for detailed monitoring, while `/health` serves as a fast liveness probe.
   269→
   270→### 2.3 Graceful shutdown (already handled)
   271→
   272→The app already uses `dumb-init` for signal handling. No changes needed — Kubernetes sends `SIGTERM` which `dumb-init` forwards correctly.
   273→
   274→### 2.4 MCP Gateway URL update
   275→
   276→The app connects to MCP Gateway at `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` (default `http://localhost:8000`). On kix, this should route through Kong:
   277→
   278→```
   279→MCP_GATEWAY_URL=http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway
   280→```
   281→
   282→This is an environment variable change only (set in Helm values), no code change.
   283→
   284→### 2.5 CORS origins update
   285→
   286→`ALLOWED_ORIGINS` needs to include the new domain. This is an environment variable change only:
   287→
   288→```
   289→ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io,https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io
   290→```
   291→
   292→### 2.6 Verify Secret Manager migration dependency
   293→
   294→Migration `00024_add_rag_pipeline_token.ts` reads from GCP Secret Manager at startup:
   295→
   296→```typescript
   297→// backend/db/migrations/00024_add_rag_pipeline_token.ts
   298→const { SecretManagerServiceClient } = await import('@google-cloud/secret-manager');
   299→```
   300→
   301→This will work on kix as long as the pod's Workload Identity has `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor`. No code change needed, but the IAM binding is a pre-requisite.
   302→
   303→### 2.7 Summary of code changes
   304→
   305→| File | Change | Required |
   306→|------|--------|----------|
   307→| `backend/routes/index.ts` | Add `/health` endpoint for K8s probes | **Yes** |
   308→| `cloudbuild.yaml` | Delete | **Yes** |
   309→| All other source files | No changes | — |
   310→
   311→Everything else is handled by environment variable changes in Helm values.
   312→
   313→---
   314→
   315→## Step 3 — Dockerfile Changes
   316→
   317→The existing Dockerfile is well-structured and mostly suitable for Kubernetes. A few targeted changes:
   318→
   319→### 3.1 Pin the base image digest
   320→
   321→Replace the mutable tag with a pinned digest for supply chain security.
   322→
   323→**Change:**
   324→```dockerfile
   325→FROM node:22-alpine AS dependencies
   326→```
   327→
   328→**To:**
   329→```dockerfile
   330→# Pin to specific digest — update monthly via Renovate
   331→FROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<current-digest> AS dependencies
   332→```
   333→
   334→Apply the same change to all four `FROM node:22-alpine` lines (dependencies, dev-dependencies, builder, runner).
   335→
   336→To get the current digest:
   337→```bash
   338→docker pull node:22-alpine
   339→docker inspect --format='{{index .RepoDigests 0}}' node:22-alpine
   340→```
   341→
   342→### 3.2 Remove the HEALTHCHECK instruction
   343→
   344→Kubernetes uses its own liveness/readiness probes — the Dockerfile `HEALTHCHECK` is ignored by K8s and adds unnecessary metadata.
   345→
   346→**Remove lines 142-157:**
   347→```dockerfile
   348→# DELETE THIS BLOCK — K8s probes replace it
   349→HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=40s --retries=3 \
   350→  CMD node -e "..."
   351→```
   352→
   353→### 3.3 Make the port configurable (optional)
   354→
   355→The port is already configurable via the `PORT` env var (default 8080). No change needed.
   356→
   357→### 3.4 Summary of Dockerfile changes
   358→
   359→| Line(s) | Change | Reason |
   360→|---------|--------|--------|
   361→| All `FROM` lines | Pin digest | Supply chain security |
   362→| 142-157 | Remove `HEALTHCHECK` | K8s probes replace it |
   363→
   364→All other aspects (multi-stage build, non-root user, dumb-init, read-only permissions) are already best practice and should be kept as-is.
   365→
   366→---
   367→
   368→## Step 4 — Helm Chart
   369→
   370→Create a `chart/` directory in the repo root.
   371→
   372→### 4.1 Chart.yaml
   373→
   374→```yaml
   375→# chart/Chart.yaml
   376→apiVersion: v2
   377→name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   378→description: Konecta IQ Portal — AI Knowledge Platform
   379→version: 0.1.0
   380→type: application
   381→
   382→dependencies:
   383→  - name: base-service-chart
   384→    version: "~1.0.0"
   385→    repository: "oci://europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/helm-charts"
   386→```
   387→
   388→### 4.2 values.yaml
   389→
   390→```yaml
   391→# chart/values.yaml
   392→# Inherits from base-service-chart — only service-specific overrides here
   393→
   394→base-service-chart:
   395→  nameOverride: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   396→
   397→  image:
   398→    repository: europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/docker/kix-usecase-iq-portal
   399→    tag: "latest"  # overridden by CI pipeline with sha-<commit>
   400→    pullPolicy: IfNotPresent
   401→
   402→  # Single container serving both API + SPA on :8080
   403→  containers:
   404→    app:
   405→      port: 8080
   406→      env:
   407→        NODE_ENV: "production"
   408→        PORT: "8080"
   409→        # DB via Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (localhost TCP)
   410→        DB_HOST: "localhost"
   411→        DB_PORT: "5432"
   412→        DB_NAME: "iqplatform"
   413→        # Non-secret GCP config
   414→        GCP_PROJECT_ID: "kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev"
   415→        GCP_LOCATION: "europe-west1"
   416→        VERTEX_AI_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   417→        VERTEX_AI_LOCATION: "us-central1"
   418→        VERTEX_AI_MODEL: "gemini-2.5-flash"
   419→        RAG_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   420→        RAG_LOCATION: "europe-west4"
   421→        RAG_CORPUS_ID: "4532873024948404224"
   422→        # MCP Gateway via Kong
   423→        MCP_GATEWAY_URL: "http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway"
   424→        # Cloud Tasks
   425→        CLOUD_TASKS_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   426→        CLOUD_TASKS_QUEUE: "rag-ingestion-poc"
   427→        CLOUD_TASKS_LOCATION: "europe-west1"
   428→        # ChannelHub (non-secret)
   429→        CHANNELHUB_URL: "https://api.konecta.cloud/one-channel-hub/prd"
   430→        CHANNELHUB_LOGIN_URL: "https://ch-prd.auth.eu-west-1.amazoncognito.com"
   431→        # Logging
   432→        LOG_LEVEL: "info"
   433→      # Secrets injected from External Secrets Operator
   434→      envFrom:
   435→        - secretRef:
   436→            name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   437→      resources:
   438→        requests:
   439→          cpu: 250m
   440→          memory: 256Mi
   441→        limits:
   442→          cpu: "1"
   443→          memory: 512Mi
   444→
   445→  # Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar
   446→  cloudSqlProxy:
   447→    enabled: true
   448→    instanceConnectionName: "kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform"
   449→    port: 5432
   450→
   451→  # Service account with Workload Identity
   452→  serviceAccount:
   453→    create: true
   454→    annotations:
   455→      iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: kix-iq-portal@kd-ix-platform.iam.gserviceaccount.com
   456→
   457→  # K8s probes
   458→  probes:
   459→    liveness:
   460→      httpGet:
   461→        path: /health
   462→        port: 8080
   463→      initialDelaySeconds: 30
   464→      periodSeconds: 15
   465→      failureThreshold: 3
   466→    readiness:
   467→      httpGet:
   468→        path: /api/health
   469→        port: 8080
   470→      initialDelaySeconds: 40
   471→      periodSeconds: 10
   472→      failureThreshold: 3
   473→    startup:
   474→      httpGet:
   475→        path: /health
   476→        port: 8080
   477→      initialDelaySeconds: 10
   478→      periodSeconds: 5
   479→      failureThreshold: 12  # 60s max startup (migrations may run)
   480→
   481→  # HPA
   482→  autoscaling:
   483→    enabled: true
   484→    minReplicas: 1
   485→    maxReplicas: 5
   486→    targetCPUUtilization: 70
   487→    targetMemoryUtilization: 80
   488→
   489→  # Dual ingress: Kong for API consumers, Gateway API for browser users
   490→  services:
   491→    api:
   492→      port: 8080
   493→      ingress:
   494→        class: kong
   495→        path: /api/iq-portal
   496→        stripPath: true
   497→        annotations:
   498→          konghq.com/strip-path: "true"
   499→          konghq.com/protocols: "https"
   500→    frontend:
   501→      port: 8080
   502→      ingress:
   503→        class: gateway-api
   504→        host: iq-portal.app.konecta.io
   505→        annotations:
   506→          networking.gke.io/managed-certificates: "iq-portal-cert"
   507→
   508→  # Pod security
   509→  podSecurityContext:
   510→    runAsNonRoot: true
   511→    runAsUser: 1001
   512→    fsGroup: 1001
   513→    seccompProfile:
   514→      type: RuntimeDefault
   515→
   516→  containerSecurityContext:
   517→    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   518→    readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
   519→    capabilities:
   520→      drop: [ALL]
   521→
   522→  # Writable tmp for Node.js
   523→  volumeMounts:
   524→    - name: tmp
   525→      mountPath: /tmp
   526→  volumes:
   527→    - name: tmp
   528→      emptyDir:
   529→        sizeLimit: 100Mi
   530→
   531→  # Network policy
   532→  networkPolicy:
   533→    enabled: true
   534→    ingress:
   535→      - from:
   536→          - namespaceSelector:
   537→              matchLabels:
   538→                name: shared-kong
   539→          - namespaceSelector:
   540→              matchLabels:
   541→                name: shared-gateway
   542→        ports:
   543→          - port: 8080
   544→            protocol: TCP
   545→```
   546→
   547→---
   548→
   549→## Step 5 — GitHub Actions CI Workflow
   550→
   551→### 5.1 Standard pipeline call
   552→
   553→Create `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`:
   554→
   555→```yaml
   556→# .github/workflows/ci.yaml
   557→name: CI Pipeline
   558→
   559→on:
   560→  push:
   561→    branches: [main, develop, staging]
   562→  pull_request:
   563→    branches: [main]
   564→
   565→permissions:
   566→  contents: read
   567→  id-token: write    # OIDC for GCP Workload Identity
   568→  security-events: write  # Upload SARIF
   569→
   570→jobs:
   571→  pipeline:
   572→    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1
   573→    with:
   574→      service-name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   575→      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile
   576→      chart-path: ./chart
   577→      node-version: "22"
   578→      build-args: |
   579→        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}
   580→        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}
   581→        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}
   582→      # IQ Portal has no test suite yet — skip test stage
   583→      skip-tests: true
   584→    secrets: inherit
   585→```
   586→
   587→### 5.2 Required GitHub repo settings
   588→
   589→Configure in the repo settings:
   590→
   591→**Variables** (Settings → Secrets and Variables → Actions → Variables):
   592→| Variable | Dev | QA | Prod |
   593→|----------|-----|-----|------|
   594→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | `805102662749-...` | `805102662749-...` | `805102662749-...` |
   595→| `ENVIRONMENT` | `development` | `staging` | `production` |
   596→
   597→**Secrets** (inherited from org — no per-repo secrets needed if using shared OIDC):
   598→- `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` — org-level
   599→- `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` — org-level
   600→
   601→### 5.3 Branch → environment mapping
   602→
   603→| Branch | Environment | Namespace |
   604→|--------|-------------|-----------|
   605→| `develop` | dev | `dev-api-services` |
   606→| `staging` | qa | `qa-api-services` |
   607→| `main` | prod | `prod-api-services` |
   608→
   609→---
   610→
   611→## Step 6 — Secrets Migration
   612→
   613→### 6.1 Identify secrets
   614→
   615→Current secrets sourced from Secret Manager on Cloud Run:
   616→
   617→| Secret | GCP Secret Name | K8s Secret Key |
   618→|--------|----------------|----------------|
   619→| Database password | `db-password` | `DB_PASSWORD` |
   620→| JWT signing key | `koniq-jwt-secret` | `JWT_SECRET` |
   621→| Google OAuth client ID | `koniq-google-client-id` | `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` |
   622→| RAG pipeline token | `kon-iq-rag-client-secret` | `RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` |
   623→| ChannelHub client ID | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` |
   624→| ChannelHub client secret | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` |
   625→| ChannelHub connection ID | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID` |
   626→
   627→### 6.2 Create GCP Secret Manager entries
   628→
   629→If secrets don't already exist in the kix project's Secret Manager, create them:
   630→
   631→```bash
   632→PROJECT=kd-ix-platform  # or whichever project ESO reads from
   633→
   634→# For each secret:
   635→echo -n "value" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-db-password \
   636→  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-
   637→
   638→echo -n "value" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret \
   639→  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-
   640→
   641→# ... repeat for each secret
   642→```
   643→
   644→### 6.3 ExternalSecret CRD
   645→
   646→Create `chart/templates/external-secret.yaml` (or include in base chart if templated):
   647→
   648→```yaml
   649→apiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1
   650→kind: ExternalSecret
   651→metadata:
   652→  name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   653→spec:
   654→  refreshInterval: 1h
   655→  secretStoreRef:
   656→    name: gcp-secret-store  # Shared SecretStore in namespace
   657→    kind: SecretStore
   658→  target:
   659→    name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   660→    creationPolicy: Owner
   661→  data:
   662→    - secretKey: DB_PASSWORD
   663→      remoteRef:
   664→        key: kix-iq-portal-db-password
   665→    - secretKey: DB_USER
   666→      remoteRef:
   667→        key: kix-iq-portal-db-user
   668→    - secretKey: JWT_SECRET
   669→      remoteRef:
   670→        key: kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret
   671→    - secretKey: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID
   672→      remoteRef:
   673→        key: kix-iq-portal-google-client-id
   674→    - secretKey: RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN
   675→      remoteRef:
   676→        key: kix-iq-portal-rag-pipeline-token
   677→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID
   678→      remoteRef:
   679→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-id
   680→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET
   681→      remoteRef:
   682→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-secret
   683→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID
   684→      remoteRef:
   685→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-connection-id
   686→    - secretKey: GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET
   687→      remoteRef:
   688→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-image-bucket
   689→    - secretKey: GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET
   690→      remoteRef:
   691→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-video-bucket
   692→    - secretKey: GCS_RAG_BUCKET
   693→      remoteRef:
   694→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-rag-bucket
   695→```
   696→
   697→---
   698→
   699→## Step 7 — GitOps Configuration
   700→
   701→### 7.1 Add environment values to kix-platform-infrastructure
   702→
   703→In `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`, create:
   704→
   705→```
   706→environments/
   707→├── dev/
   708→│   └── api-services/
   709→│       └── kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml
   710→├── qa/
   711→│   └── api-services/
   712→│       └── kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml
   713→└── prod/
   714→    └── api-services/
   715→        └── kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml
   716→```
   717→
   718→### 7.2 Dev environment values
   719→
   720→```yaml
   721→# environments/dev/api-services/kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml
   722→base-service-chart:
   723→  image:
   724→    tag: "sha-<initial-commit>"  # updated by CI pipeline
   725→
   726→  containers:
   727→    app:
   728→      env:
   729→        NODE_ENV: "development"
   730→        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT: "development"
   731→        ALLOWED_ORIGINS: "https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io"
   732→        CLOUD_TASKS_HANDLER_URL: "https://rag-ingestion.dev.konecta.io/poc"
   733→        LOG_LEVEL: "debug"
   734→
   735→  autoscaling:
   736→    minReplicas: 1
   737→    maxReplicas: 2
   738→
   739→  services:
   740→    frontend:
   741→      ingress:
   742→        host: iq-portal.dev.konecta.io
   743→```
   744→
   745→### 7.3 Prod environment values
   746→
   747→```yaml
   748→# environments/prod/api-services/kix-usecase-iq-portal.yaml
   749→base-service-chart:
   750→  image:
   751→    tag: "sha-<promoted-commit>"  # updated by promotion workflow
   752→
   753→  containers:
   754→    app:
   755→      env:
   756→        NODE_ENV: "production"
   757→        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT: "production"
   758→        ALLOWED_ORIGINS: "https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io"
   759→        CLOUD_TASKS_HANDLER_URL: "https://rag-ingestion.app.konecta.io/poc"
   760→        LOG_LEVEL: "info"
   761→      resources:
   762→        requests:
   763→          cpu: 500m
   764→          memory: 512Mi
   765→        limits:
   766→          cpu: "2"
   767→          memory: 1Gi
   768→
   769→  autoscaling:
   770→    minReplicas: 2
   771→    maxReplicas: 5
   772→
   773→  services:
   774→    frontend:
   775→      ingress:
   776→        host: iq-portal.app.konecta.io
   777→```
   778→
   779→### 7.4 ArgoCD auto-discovers
   780→
   781→No ArgoCD Application manifest needed — the ApplicationSet with git directory generator will automatically detect the new file and create the ArgoCD Application.
   782→
   783→---
   784→
   785→## Step 8 — Database Migration
   786→
   787→### 8.1 Strategy
   788→
   789→The app runs migrations automatically on startup (`runMigrations()` in `backend/index.ts`). This is safe for the initial deployment because:
   790→
   791→- The Cloud SQL instance already exists with the schema from Cloud Run
   792→- Migrations are idempotent (tracked in `schema_migrations` table)
   793→- No schema changes are part of this migration — same database, same schema
   794→
   795→### 8.2 If using a new database
   796→
   797→If creating a fresh Cloud SQL database for kix:
   798→
   799→```bash
   800→# Connect to Cloud SQL
   801→gcloud sql connect konecta-iqplatform --project=kd-lab-464110 --user=konecta
   802→
   803→# Create database
   804→CREATE DATABASE iqplatform;
   805→```
   806→
   807→Then deploy the app — the 33 migrations will run automatically on first startup, creating the full schema and seed data.
   808→
   809→### 8.3 If sharing the existing database
   810→
   811→If pointing kix at the same Cloud SQL instance used by Cloud Run:
   812→
   813→1. Verify the `schema_migrations` table is up to date:
   814→   ```bash
   815→   # From a machine with Cloud SQL access
   816→   psql -h <cloud-sql-ip> -U konecta -d iqplatform \
   817→     -c "SELECT * FROM schema_migrations ORDER BY id"
   818→   ```
   819→2. Deploy to kix — migrations will detect all 33 have already run and skip them
   820→3. Once verified, decommission the Cloud Run service
   821→
   822→### 8.4 Cloud SQL Auth Proxy connectivity test
   823→
   824→Before deploying, verify the sidecar can reach Cloud SQL:
   825→
   826→```bash
   827→# Deploy a test pod with cloud-sql-proxy
   828→kubectl run sql-test --rm -it \
   829→  --image=gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.14.3 \
   830→  --namespace=dev-api-services \
   831→  --serviceaccount=kix-usecase-iq-portal \
   832→  -- --private-ip kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform
   833→```
   834→
   835→In another terminal:
   836→```bash
   837→kubectl exec -it sql-test -n dev-api-services -- \
   838→  psql -h localhost -U konecta -d iqplatform -c "SELECT 1"
   839→```
   840→
   841→---
   842→
   843→## Step 9 — Verification & Smoke Tests
   844→
   845→### 9.1 Deployment checklist
   846→
   847→After the first CI pipeline runs and ArgoCD syncs:
   848→
   849→```bash
   850→NAMESPACE=dev-api-services
   851→
   852→# 1. Pod is running (2/2 = app + cloud-sql-proxy)
   853→kubectl get pods -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal
   854→
   855→# 2. Both containers healthy
   856→kubectl describe pod -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal | grep -A5 "Conditions"
   857→
   858→# 3. Liveness probe passing
   859→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app | grep "running on"
   860→
   861→# 4. Cloud SQL proxy connected
   862→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c cloud-sql-proxy | grep "ready"
   863→
   864→# 5. Migrations ran
   865→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app | grep "migration"
   866→
   867→# 6. Service exists
   868→kubectl get svc -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal
   869→
   870→# 7. Kong route active
   871→kubectl get ingress -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal-api
   872→
   873→# 8. Gateway route active
   874→kubectl get httproute -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal-frontend
   875→```
   876→
   877→### 9.2 Functional smoke tests
   878→
   879→```bash
   880→BASE_URL="https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io"
   881→
   882→# Health check (lightweight)
   883→curl -s $BASE_URL/health | jq .
   884→# Expected: {"status":"ok"}
   885→
   886→# Health check (full — DB + GCP)
   887→curl -s $BASE_URL/api/health | jq .
   888→# Expected: {"status":"ok","checks":{"database":"ok","gcp":"ok"}}
   889→
   890→# Version endpoint
   891→curl -s $BASE_URL/api/version | jq .
   892→# Expected: {"version":"0.9.1","commit":"<sha>"}
   893→
   894→# Frontend loads (SPA index.html)
   895→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/
   896→# Expected: 200
   897→
   898→# Swagger docs
   899→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/docs
   900→# Expected: 200 (or 301 redirect)
   901→
   902→# API auth (should return 401 without token)
   903→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/api/agents
   904→# Expected: 401
   905→```
   906→
   907→### 9.3 Kong API route test
   908→
   909→```bash
   910→KONG_URL="https://api.konecta.io"
   911→
   912→# Via Kong (API consumers path)
   913→curl -s $KONG_URL/api/iq-portal/health | jq .
   914→# Expected: {"status":"ok"}
   915→```
   916→
   917→---
   918→
   919→## Step 10 — DNS & Domain Cutover
   920→
   921→Since nothing is production and services will migrate to new domains, this is straightforward.
   922→
   923→### 10.1 New domains
   924→
   925→| Path | Domain | Managed By |
   926→|------|--------|-----------|
   927→| Frontend (browser) | `iq-portal.app.konecta.io` | GKE Gateway API + IAP |
   928→| Frontend (dev) | `iq-portal.dev.konecta.io` | GKE Gateway API |
   929→| API (machine) | `api.konecta.io/api/iq-portal` | Kong |
   930→
   931→### 10.2 DNS records
   932→
   933→```
   934→iq-portal.app.konecta.io  → A record → Frontend LB external IP
   935→iq-portal.dev.konecta.io  → A record → Frontend LB external IP (dev)
   936→api.konecta.io            → A record → Kong LB external IP (shared)
   937→```
   938→
   939→### 10.3 Decommission Cloud Run
   940→
   941→Once kix deployment is verified:
   942→
   943→```bash
   944→# Stop Cloud Run service (no traffic)
   945→gcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \
   946→  --region=europe-west1 \
   947→  --max-instances=0
   948→
   949→# After confidence period (1-2 weeks), delete
   950→gcloud run services delete kon-iq-portal \
   951→  --region=europe-west1
   952→```
   953→
   954→---
   955→
   956→## Appendix A — Full Environment Variable Mapping
   957→
   958→Complete mapping of every environment variable from Cloud Run to kix Helm values:
   959→
   960→| Variable | Source on Cloud Run | Source on kix | Value / Notes |
   961→|----------|-------------------|--------------|---------------|
   962→| `NODE_ENV` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `production` |
   963→| `PORT` | Default (8080) | Helm `env` | `8080` |
   964→| `ENVIRONMENT` | Cloud Build substitution | Helm `env` | `production` / `development` |
   965→| `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` | Terraform | Helm `env` | New domain(s) |
   966→| `DB_HOST` | Terraform (`/cloudsql/...`) | Helm `env` | `localhost` (sidecar) |
   967→| `DB_PORT` | Default (5432) | Helm `env` | `5432` |
   968→| `DB_NAME` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `iqplatform` |
   969→| `DB_USER` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | `konecta` |
   970→| `DB_PASSWORD` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
   971→| `DB_POOL_MIN` | Default (2) | Helm `env` | `2` |
   972→| `DB_POOL_MAX` | Default (10) | Helm `env` | `10` |
   973→| `JWT_SECRET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
   974→| `JWT_COOKIE_MAX_AGE_DAYS` | Default (7) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `7` |
   975→| `JWT_EXPIRY` | Default (7d) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `7d` |
   976→| `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
   977→| `DEFAULT_ALLOWED_DOMAIN` | Default | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `konecta.com` |
   978→| `GCP_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev` |
   979→| `GCP_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west4` |
   980→| `VERTEX_AI_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |
   981→| `VERTEX_AI_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `us-central1` |
   982→| `VERTEX_AI_MODEL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `gemini-2.5-flash` |
   983→| `RAG_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |
   984→| `RAG_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west4` |
   985→| `RAG_CORPUS_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `4532873024948404224` |
   986→| `RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
   987→| `RAG_SECRET_NAME` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kon-iq-rag-client-secret` |
   988→| `GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |
   989→| `GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |
   990→| `GCS_RAG_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |
   991→| `MAX_FILE_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB` | Default (100) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `100` |
   992→| `CLOUD_TASKS_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |
   993→| `CLOUD_TASKS_QUEUE` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `rag-ingestion-poc` |
   994→| `CLOUD_TASKS_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west1` |
   995→| `CLOUD_TASKS_HANDLER_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` (per-env) | New kix ingestion URL |
   996→| `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` | Default | Helm `env` | `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway` |
   997→| `CHANNELHUB_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `https://api.konecta.cloud/one-channel-hub/prd` |
   998→| `CHANNELHUB_LOGIN_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `https://ch-prd.auth.eu-west-1.amazoncognito.com` |
   999→| `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1000→| `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1001→| `CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1002→| `LOG_LEVEL` | Default (info) | Helm `env` | `info` (prod) / `debug` (dev) |
  1003→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | Cloud Build arg | CI build arg | `805102662749-...` |
  1004→| `NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT` | Cloud Build arg | CI build arg | `production` / `development` |
  1005→
  1006→**Build-time only** (not needed at runtime):
  1007→| Variable | Source |
  1008→|----------|--------|
  1009→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | GitHub Actions variable → `--build-arg` |
  1010→| `NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT` | GitHub Actions variable → `--build-arg` |
  1011→| `COMMIT_SHA` | `${{ github.sha }}` → `--build-arg` |
  1012→
  1013→---
  1014→
  1015→## Appendix B — Rollback Procedure
  1016→
  1017→### Automatic (ArgoCD)
  1018→
  1019→ArgoCD self-heals. To roll back:
  1020→
  1021→```bash
  1022→# Revert the image tag in the gitops repo
  1023→cd kix-platform-infrastructure
  1024→git revert HEAD
  1025→git push
  1026→# ArgoCD syncs the previous image tag automatically
  1027→```
  1028→
  1029→### Manual (kubectl)
  1030→
  1031→```bash
  1032→# Roll back to previous ReplicaSet
  1033→kubectl rollout undo deployment/kix-usecase-iq-portal -n dev-api-services
  1034→
  1035→# Verify
  1036→kubectl rollout status deployment/kix-usecase-iq-portal -n dev-api-services
  1037→```
  1038→
  1039→### Fall back to Cloud Run
  1040→
  1041→If the entire kix deployment is problematic:
  1042→
  1043→```bash
  1044→# Re-enable Cloud Run
  1045→gcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \
  1046→  --region=europe-west1 \
  1047→  --max-instances=10
  1048→
  1049→# Point DNS back (if changed)
  1050→# Update iq-portal.app.konecta.io → Cloud Run URL
  1051→```
  1052→
  1053→Since nothing is production and Cloud Run will remain available during migration, this is low risk.
  1054→

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-tic/KIX_MIGRATION_BLUEPRINT.md",
  "old_string": "### 8.9 Onboarding a New Service\n\nAdding a new service to the platform:\n\n1. **Create repo** in the appropriate org from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template`:\n   - Standard `Dockerfile` (Python or Node.js variant)\n   - Standard `chart/` directory with `Chart.yaml` depending on base chart\n   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling shared pipeline\n   - Standard `.dockerignore`, `CODEOWNERS`\n2. **Add to gitops repo**: Create `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/{service-name}.yaml` in `kix-platform-infrastructure`\n3. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** via ApplicationSet git directory generator\n4. **First push** triggers the standard pipeline — fully operational\n\nNo pipeline code to write. No Helm templates to create. Just fill in `values.yaml` with service-specific config.",
  "new_string": "### 8.9 Onboarding a New Service\n\nAdding a new service to the platform:\n\n**Option A — Via Backstage Software Template (recommended)**\n\n1. **Open Backstage** at `https://backstage.app.konecta.io`\n2. **Create Component** → Select the appropriate template (Python API, Node.js API, Nuxt Frontend, MCP Server)\n3. **Fill in the form**: service name, owning team, GitHub org, Cloud SQL requirement, ingress type\n4. **Backstage scaffolds the repo** in the correct org with:\n   - Standard `Dockerfile` (Python or Node.js variant)\n   - Standard `chart/` directory with `Chart.yaml` depending on base chart\n   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling shared pipeline\n   - Standard `.dockerignore`, `CODEOWNERS`\n   - Pre-filled `catalog-info.yaml` with metadata, owner, and dependency annotations\n   - `docs/index.md` stub for TechDocs\n5. **Backstage creates the gitops entries**: `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/{service-name}.yaml` in `kix-platform-infrastructure`\n6. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** via ApplicationSet git directory generator\n7. **First push** triggers the standard pipeline — fully operational\n8. **Service appears in Backstage catalog** with CI, deployment, and docs linked\n\n**Option B — Manual (fallback)**\n\n1. **Create repo** in the appropriate org from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template`:\n   - Standard `Dockerfile` (Python or Node.js variant)\n   - Standard `chart/` directory with `Chart.yaml` depending on base chart\n   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling shared pipeline\n   - Standard `.dockerignore`, `CODEOWNERS`\n   - `catalog-info.yaml` (see section 8.10)\n2. **Add to gitops repo**: Create `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/{service-name}.yaml` in `kix-platform-infrastructure`\n3. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** via ApplicationSet git directory generator\n4. **Backstage auto-discovers** the `catalog-info.yaml` from the repo\n5. **First push** triggers the standard pipeline — fully operational\n\nNo pipeline code to write. No Helm templates to create. Just fill in `values.yaml` with service-specific config.\n\n### 8.10 Backstage Integration\n\n**Backstage** (`shared-backstage` namespace) serves as the internal developer portal for the kix platform, providing a single pane of glass across all 24+ services spread across 6 GitHub orgs.\n\n**Core capabilities:**\n\n| Capability | Backstage Feature | Integration |\n|------------|------------------|-------------|\n| Service catalog | Software Catalog | Auto-discovers `catalog-info.yaml` from all GitHub orgs |\n| New service creation | Software Templates (Scaffolder) | Pre-built templates for Python/Node.js/MCP services |\n| Documentation | TechDocs | Renders `docs/` markdown from each repo into unified portal |\n| CI/CD visibility | GitHub Actions plugin | Shows pipeline status, build history per service |\n| Deployment status | ArgoCD plugin | Shows sync status, deployed image tag, health per environment |\n| Live K8s status | Kubernetes plugin | Shows pod count, status, resource usage, logs |\n| Security posture | SonarQube + Dependency-Track plugins | Shows code quality, vulnerability counts per service |\n| API documentation | API docs plugin | Renders OpenAPI/Swagger specs from service repos |\n| Ownership | Catalog model | Maps services → teams → GitHub orgs for clear ownership |\n\n**catalog-info.yaml (required in every service repo):**\n\n```yaml\n# catalog-info.yaml\napiVersion: backstage.io/v1alpha1\nkind: Component\nmetadata:\n  name: kix-mcp-gateway\n  description: MCP Gateway — routes MCP protocol requests to backend servers\n  annotations:\n    # GitHub\n    github.com/project-slug: konecta-ix-mcp-servers/kix-mcp-gateway\n    # ArgoCD\n    argocd/app-name: kix-mcp-gateway\n    # Kubernetes\n    backstage.io/kubernetes-id: kix-mcp-gateway\n    backstage.io/kubernetes-namespace: dev-mcp-servers,qa-mcp-servers,prod-mcp-servers\n    # TechDocs\n    backstage.io/techdocs-ref: dir:.\n    # SonarQube\n    sonarqube.org/project-key: kix-mcp-gateway\n  tags:\n    - python\n    - mcp\n    - fastmcp\n  links:\n    - url: https://api.konecta.io/api/mcp-gateway/health\n      title: Health Check\n      icon: dashboard\nspec:\n  type: service\n  lifecycle: production\n  owner: group:mcp-integration-team\n  system: kix-platform\n  providesApis:\n    - mcp-gateway-api\n  dependsOn:\n    - component:kix-services-kong\n    - component:kix-services-litellm\n```\n\n**Software Templates (Scaffolder):**\n\nBackstage hosts 4 Software Templates in `kix-platform-backstage/templates/`:\n\n| Template | Creates | Includes |\n|----------|---------|----------|\n| `python-api` | Python FastAPI service repo | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |\n| `nodejs-api` | Node.js Express service repo | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |\n| `mcp-server` | Python FastMCP server repo | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |\n| `nuxt-frontend` | Nuxt 3 frontend repo | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |\n\nEach template prompts for: service name, owning team, GitHub org, Cloud SQL (yes/no), ingress type (Kong/Gateway API/both), and generates all boilerplate including the gitops entries.\n\n**Catalog discovery:**\n\nBackstage discovers services via GitHub org-wide discovery configured in `app-config.yaml`:\n\n```yaml\n# Backstage app-config.yaml (in kix-platform-backstage)\ncatalog:\n  providers:\n    github:\n      konecta-ix-platform:\n        organization: konecta-ix-platform\n        catalogPath: /catalog-info.yaml\n        schedule:\n          frequency: { minutes: 30 }\n          timeout: { minutes: 3 }\n      konecta-ix-mcp-servers:\n        organization: konecta-ix-mcp-servers\n        catalogPath: /catalog-info.yaml\n        schedule:\n          frequency: { minutes: 30 }\n          timeout: { minutes: 3 }\n      konecta-ix-apps:\n        organization: konecta-ix-apps\n        catalogPath: /catalog-info.yaml\n        schedule:\n          frequency: { minutes: 30 }\n          timeout: { minutes: 3 }\n      konecta-ix-workloads:\n        organization: konecta-ix-workloads\n        catalogPath: /catalog-info.yaml\n        schedule:\n          frequency: { minutes: 30 }\n          timeout: { minutes: 3 }\n      konecta-ix-demos:\n        organization: konecta-ix-demos\n        catalogPath: /catalog-info.yaml\n        schedule:\n          frequency: { minutes: 30 }\n          timeout: { minutes: 3 }\n```\n\n**Backstage deployment:**\n\n- Runs in `shared-backstage` namespace\n- Deployed via Helm chart from `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-backstage`\n- Uses Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL) for catalog storage\n- Authenticates via Google OAuth (same identity as IAP)\n- Exposed on `backstage.app.konecta.io` via Gateway API + IAP (same frontend LB as other apps)"
}
```
