```json
  "new_string": "### 1.7 Add catalog-info.yaml (Backstage)\n\nCreate `catalog-info.yaml` in the repo root so Backstage discovers and indexes the service:\n\n```yaml\n# catalog-info.yaml\napiVersion: backstage.io/v1alpha1\nkind: Component\nmetadata:\n  name: kix-usecase-iq-portal\n  description: Konecta IQ Portal — AI-powered knowledge platform with agent catalog, RAG, and use case management\n  annotations:\n    github.com/project-slug: konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal\n    argocd/app-name: kix-usecase-iq-portal\n    backstage.io/kubernetes-id: kix-usecase-iq-portal\n    backstage.io/kubernetes-namespace: dev-api-services,qa-api-services,prod-api-services\n    backstage.io/techdocs-ref: dir:.\n    sonarqube.org/project-key: kix-usecase-iq-portal\n  tags:\n    - nodejs\n    - express\n    - nuxt\n    - typescript\n    - cloud-sql\n  links:\n    - url: https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io\n      title: Production\n      icon: dashboard\n    - url: https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io\n      title: Dev\n      icon: dashboard\n    - url: https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io/docs\n      title: Swagger API Docs\n      icon: docs\nspec:\n  type: service\n  lifecycle: production\n  owner: group:iq-portal-team\n  system: kix-platform\n  dependsOn:\n    - resource:cloud-sql-iqplatform\n    - component:kix-services-kong\n    - component:kix-mcp-gateway\n    - resource:gcs-portal-images\n    - resource:gcs-portal-videos\n    - resource:gcs-portal-rag\n    - resource:vertex-ai-rag-corpus\n```\n\n### 1.8 Add TechDocs stub\n\nCreate `docs/index.md` with a basic service overview. Backstage TechDocs renders this into the developer portal.\n\n```markdown\n# IQ Portal\n\nAI-powered knowledge platform for Konecta.\n\n## Architecture\n\nSingle-container Express.js monolith serving a Nuxt 4 SPA and REST API.\n\n## API\n\nSwagger documentation available at `/docs` on the running service.\n\n## Runbook\n\n- Health check: `GET /health` (liveness), `GET /api/health` (readiness with DB + GCP checks)\n- Logs: `kubectl logs -n {env}-api-services -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app`\n- Database: Cloud SQL `kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform`\n```\n\nAlso add `mkdocs.yml` in the repo root (required by TechDocs):\n\n```yaml\n# mkdocs.yml\nsite_name: IQ Portal\nnav:\n  - Home: index.md\nplugins:\n  - techdocs-core\n```\n\n---\n\n## Step 2 — Application Code Changes"
```

     1→# CLAUDE.md — AI Assistant Guidelines for kix-infrastructure Migration
     2→
     3→This file provides context for AI assistants working on migrating Konecta TIC services from GCP Cloud Run to the kix-infrastructure Kubernetes platform.
     4→
     5→## Reference Documents
     6→
     7→| Document | Path | Purpose |
     8→|----------|------|---------|
     9→| Architecture Blueprint | `KIX_MIGRATION_BLUEPRINT.md` | Target architecture, Helm design, CI/CD pipeline, security, namespaces, service inventory |
    10→| IQ Portal Migration Guide | `IQ_PORTAL_MIGRATION_GUIDE.md` | Pilot app migration — use as the template for all subsequent migration guides |
    11→
    12→**Read both documents before producing any migration guide or making architectural decisions.** They are the source of truth for all conventions described below.
    13→
    14→---
    15→
    16→## Platform Overview
    17→
    18→~24 microservices migrating from GCP Cloud Run to GKE (kix-infrastructure). Repo-per-service model across 6 GitHub Enterprise orgs. Shared CI workflows, a single GitOps repo, and a base Helm library chart.
    19→
    20→### GitHub Enterprise Org Structure
    21→
    22→| Org | Purpose | Repo Prefix |
    23→|-----|---------|-------------|
    24→| `konecta-ix-platform` | Platform engineering: GitOps, CI workflows, Helm base chart, templates, docs | `kix-platform-*` |
    25→| `konecta-ix-mcp-servers` | MCP protocol servers | `kix-mcp-*` |
    26→| `konecta-ix-apps` | Business applications | `kix-app-*` |
    27→| `konecta-ix-workloads` | Use case workloads | `kix-usecase-*` |
    28→| `konecta-ix-demos` | Demos and prototypes | `kix-demo-*` |
    29→| `konecta-ix-services` | Shared platform services (Kong, LiteLLM, monitoring) | `kix-services-*` |
    30→
    31→### Key Platform Repos
    32→
    33→| Repo | Org | Role |
    34→|------|-----|------|
    35→| `kix-platform-infrastructure` | `konecta-ix-platform` | GitOps repo — ArgoCD watches this. Helm values per env per service. |
    36→| `kix-platform-ci-workflows` | `konecta-ix-platform` | 11 reusable GitHub Actions workflows (standard pipeline) |
    37→| `kix-platform-modules` | `konecta-ix-platform` | Base Helm library chart + Terraform modules |
    38→| `kix-platform-service-template` | `konecta-ix-platform` | Template repo for bootstrapping new services |
    39→
    40→---
    41→
    42→## Naming Conventions
    43→
    44→### Repo Names
    45→
    46→Map from current `kon-` prefix to `kix-` prefix:
    47→
    48→| Current Pattern | New Pattern | Example |
    49→|----------------|-------------|---------|
    50→| `kon-mcp-{name}` | `kix-mcp-{name}` | `kon-mcp-gateway` → `kix-mcp-gateway` |
    51→| `kon-app-{name}` | `kix-app-{name}` | `kon-app-hrfraud` → `kix-app-hrfraud` |
    52→| `kon-iq-portal` | `kix-usecase-iq-portal` | Workload, goes to `konecta-ix-workloads` |
    53→| `kon-testiq` | `kix-usecase-testiq` | Workload |
    54→| `kon-bot-{name}` | `kix-demo-{name}` | `kon-bot-porfolioguru` → `kix-demo-porfolioguru` |
    55→| `kon-flow-{name}` | Out of scope | CrewAI flows excluded |
    56→| `kon-crewai-{name}` | Out of scope | CrewAI tools excluded |
    57→
    58→### Domain Names
    59→
    60→| Path | Pattern |
    61→|------|---------|
    62→| API (via Kong) | `api.konecta.io/api/{service-name}` |
    63→| Frontend (via Gateway API + IAP) | `{service-name}.app.konecta.io` |
    64→| Frontend (dev) | `{service-name}.dev.konecta.io` |
    65→
    66→### Namespace Mapping
    67→
    68→Services go into `{env}-{domain}` namespaces:
    69→
    70→| Domain | Namespace | Services |
    71→|--------|-----------|----------|
    72→| `api-services` | `{env}-api-services` | FastAPI backends, Express.js backends, bots, data pipelines |
    73→| `mcp-servers` | `{env}-mcp-servers` | MCP protocol servers |
    74→| `web-apps` | `{env}-web-apps` | Standalone Nuxt frontends (no backend) |
    75→
    76→Environments: `dev`, `qa`, `prod`.
    77→
    78→---
    79→
    80→## Service Architecture Patterns
    81→
    82→When creating a migration guide for a service, first determine which pattern it follows:
    83→
    84→### Pattern 1 — Single Container (API or monolith)
    85→
    86→**Use when**: Service is a single process (Python FastAPI, Express.js monolith, etc.)
    87→**Example**: Most MCP servers, IQ Portal (Express serves both API + static SPA)
    88→
    89→```
    90→Pod:
    91→  ┌─────────────┐   ┌──────────────────┐
    92→  │  app         │   │  cloud-sql-proxy │  (optional)
    93→  │  :8080/:8000 │   │  :5432           │
    94→  └─────────────┘   └──────────────────┘
    95→```
    96→
    97→- One container serves everything
    98→- Single K8s Service, dual ingress if the service has both API and browser users
    99→- Cloud SQL sidecar if needed (opt-in)
   100→- IQ Portal follows this pattern — Express.js serves API on `/api/*` and static Nuxt SPA on `/*`, all on port 8080
   101→
   102→### Pattern 2 — Multi-Container Pod (Frontend + Backend)
   103→
   104→**Use when**: Service has separate frontend (Nuxt SSR) and backend (FastAPI) processes that are tightly coupled
   105→**Example**: HR Fraud (Nuxt 3 SSR + FastAPI + cloud-sql-proxy)
   106→
   107→```
   108→Pod:
   109→  ┌──────────────┐   ┌──────────────┐   ┌──────────────────┐
   110→  │  frontend     │   │  backend      │   │  cloud-sql-proxy │
   111→  │  (Nuxt SSR)   │   │  (FastAPI)    │   │  :5432           │
   112→  │  :3000        │──▶│  :8000        │──▶│                  │
   113→  └──────────────┘   └──────────────┘   └──────────────────┘
   114→     localhost:8000       localhost:5432
   115→```
   116→
   117→- Frontend calls backend on `localhost:8000` (zero network hop)
   118→- Two K8s Services on the same pod: one for Kong (API), one for Gateway API (frontend)
   119→- Atomic deploys — frontend and backend always update together
   120→- Two Docker images built from same repo, both tags updated in one gitops commit
   121→
   122→### Pattern 3 — Standalone Frontend
   123→
   124→**Use when**: Nuxt/Vue SPA with no backend process (API served by another service)
   125→**Example**: HR Portal (frontend-only, calls external APIs)
   126→
   127→```
   128→Pod:
   129→  ┌──────────────┐
   130→  │  frontend     │
   131→  │  (Nuxt)       │
   132→  │  :3000        │
   133→  └──────────────┘
   134→```
   135→
   136→- Single container, single Service
   137→- Gateway API + IAP ingress only (no Kong)
   138→- Namespace: `{env}-web-apps`
   139→
   140→### Pattern 4 — Python MCP Server
   141→
   142→**Use when**: MCP protocol server (most common service type, ~14 services)
   143→**Example**: `kon-mcp-gateway`, `kon-mcp-ragengine`, etc.
   144→
   145→```
   146→Pod:
   147→  ┌──────────────┐
   148→  │  app          │
   149→  │  (FastMCP)    │
   150→  │  :8000        │
   151→  └──────────────┘
   152→```
   153→
   154→- Single container, port 8000
   155→- Kong ingress only (no browser frontend)
   156→- Namespace: `{env}-mcp-servers`
   157→- Lightweight resources (100m/256Mi)
   158→- Most have no database — those that do get Cloud SQL sidecar
   159→
   160→---
   161→
   162→## Standard Migration Guide Structure
   163→
   164→When asked to create a migration guide for a service, follow this structure (derived from the IQ Portal guide):
   165→
   166→### Investigation Phase
   167→
   168→Before writing, explore the service directory thoroughly:
   169→
   170→1. **Directory structure** — full tree
   171→2. **Dockerfile(s)** — base images, stages, security posture
   172→3. **docker-compose.yml / cloudbuild.yaml** — current orchestration and CI
   173→4. **Backend stack** — framework, language version, config pattern, database connection
   174→5. **Frontend** — framework, build output (SSR vs SPA vs static), API base URL config
   175→6. **Environment variables** — `.env.example`, `config.py`/`constants.ts`, all `process.env`/`os.environ` refs
   176→7. **Database** — migration system (Alembic, custom SQL, Prisma), connection method (Cloud SQL socket, local Postgres, Firestore)
   177→8. **GCP service dependencies** — Cloud Storage, Vertex AI, Pub/Sub, Cloud Tasks, Secret Manager, Firestore
   178→9. **Health endpoints** — path, what they check, suitability for K8s probes
   179→10. **Existing CI/CD** — Cloud Build, GitHub Actions, Bitbucket triggers
   180→
   181→### Guide Sections (10 steps)
   182→
   183→| Step | Title | Content |
   184→|------|-------|---------|
   185→| Header | Service metadata | Service name mapping, target org, phase, status |
   186→| 1 | Current State Assessment | Architecture diagram, tech stack table, GCP dependencies table |
   187→| 2 | Target State | Pod diagram with containers, ingress paths, key differences table |
   188→| 3 | Pre-requisites | Checklist of platform, IAM, and infrastructure requirements |
   189→| Step 1 | Create New Repository | Create from template, copy source, remove Cloud Build config, add CODEOWNERS + .dockerignore |
   190→| Step 2 | Application Code Changes | **Only changes required for K8s** — health endpoints, connection string adjustments, env var updates. List every file that changes and why. Most services need minimal/zero code changes. |
   191→| Step 3 | Dockerfile Changes | Pin base image digests, remove Docker HEALTHCHECK (K8s probes replace it), other security hardening |
   192→| Step 4 | Helm Chart | `chart/Chart.yaml` + `chart/values.yaml` using base chart dependency. Include: containers, probes, resources, cloudSqlProxy, serviceAccount, ingress, networkPolicy, security contexts, volumes |
   193→| Step 5 | GitHub Actions CI | `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling the shared standard pipeline. Build args, env-specific variables, branch→env mapping |
   194→| Step 6 | Secrets Migration | Map every secret from Cloud Run / Secret Manager to ExternalSecret CRD keys |
   195→| Step 7 | GitOps Configuration | Environment values files in `kix-platform-infrastructure` for dev/qa/prod |
   196→| Step 8 | Database Migration | Strategy for schema (reuse existing DB vs fresh), migration runner behaviour, connectivity test |
   197→| Step 9 | Verification & Smoke Tests | kubectl commands to verify pod health, probe output, service/ingress. curl commands for functional validation. |
   198→| Step 10 | DNS & Domain Cutover | New domain records, Cloud Run decommission steps |
   199→| Appendix A | Full Environment Variable Mapping | Complete table: every env var, its source on Cloud Run, source on kix, value |
   200→| Appendix B | Rollback Procedure | ArgoCD revert, kubectl rollout undo, Cloud Run fallback |
   201→
   202→---
   203→
   204→## Key Architectural Decisions
   205→
   206→Reference these when making decisions in migration guides:
   207→
   208→### Database Connectivity
   209→- **Cloud Run**: Unix socket via VPC connector (`DB_HOST=/cloudsql/project:region:instance`)
   210→- **kix**: Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar, TCP on `localhost:5432`
   211→- Most apps support both — check the DB connection code for `DATABASE_URL` or individual `DB_HOST`/`DB_PORT` vars
   212→- If the app currently hardcodes a Unix socket path, that's a code change. If it uses `DB_HOST` env var, it's just a config change.
   213→
   214→### LiteLLM / LLM Access
   215→- All LLM traffic routes through Kong: `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/llm`
   216→- If a service uses `LITELLM_ENDPOINT`, `LITELLM_BASE_URL`, or similar — change to the Kong internal URL
   217→- NetworkPolicy enforces Kong-only access to `shared-litellm` namespace
   218→
   219→### MCP Gateway Access
   220→- Services calling MCP Gateway use: `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway`
   221→- Replace any direct MCP Gateway URLs (`http://localhost:8000` or Cloud Run URLs)
   222→
   223→### Secrets
   224→- **Never in Helm values or Git.** All secrets go through External Secrets Operator → GCP Secret Manager → K8s Secrets
   225→- Non-secret config (GCP project IDs, region, model names, bucket names, URLs) can go in Helm `env` block
   226→- Secrets are mounted via `envFrom.secretRef` pointing to an ExternalSecret-managed K8s Secret
   227→
   228→### Health Probes
   229→- **Liveness**: Lightweight, app-internal only (is the process alive?). Path: `/health` or `/healthz`. No external dependency checks.
   230→- **Readiness**: Can include dependency checks (DB, GCP). Path: `/api/health` or `/readyz`. Failing readiness removes pod from service — acceptable for warm-up.
   231→- **Startup**: Use generous `failureThreshold` if the app runs migrations on boot or has slow initialization.
   232→
   233→### Ingress
   234→- **API-only services** (MCP servers, backend APIs): Kong ingress only
   235→- **Frontend-only services** (standalone Nuxt): Gateway API + IAP ingress only
   236→- **Services with both API and browser users**: Dual ingress — both Kong and Gateway API routes pointing to the same pod (different or same port)
   237→
   238→### Port Conventions
   239→| Stack | Default Port | Notes |
   240→|-------|-------------|-------|
   241→| Python FastAPI/FastMCP | 8000 | Standard across all Python services |
   242→| Node.js Express (production) | 8080 | Cloud Run convention, kept for consistency |
   243→| Nuxt SSR | 3000 | Default Nuxt port |
   244→
   245→---
   246→
   247→## Dockerfile Conventions
   248→
   249→### Python Services
   250→```dockerfile
   251→FROM python:3.11-slim@sha256:<digest> AS builder
   252→WORKDIR /app
   253→COPY requirements.txt .
   254→RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --prefix=/install -r requirements.txt
   255→
   256→FROM python:3.11-slim@sha256:<digest> AS runtime
   257→COPY --from=builder /install /usr/local
   258→COPY . .
   259→RUN adduser --disabled-password --uid 65532 appuser
   260→USER appuser
   261→ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
   262→EXPOSE 8000
   263→CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]
   264→```
   265→
   266→### Node.js Services
   267→```dockerfile
   268→FROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest> AS dependencies
   269→WORKDIR /app
   270→COPY package*.json ./
   271→RUN npm ci --omit=dev
   272→
   273→FROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest> AS builder
   274→WORKDIR /app
   275→COPY package*.json ./
   276→RUN npm ci
   277→COPY . .
   278→RUN npm run build
   279→
   280→FROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest> AS runner
   281→RUN addgroup -g 1001 -S nodejs && adduser -S nodejs -u 1001
   282→COPY --from=dependencies /app/node_modules /app/node_modules
   283→COPY --from=builder /app/.output /app/.output
   284→USER nodejs
   285→EXPOSE 8080
   286→CMD ["node", ".output/server/index.mjs"]
   287→```
   288→
   289→### Required Changes for K8s (all Dockerfiles)
   290→1. **Pin base image digests** — replace `python:3.11-slim` with `python:3.11-slim@sha256:<digest>`
   291→2. **Remove HEALTHCHECK instruction** — K8s probes replace it
   292→3. **Ensure non-root user** — `USER` instruction must be present
   293→4. **No secrets in layers** — use `--secret` mount or env vars at runtime
   294→
   295→---
   296→
   297→## CI/CD Conventions
   298→
   299→### Standard ci.yaml (every repo)
   300→```yaml
   301→name: CI
   302→on:
   303→  push:
   304→    branches: [main, develop, staging]
   305→  pull_request:
   306→    branches: [main]
   307→
   308→jobs:
   309→  pipeline:
   310→    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1
   311→    with:
   312→      service-name: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}
   313→      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile
   314→      chart-path: ./chart
   315→      python-version: "3.11"     # OR node-version: "22"
   316→    secrets: inherit
   317→```
   318→
   319→### Build Args for Frontends
   320→If the frontend embeds config at build time (e.g., `VITE_*` variables):
   321→```yaml
   322→    with:
   323→      build-args: |
   324→        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}
   325→        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}
   326→        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}
   327→```
   328→
   329→### Branch → Environment Mapping
   330→| Branch | Environment | Namespace suffix |
   331→|--------|-------------|-----------------|
   332→| `develop` | dev | `dev-*` |
   333→| `staging` | qa | `qa-*` |
   334→| `main` | prod | `prod-*` |
   335→
   336→---
   337→
   338→## Helm Chart Conventions
   339→
   340→### chart/Chart.yaml
   341→```yaml
   342→apiVersion: v2
   343→name: <service-name>           # e.g., kix-mcp-gateway
   344→description: <one-liner>
   345→version: 0.1.0
   346→type: application
   347→dependencies:
   348→  - name: base-service-chart
   349→    version: "~1.0.0"
   350→    repository: "oci://europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/helm-charts"
   351→```
   352→
   353→### chart/values.yaml Structure
   354→All values nested under `base-service-chart:` key:
   355→
   356→```yaml
   357→base-service-chart:
   358→  nameOverride: <service-name>
   359→  image:
   360→    repository: europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/docker/<service-name>
   361→    tag: "latest"  # overridden by CI
   362→  containers:
   363→    app:
   364→      port: <8000|8080|3000>
   365→      env: { ... }               # Non-secret config
   366→      envFrom:
   367→        - secretRef:
   368→            name: <service-name>-secrets
   369→      resources:
   370→        requests: { cpu: ..., memory: ... }
   371→        limits: { cpu: ..., memory: ... }
   372→  cloudSqlProxy:                  # Only if service uses Cloud SQL
   373→    enabled: true/false
   374→    instanceConnectionName: "project:region:instance"
   375→    port: 5432
   376→  serviceAccount:
   377→    create: true
   378→    annotations:
   379→      iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: <sa>@<project>.iam.gserviceaccount.com
   380→  probes:
   381→    liveness: { httpGet: { path: /health, port: <port> } }
   382→    readiness: { httpGet: { path: /readyz, port: <port> } }
   383→  autoscaling:
   384→    enabled: true
   385→    minReplicas: 1
   386→    maxReplicas: 5
   387→  services:
   388→    api:                          # Kong ingress
   389→      port: <port>
   390→      ingress:
   391→        class: kong
   392→        path: /api/<service-name>
   393→    frontend:                     # Gateway API ingress (only if browser-facing)
   394→      port: <port>
   395→      ingress:
   396→        class: gateway-api
   397→        host: <service>.app.konecta.io
   398→  podSecurityContext:
   399→    runAsNonRoot: true
   400→    seccompProfile: { type: RuntimeDefault }
   401→  containerSecurityContext:
   402→    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   403→    readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
   404→    capabilities: { drop: [ALL] }
   405→  networkPolicy:
   406→    enabled: true
   407→```
   408→
   409→---
   410→
   411→## GitOps Values Structure
   412→
   413→In `kix-platform-infrastructure/environments/{env}/{domain}/{service}.yaml`:
   414→
   415→```yaml
   416→base-service-chart:
   417→  image:
   418→    tag: "sha-abc1234"           # Updated by CI pipeline
   419→  containers:
   420→    app:
   421→      env:
   422→        # Environment-specific overrides only
   423→        LOG_LEVEL: "debug"       # dev
   424→        ALLOWED_ORIGINS: "https://<service>.dev.konecta.io"
   425→  autoscaling:
   426→    minReplicas: 1               # dev: 1, prod: 2
   427→```
   428→
   429→---
   430→
   431→## Service Inventory (In Scope)
   432→
   433→| Current Repo | New Repo | Org | Namespace Domain | Pattern | Cloud SQL | Priority |
   434→|-------------|----------|-----|-----------------|---------|-----------|----------|
   435→| `kon-iq-portal` | `kix-usecase-iq-portal` | workloads | api-services | Single container (Express monolith) | Yes (existing) | P0 (pilot) |
   436→| `kon-mcp-gateway` | `kix-mcp-gateway` | mcp-servers | mcp-servers | Single container (FastMCP) | No | P1 |

   448→| `kon-mcp-gcs` | `kix-mcp-gcs` | mcp-servers | mcp-servers | Single container (FastMCP) | No | P3 |

   451→| `kon-app-hrportal` | `kix-app-hrportal` | apps | web-apps | Standalone frontend (Nuxt) | No | P2 |
   452→| `kon-testiq` | `kix-usecase-testiq` | workloads | web-apps | Standalone frontend | No | P3 |

   455→| `kon-rag-googledrive` | `kix-usecase-rag-googledrive` | workloads | api-services | Single container (Python) | No | P3 |

   457→
   458→### Out of Scope
   459→- **CrewAI Flows & Tools** (12 repos): `kon-flow-*`, `kon-crewai-*`, `kon-crew-*`
   460→- **Infrastructure Repos** (4): `kon-gcp-platform`, `kon-argocd-*`, `kon-tic-apps-terraform`
   461→- **Config/Proxies** (2): `kon-litellm`, `kon-argocd-platform`
   462→
   463→---
   464→
   465→## Common Investigation Patterns
   466→
   467→### Finding Database Usage
   468→```
   469→# Check for Cloud SQL connections
   470→grep -r "cloudsql\|DB_HOST\|DATABASE_URL\|SQLALCHEMY\|asyncpg\|pg\b" backend/ --include="*.py" --include="*.ts"
   471→
   472→# Check for Firestore
   473→grep -r "firestore\|Firestore" backend/ --include="*.py" --include="*.ts"
   474→
   475→# Check for migration system
   476→ls backend/alembic/ 2>/dev/null    # Python (Alembic)
   477→ls backend/db/migrations/ 2>/dev/null  # Custom SQL migrations
   478→```
   479→
   480→### Finding GCP Dependencies
   481→```
   482→# Python
   483→grep -r "google.cloud\|google-cloud\|vertexai\|aiplatform" requirements.txt pyproject.toml
   484→
   485→# Node.js
   486→grep -r "@google-cloud" package.json
   487→```
   488→
   489→### Finding Health Endpoints
   490→```
   491→grep -r "health\|healthz\|readyz" backend/ --include="*.py" --include="*.ts" -l
   492→```
   493→
   494→### Finding All Environment Variables
   495→```
   496→# Python
   497→grep -roh "os\.environ\[.*?\]\|os\.getenv(.*?)" backend/ --include="*.py" | sort -u
   498→
   499→# Node.js
   500→grep -roh "process\.env\.\w+" backend/ --include="*.ts" | sort -u
   501→```
   502→
   503→---
   504→
   505→## Important Reminders
   506→
   507→1. **Read the service's CLAUDE.md first** if one exists (e.g., `kon-app-hrfraud/CLAUDE.md`). It contains service-specific conventions.
   508→2. **Minimal code changes.** The goal is to deploy the same application on K8s. Don't refactor, improve, or add features. Only change what's necessary for K8s compatibility.
   509→3. **No secrets in Git.** Every secret must go through ExternalSecret CRDs → GCP Secret Manager.
   510→4. **Nothing is production.** No downtime concerns. Services migrate to new domains.
   511→5. **IQ Portal is the reference.** When in doubt, check how it was handled in `IQ_PORTAL_MIGRATION_GUIDE.md`.
   512→6. **The blueprint is the source of truth** for architecture, pipeline, security, and namespace decisions. Don't contradict it.
   513→7. **Each service's cloudbuild.yaml gets deleted.** Replaced by `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` calling the shared pipeline.
   514→8. **Docker HEALTHCHECK instructions get removed.** K8s probes replace them.
   515→9. **Base image digests get pinned.** No mutable tags (`:latest`, `:3.11-slim`).
   516→10. **LiteLLM always goes through Kong.** Never set a direct LiteLLM URL.
   517→

    29→
    30→## 1. Current State Assessment
    31→
    32→### Architecture
    33→
    34→IQ Portal is a **single-container monolith**: an Express.js (TypeScript) backend serves both the API (`/api/*`) and a pre-generated Nuxt 4 SPA (static files from `/dist`). Everything runs on port **8080** in production.
    35→
    36→```
    37→┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
    38→│  Cloud Run (kon-iq-portal)          │
    39→│                                     │
    40→│  Express.js :8080                   │
    41→│  ├── /api/*  → API routes           │
    42→│  ├── /docs   → Swagger UI           │
    43→│  └── /*      → Static Nuxt SPA      │
    44→│                                     │
    45→│  Cloud SQL (Unix socket)            │
    46→│  /cloudsql/project:region:instance  │
    47→└─────────────────────────────────────┘
    48→```
    49→
    50→### Current Tech Stack
    51→
    52→| Component | Detail |
    53→|-----------|--------|
    54→| Runtime | Node.js 22 (Alpine) |
    55→| Frontend | Nuxt 4 (SPA, `ssr: false`, pre-generated static) |
    56→| Backend | Express 5 (TypeScript, via `tsx`) |
    57→| Database | PostgreSQL on Cloud SQL (`kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform`) |
    58→| DB Connection | Unix socket via VPC connector (`DB_HOST=/cloudsql/...`) |
    59→| Migrations | Custom SQL/TS runner (33 migrations, runs on startup) |
    60→| Auth | Google OAuth → JWT in HttpOnly cookies |
    61→| Storage | GCS (3 buckets: images, videos, RAG docs) |
    62→| AI | Vertex AI (Gemini) + RAG corpus |
    63→| Async Jobs | Cloud Tasks → kon-rag-ingestion |
    64→| Email | ChannelHub (external Cognito-auth API) |
    65→| CI/CD | Cloud Build from Bitbucket |
    66→| Port | 8080 (production), 3002 (dev) |
    67→
    68→### GCP Service Dependencies
    69→
    70→| Service | Project | Used For |
    71→|---------|---------|----------|
    72→| Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL) | `kd-lab-464110` | Primary database |
    73→| Cloud Storage | `kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev` | Image/video/RAG file storage |
    74→| Vertex AI | `kd-lab-464110` | Gemini LLM + RAG retrieval |
    75→| Cloud Tasks | `kd-lab-464110` | Async RAG document ingestion |
    76→| Secret Manager | `kd-lab-464110` | JWT secret, DB password, RAG token |
    77→
    78→---
    79→
    80→## 2. Target State
    81→
    82→```
    83→┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    84→│  kix-infrastructure GKE Cluster                                   │
    85→│                                                                   │
    86→│  Namespace: dev-api-services (then qa-api-services, prod-...)     │
    87→│                                                                   │
    88→│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐      │
    89→│  │  Pod: kix-usecase-iq-portal                             │      │
    90→│  │                                                         │      │
    91→│  │  ┌──────────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────────┐   │      │
    92→│  │  │  app (Express)   │   │  cloud-sql-proxy sidecar  │   │      │
    93→│  │  │  :8080           │──▶│  :5432 (TCP)              │   │      │
    94→│  │  │  /api/* + SPA    │   │  --private-ip             │   │      │
    95→│  │  └──────────────────┘   └──────────────────────────┘   │      │
    96→│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘      │
    97→│      │                                                            │
    98→│      ▼                                                            │
    99→│  Service: kix-usecase-iq-portal :8080                             │
   100→│      │                         │                                  │
   101→│      ▼                         ▼                                  │
   102→│  Kong Ingress              GKE Gateway API                        │
   103→│  /api/iq-portal/*          iq-portal.app.konecta.io               │
   104→│  (API consumers)           (browser users via IAP)                │
   105→└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   106→```
   107→
   108→### Key Differences from Cloud Run
   109→
   110→| Aspect | Cloud Run | kix-infrastructure |
   111→|--------|-----------|-------------------|
   112→| DB connection | Unix socket via VPC connector | Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (TCP `localhost:5432`) |
   113→| Secrets | Secret Manager direct access | External Secrets Operator → K8s Secrets |
   114→| Scaling | 0-10 instances | HPA 1-5 replicas |
   115→| Ingress | Direct HTTPS | Dual: Kong (API) + Gateway API + IAP (frontend) |
   116→| CI/CD | Cloud Build + Bitbucket | GitHub Actions + ArgoCD |
   117→| Health checks | Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK | K8s liveness/readiness probes |
   118→
   119→---
   120→
   121→## 3. Pre-requisites
   122→
   123→Before starting, ensure:
   124→
   125→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-workloads` GitHub org (write)
   126→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure` GitOps repo (write)
   127→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows` (read)
   128→- [ ] `kix-platform-modules` base Helm chart published to Artifact Registry
   129→- [ ] ArgoCD ApplicationSet configured with git directory generator
   130→- [ ] Shared Kong gateway operational in `shared-kong` namespace
   131→- [ ] GKE Gateway API + IAP configured on frontend LB
   132→- [ ] External Secrets Operator running in `shared-secrets` namespace
   133→- [ ] GCP Workload Identity pool configured for GitHub Actions OIDC
   134→- [ ] Cloud SQL instance accessible from GKE cluster (private IP, same VPC)
   135→- [ ] GCP service account for IQ Portal workload (`kix-iq-portal@<project>.iam.gserviceaccount.com`) with IAM roles:
   136→  - `roles/cloudsql.client`
   137→  - `roles/storage.objectAdmin` (for GCS buckets)
   138→  - `roles/aiplatform.user` (for Vertex AI)
   139→  - `roles/cloudtasks.enqueuer` (for Cloud Tasks)
   140→  - `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor` (for migration 00024)
   141→

   144→## Step 1 — Create the New Repository
   145→
   146→### 1.1 Create repo from template

   150→  --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \

   153→
   154→### 1.2 Clone and set up
   155→

   158→cd kix-usecase-iq-portal
   159→```
   160→
   161→### 1.3 Copy application code
   162→
   163→Copy the entire application source from the current `kon-iq-portal` repo into the new repo root. The final structure should be:
   164→
   165→```
   166→kix-usecase-iq-portal/
   167→├── backend/              # Express.js API (existing)
   168→├── src/                  # Nuxt 4 frontend (existing)
   169→├── public/               # Static assets (existing)
   170→├── scripts/              # Deployment scripts (existing)
   171→├── nuxt.config.ts        # (existing)
   172→├── package.json          # (existing)
   173→├── package-lock.json     # (existing)
   174→├── tsconfig*.json        # (existing)
   175→├── version.txt           # (existing)

   274→### 1.8 Add TechDocs stub
   275→

   277→

   279→# IQ Portal
   280→
   281→AI-powered knowledge platform for Konecta.
   282→
   283→## Architecture
   284→
   285→Single-container Express.js monolith serving a Nuxt 4 SPA and REST API.
   286→
   287→## API
   288→
   289→Swagger documentation available at `/docs` on the running service.
   290→
   291→## Runbook
   292→
   293→- Health check: `GET /health` (liveness), `GET /api/health` (readiness with DB + GCP checks)
   294→- Logs: `kubectl logs -n {env}-api-services -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app`
   295→- Database: Cloud SQL `kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform`
   296→```
   297→
   298→Also add `mkdocs.yml` in the repo root (required by TechDocs):
   299→
   300→```yaml
   301→# mkdocs.yml
   302→site_name: IQ Portal

   306→  - techdocs-core

   308→
   309→---
   310→
   311→## Step 2 — Application Code Changes
   312→
   313→### 2.1 Database connection — switch from Unix socket to TCP localhost
   314→
   315→The Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar exposes the database on `localhost:5432` (TCP), replacing the Unix socket path used on Cloud Run.
   316→
   317→**No code changes required.** The existing `database.ts` already supports both modes:
   318→
   319→```typescript
   320→// backend/db/database.ts — already handles both
   321→const pool = process.env.DATABASE_URL
   322→    ? new Pool({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL })
   323→    : new Pool({
   324→        host: process.env.DB_HOST || 'localhost',  // was /cloudsql/... on Cloud Run
   325→        port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT || '5432'),
   326→        database: process.env.DB_NAME || 'iqplatform',
   327→        user: process.env.DB_USER || 'konecta',
   328→        password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD || 'konecta'
   329→    });
   330→```
   331→
   332→On kix, the environment variables will be:
   333→```
   334→DB_HOST=localhost        # Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar
   335→DB_PORT=5432
   336→```
   337→
   338→This is the default — so the app will work without any code change.
   339→
   340→### 2.2 Add a root-level health endpoint
   341→
   342→The Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK probes `/health`, but the app only has `/api/health`. Kubernetes probes should hit a lightweight path. Add a simple `/health` alias.
   343→
   344→**File: `backend/routes/index.ts`** — add before the existing `/api/health` block:
   345→
   346→```typescript
   347→// ==================== K8S HEALTH PROBES ====================
   348→// Lightweight endpoint for Kubernetes liveness/readiness probes
   349→// Separate from /api/health which checks DB + GCP connectivity
   350→app.get('/health', (req, res) => {
   351→    res.status(200).json({ status: 'ok' });
   352→});
   353→```
   354→
   355→This keeps the existing `/api/health` (with DB + GCP checks) for detailed monitoring, while `/health` serves as a fast liveness probe.
   356→
   357→### 2.3 Graceful shutdown (already handled)
   358→
   359→The app already uses `dumb-init` for signal handling. No changes needed — Kubernetes sends `SIGTERM` which `dumb-init` forwards correctly.
   360→
   361→### 2.4 MCP Gateway URL update
   362→
   363→The app connects to MCP Gateway at `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` (default `http://localhost:8000`). On kix, this should route through Kong:
   364→
   365→```
   366→MCP_GATEWAY_URL=http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway
   367→```
   368→
   369→This is an environment variable change only (set in Helm values), no code change.
   370→
   371→### 2.5 CORS origins update
   372→
   373→`ALLOWED_ORIGINS` needs to include the new domain. This is an environment variable change only:
   374→
   375→```
   376→ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io,https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io
   377→```
   378→
   379→### 2.6 Verify Secret Manager migration dependency
   380→
   381→Migration `00024_add_rag_pipeline_token.ts` reads from GCP Secret Manager at startup:
   382→
   383→```typescript
   384→// backend/db/migrations/00024_add_rag_pipeline_token.ts
   385→const { SecretManagerServiceClient } = await import('@google-cloud/secret-manager');
   386→```
   387→
   388→This will work on kix as long as the pod's Workload Identity has `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor`. No code change needed, but the IAM binding is a pre-requisite.
   389→
   390→### 2.7 Summary of code changes
   391→
   392→| File | Change | Required |
   393→|------|--------|----------|
   394→| `backend/routes/index.ts` | Add `/health` endpoint for K8s probes | **Yes** |
   395→| `cloudbuild.yaml` | Delete | **Yes** |
   396→| All other source files | No changes | — |
   397→
   398→Everything else is handled by environment variable changes in Helm values.
   399→
   400→---
   401→
   402→## Step 3 — Dockerfile Changes
   403→
   404→The existing Dockerfile is well-structured and mostly suitable for Kubernetes. A few targeted changes:
   405→
   406→### 3.1 Pin the base image digest
   407→
   408→Replace the mutable tag with a pinned digest for supply chain security.
   409→
   410→**Change:**
   411→```dockerfile
   412→FROM node:22-alpine AS dependencies
   413→```
   414→
   415→**To:**
   416→```dockerfile
   417→# Pin to specific digest — update monthly via Renovate
   418→FROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<current-digest> AS dependencies
   419→```
   420→
   421→Apply the same change to all four `FROM node:22-alpine` lines (dependencies, dev-dependencies, builder, runner).
   422→
   423→To get the current digest:
   424→```bash
   425→docker pull node:22-alpine
   426→docker inspect --format='{{index .RepoDigests 0}}' node:22-alpine
   427→```
   428→
   429→### 3.2 Remove the HEALTHCHECK instruction
   430→
   431→Kubernetes uses its own liveness/readiness probes — the Dockerfile `HEALTHCHECK` is ignored by K8s and adds unnecessary metadata.
   432→
   433→**Remove lines 142-157:**
   434→```dockerfile
   435→# DELETE THIS BLOCK — K8s probes replace it
   436→HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=40s --retries=3 \
   437→  CMD node -e "..."
   438→```
   439→
   440→### 3.3 Make the port configurable (optional)
   441→
   442→The port is already configurable via the `PORT` env var (default 8080). No change needed.
   443→
   444→### 3.4 Summary of Dockerfile changes
   445→
   446→| Line(s) | Change | Reason |
   447→|---------|--------|--------|
   448→| All `FROM` lines | Pin digest | Supply chain security |
   449→| 142-157 | Remove `HEALTHCHECK` | K8s probes replace it |
   450→
   451→All other aspects (multi-stage build, non-root user, dumb-init, read-only permissions) are already best practice and should be kept as-is.
   452→
   453→---
   454→
   455→## Step 4 — Helm Chart
   456→
   457→Create a `chart/` directory in the repo root.
   458→
   459→### 4.1 Chart.yaml
   460→
   461→```yaml
   462→# chart/Chart.yaml
   463→apiVersion: v2
   464→name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   465→description: Konecta IQ Portal — AI Knowledge Platform
   466→version: 0.1.0
   467→type: application
   468→
   469→dependencies:
   470→  - name: base-service-chart
   471→    version: "~1.0.0"
   472→    repository: "oci://europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/helm-charts"
   473→```
   474→
   475→### 4.2 values.yaml
   476→
   477→```yaml
   478→# chart/values.yaml
   479→# Inherits from base-service-chart — only service-specific overrides here
   480→
   481→base-service-chart:
   482→  nameOverride: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   483→
   484→  image:
   485→    repository: europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/docker/kix-usecase-iq-portal
   486→    tag: "latest"  # overridden by CI pipeline with sha-<commit>
   487→    pullPolicy: IfNotPresent
   488→
   489→  # Single container serving both API + SPA on :8080
   490→  containers:
   491→    app:
   492→      port: 8080
   493→      env:
   494→        NODE_ENV: "production"
   495→        PORT: "8080"
   496→        # DB via Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (localhost TCP)
   497→        DB_HOST: "localhost"
   498→        DB_PORT: "5432"
   499→        DB_NAME: "iqplatform"
   500→        # Non-secret GCP config
   501→        GCP_PROJECT_ID: "kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev"
   502→        GCP_LOCATION: "europe-west1"
   503→        VERTEX_AI_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   504→        VERTEX_AI_LOCATION: "us-central1"
   505→        VERTEX_AI_MODEL: "gemini-2.5-flash"
   506→        RAG_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   507→        RAG_LOCATION: "europe-west4"
   508→        RAG_CORPUS_ID: "4532873024948404224"
   509→        # MCP Gateway via Kong
   510→        MCP_GATEWAY_URL: "http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway"
   511→        # Cloud Tasks
   512→        CLOUD_TASKS_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   513→        CLOUD_TASKS_QUEUE: "rag-ingestion-poc"
   514→        CLOUD_TASKS_LOCATION: "europe-west1"
   515→        # ChannelHub (non-secret)
   516→        CHANNELHUB_URL: "https://api.konecta.cloud/one-channel-hub/prd"
   517→        CHANNELHUB_LOGIN_URL: "https://ch-prd.auth.eu-west-1.amazoncognito.com"
   518→        # Logging
   519→        LOG_LEVEL: "info"
   520→      # Secrets injected from External Secrets Operator
   521→      envFrom:
   522→        - secretRef:
   523→            name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   524→      resources:
   525→        requests:
   526→          cpu: 250m
   527→          memory: 256Mi
   528→        limits:
   529→          cpu: "1"
   530→          memory: 512Mi
   531→
   532→  # Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar
   533→  cloudSqlProxy:
   534→    enabled: true
   535→    instanceConnectionName: "kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform"
   536→    port: 5432
   537→
   538→  # Service account with Workload Identity
   539→  serviceAccount:
   540→    create: true
   541→    annotations:
   542→      iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: kix-iq-portal@kd-ix-platform.iam.gserviceaccount.com
   543→
   544→  # K8s probes
   545→  probes:
   546→    liveness:
   547→      httpGet:
   548→        path: /health
   549→        port: 8080
   550→      initialDelaySeconds: 30
   551→      periodSeconds: 15
   552→      failureThreshold: 3
   553→    readiness:
   554→      httpGet:
   555→        path: /api/health
   556→        port: 8080
   557→      initialDelaySeconds: 40
   558→      periodSeconds: 10
   559→      failureThreshold: 3
   560→    startup:
   561→      httpGet:
   562→        path: /health
   563→        port: 8080
   564→      initialDelaySeconds: 10
   565→      periodSeconds: 5
   566→      failureThreshold: 12  # 60s max startup (migrations may run)
   567→
   568→  # HPA
   569→  autoscaling:
   570→    enabled: true
   571→    minReplicas: 1
   572→    maxReplicas: 5
   573→    targetCPUUtilization: 70
   574→    targetMemoryUtilization: 80
   575→
   576→  # Dual ingress: Kong for API consumers, Gateway API for browser users
   577→  services:
   578→    api:
   579→      port: 8080
   580→      ingress:
   581→        class: kong
   582→        path: /api/iq-portal
   583→        stripPath: true
   584→        annotations:
   585→          konghq.com/strip-path: "true"
   586→          konghq.com/protocols: "https"
   587→    frontend:
   588→      port: 8080
   589→      ingress:
   590→        class: gateway-api
   591→        host: iq-portal.app.konecta.io
   592→        annotations:
   593→          networking.gke.io/managed-certificates: "iq-portal-cert"
   594→
   595→  # Pod security
   596→  podSecurityContext:
   597→    runAsNonRoot: true
   598→    runAsUser: 1001
   599→    fsGroup: 1001
   600→    seccompProfile:
   601→      type: RuntimeDefault
   602→
   603→  containerSecurityContext:
   604→    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   605→    readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
   606→    capabilities:
   607→      drop: [ALL]
   608→
   609→  # Writable tmp for Node.js
   610→  volumeMounts:
   611→    - name: tmp
   612→      mountPath: /tmp
   613→  volumes:
   614→    - name: tmp
   615→      emptyDir:
   616→        sizeLimit: 100Mi
   617→
   618→  # Network policy
   619→  networkPolicy:
   620→    enabled: true
   621→    ingress:
   622→      - from:
   623→          - namespaceSelector:
   624→              matchLabels:
   625→                name: shared-kong
   626→          - namespaceSelector:
   627→              matchLabels:
   628→                name: shared-gateway
   629→        ports:
   630→          - port: 8080
   631→            protocol: TCP
   632→```
   633→
   634→---
   635→
   636→## Step 5 — GitHub Actions CI Workflow
   637→
   638→### 5.1 Standard pipeline call
   639→
   640→Create `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`:
   641→
   642→```yaml
   643→# .github/workflows/ci.yaml
   644→name: CI Pipeline
   645→
   646→on:
   647→  push:
   648→    branches: [main, develop, staging]
   649→  pull_request:
   650→    branches: [main]
   651→
   652→permissions:
   653→  contents: read
   654→  id-token: write    # OIDC for GCP Workload Identity
   655→  security-events: write  # Upload SARIF
   656→
   657→jobs:
   658→  pipeline:
   659→    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1
   660→    with:
   661→      service-name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   662→      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile
   663→      chart-path: ./chart
   664→      node-version: "22"
   665→      build-args: |
   666→        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}
   667→        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}
   668→        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}
   669→      # IQ Portal has no test suite yet — skip test stage
   670→      skip-tests: true
   671→    secrets: inherit
   672→```
   673→
   674→### 5.2 Required GitHub repo settings
   675→
   676→Configure in the repo settings:
   677→
   678→**Variables** (Settings → Secrets and Variables → Actions → Variables):
   679→| Variable | Dev | QA | Prod |
   680→|----------|-----|-----|------|
   681→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | `805102662749-...` | `805102662749-...` | `805102662749-...` |
   682→| `ENVIRONMENT` | `development` | `staging` | `production` |
   683→
   684→**Secrets** (inherited from org — no per-repo secrets needed if using shared OIDC):
   685→- `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` — org-level
   686→- `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` — org-level
   687→
   688→### 5.3 Branch → environment mapping
   689→
   690→| Branch | Environment | Namespace |
   691→|--------|-------------|-----------|
   692→| `develop` | dev | `dev-api-services` |
   693→| `staging` | qa | `qa-api-services` |
   694→| `main` | prod | `prod-api-services` |
   695→
   696→---
   697→
   698→## Step 6 — Secrets Migration
   699→
   700→### 6.1 Identify secrets
   701→
   702→Current secrets sourced from Secret Manager on Cloud Run:
   703→
   704→| Secret | GCP Secret Name | K8s Secret Key |
   705→|--------|----------------|----------------|
   706→| Database password | `db-password` | `DB_PASSWORD` |
   707→| JWT signing key | `koniq-jwt-secret` | `JWT_SECRET` |
   708→| Google OAuth client ID | `koniq-google-client-id` | `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` |
   709→| RAG pipeline token | `kon-iq-rag-client-secret` | `RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` |
   710→| ChannelHub client ID | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` |
   711→| ChannelHub client secret | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` |
   712→| ChannelHub connection ID | (terraform-managed) | `CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID` |
   713→
   714→### 6.2 Create GCP Secret Manager entries
   715→
   716→If secrets don't already exist in the kix project's Secret Manager, create them:
   717→
   718→```bash
   719→PROJECT=kd-ix-platform  # or whichever project ESO reads from
   720→
   721→# For each secret:
   722→echo -n "value" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-db-password \
   723→  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-
   724→
   725→echo -n "value" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret \
   726→  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-
   727→
   728→# ... repeat for each secret
   729→```
   730→
   731→### 6.3 ExternalSecret CRD
   732→
   733→Create `chart/templates/external-secret.yaml` (or include in base chart if templated):
   734→
   735→```yaml
   736→apiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1
   737→kind: ExternalSecret
   738→metadata:
   739→  name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   740→spec:
   741→  refreshInterval: 1h
   742→  secretStoreRef:
   743→    name: gcp-secret-store  # Shared SecretStore in namespace
   744→    kind: SecretStore
   745→  target:
   746→    name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   747→    creationPolicy: Owner
   748→  data:
   749→    - secretKey: DB_PASSWORD
   750→      remoteRef:
   751→        key: kix-iq-portal-db-password
   752→    - secretKey: DB_USER
   753→      remoteRef:
   754→        key: kix-iq-portal-db-user
   755→    - secretKey: JWT_SECRET
   756→      remoteRef:
   757→        key: kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret
   758→    - secretKey: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID
   759→      remoteRef:
   760→        key: kix-iq-portal-google-client-id
   761→    - secretKey: RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN
   762→      remoteRef:
   763→        key: kix-iq-portal-rag-pipeline-token
   764→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID
   765→      remoteRef:
   766→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-id
   767→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET
   768→      remoteRef:
   769→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-secret
   770→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID
   771→      remoteRef:
   772→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-connection-id
   773→    - secretKey: GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET
   774→      remoteRef:
   775→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-image-bucket
   776→    - secretKey: GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET
   777→      remoteRef:
   778→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-video-bucket
   779→    - secretKey: GCS_RAG_BUCKET
   780→      remoteRef:
   781→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-rag-bucket
   782→```
   783→

   866→### 7.4 ArgoCD auto-discovers

   871→
   872→## Step 8 — Database Migration
   873→
   874→### 8.1 Strategy
   875→
   876→The app runs migrations automatically on startup (`runMigrations()` in `backend/index.ts`). This is safe for the initial deployment because:
   877→
   878→- The Cloud SQL instance already exists with the schema from Cloud Run
   879→- Migrations are idempotent (tracked in `schema_migrations` table)
   880→- No schema changes are part of this migration — same database, same schema
   881→
   882→### 8.2 If using a new database
   883→
   884→If creating a fresh Cloud SQL database for kix:
   885→
   886→```bash
   887→# Connect to Cloud SQL
   888→gcloud sql connect konecta-iqplatform --project=kd-lab-464110 --user=konecta
   889→
   890→# Create database
   891→CREATE DATABASE iqplatform;
   892→```
   893→
   894→Then deploy the app — the 33 migrations will run automatically on first startup, creating the full schema and seed data.
   895→
   896→### 8.3 If sharing the existing database
   897→
   898→If pointing kix at the same Cloud SQL instance used by Cloud Run:
   899→
   900→1. Verify the `schema_migrations` table is up to date:
   901→   ```bash
   902→   # From a machine with Cloud SQL access
   903→   psql -h <cloud-sql-ip> -U konecta -d iqplatform \
   904→     -c "SELECT * FROM schema_migrations ORDER BY id"
   905→   ```
   906→2. Deploy to kix — migrations will detect all 33 have already run and skip them
   907→3. Once verified, decommission the Cloud Run service
   908→
   909→### 8.4 Cloud SQL Auth Proxy connectivity test
   910→
   911→Before deploying, verify the sidecar can reach Cloud SQL:
   912→
   913→```bash
   914→# Deploy a test pod with cloud-sql-proxy
   915→kubectl run sql-test --rm -it \
   916→  --image=gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.14.3 \
   917→  --namespace=dev-api-services \
   918→  --serviceaccount=kix-usecase-iq-portal \
   919→  -- --private-ip kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform
   920→```
   921→
   922→In another terminal:
   923→```bash
   924→kubectl exec -it sql-test -n dev-api-services -- \
   925→  psql -h localhost -U konecta -d iqplatform -c "SELECT 1"
   926→```
   927→
   928→---
   929→
   930→## Step 9 — Verification & Smoke Tests
   931→
   932→### 9.1 Deployment checklist
   933→
   934→After the first CI pipeline runs and ArgoCD syncs:
   935→
   936→```bash
   937→NAMESPACE=dev-api-services
   938→
   939→# 1. Pod is running (2/2 = app + cloud-sql-proxy)
   940→kubectl get pods -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal
   941→
   942→# 2. Both containers healthy
   943→kubectl describe pod -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal | grep -A5 "Conditions"
   944→
   945→# 3. Liveness probe passing
   946→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app | grep "running on"
   947→
   948→# 4. Cloud SQL proxy connected
   949→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c cloud-sql-proxy | grep "ready"
   950→
   951→# 5. Migrations ran
   952→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app | grep "migration"
   953→
   954→# 6. Service exists
   955→kubectl get svc -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal
   956→
   957→# 7. Kong route active
   958→kubectl get ingress -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal-api
   959→
   960→# 8. Gateway route active
   961→kubectl get httproute -n $NAMESPACE kix-usecase-iq-portal-frontend
   962→```
   963→
   964→### 9.2 Functional smoke tests
   965→
   966→```bash
   967→BASE_URL="https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io"
   968→
   969→# Health check (lightweight)
   970→curl -s $BASE_URL/health | jq .
   971→# Expected: {"status":"ok"}
   972→
   973→# Health check (full — DB + GCP)
   974→curl -s $BASE_URL/api/health | jq .
   975→# Expected: {"status":"ok","checks":{"database":"ok","gcp":"ok"}}
   976→
   977→# Version endpoint
   978→curl -s $BASE_URL/api/version | jq .
   979→# Expected: {"version":"0.9.1","commit":"<sha>"}
   980→
   981→# Frontend loads (SPA index.html)
   982→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/
   983→# Expected: 200
   984→
   985→# Swagger docs
   986→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/docs
   987→# Expected: 200 (or 301 redirect)
   988→
   989→# API auth (should return 401 without token)
   990→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/api/agents
   991→# Expected: 401
   992→```
   993→
   994→### 9.3 Kong API route test
   995→
   996→```bash
   997→KONG_URL="https://api.konecta.io"
   998→
   999→# Via Kong (API consumers path)
  1000→curl -s $KONG_URL/api/iq-portal/health | jq .
  1001→# Expected: {"status":"ok"}
  1002→```
  1003→
  1004→---
  1005→
  1006→## Step 10 — DNS & Domain Cutover
  1007→
  1008→Since nothing is production and services will migrate to new domains, this is straightforward.
  1009→
  1010→### 10.1 New domains
  1011→
  1012→| Path | Domain | Managed By |
  1013→|------|--------|-----------|
  1014→| Frontend (browser) | `iq-portal.app.konecta.io` | GKE Gateway API + IAP |
  1015→| Frontend (dev) | `iq-portal.dev.konecta.io` | GKE Gateway API |
  1016→| API (machine) | `api.konecta.io/api/iq-portal` | Kong |
  1017→
  1018→### 10.2 DNS records
  1019→
  1020→```
  1021→iq-portal.app.konecta.io  → A record → Frontend LB external IP
  1022→iq-portal.dev.konecta.io  → A record → Frontend LB external IP (dev)
  1023→api.konecta.io            → A record → Kong LB external IP (shared)
  1024→```
  1025→
  1026→### 10.3 Decommission Cloud Run
  1027→
  1028→Once kix deployment is verified:
  1029→
  1030→```bash
  1031→# Stop Cloud Run service (no traffic)
  1032→gcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \
  1033→  --region=europe-west1 \
  1034→  --max-instances=0
  1035→
  1036→# After confidence period (1-2 weeks), delete
  1037→gcloud run services delete kon-iq-portal \
  1038→  --region=europe-west1
  1039→```
  1040→
  1041→---
  1042→
  1043→## Appendix A — Full Environment Variable Mapping
  1044→
  1045→Complete mapping of every environment variable from Cloud Run to kix Helm values:
  1046→
  1047→| Variable | Source on Cloud Run | Source on kix | Value / Notes |
  1048→|----------|-------------------|--------------|---------------|
  1049→| `NODE_ENV` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `production` |
  1050→| `PORT` | Default (8080) | Helm `env` | `8080` |
  1051→| `ENVIRONMENT` | Cloud Build substitution | Helm `env` | `production` / `development` |
  1052→| `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` | Terraform | Helm `env` | New domain(s) |
  1053→| `DB_HOST` | Terraform (`/cloudsql/...`) | Helm `env` | `localhost` (sidecar) |
  1054→| `DB_PORT` | Default (5432) | Helm `env` | `5432` |
  1055→| `DB_NAME` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `iqplatform` |
  1056→| `DB_USER` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | `konecta` |
  1057→| `DB_PASSWORD` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1058→| `DB_POOL_MIN` | Default (2) | Helm `env` | `2` |
  1059→| `DB_POOL_MAX` | Default (10) | Helm `env` | `10` |
  1060→| `JWT_SECRET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1061→| `JWT_COOKIE_MAX_AGE_DAYS` | Default (7) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `7` |
  1062→| `JWT_EXPIRY` | Default (7d) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `7d` |
  1063→| `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1064→| `DEFAULT_ALLOWED_DOMAIN` | Default | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `konecta.com` |
  1065→| `GCP_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev` |
  1066→| `GCP_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west4` |
  1067→| `VERTEX_AI_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |
  1068→| `VERTEX_AI_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `us-central1` |
  1069→| `VERTEX_AI_MODEL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `gemini-2.5-flash` |
  1070→| `RAG_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |
  1071→| `RAG_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west4` |
  1072→| `RAG_CORPUS_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `4532873024948404224` |
  1073→| `RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1074→| `RAG_SECRET_NAME` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kon-iq-rag-client-secret` |
  1075→| `GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |
  1076→| `GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |
  1077→| `GCS_RAG_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | bucket name |
  1078→| `MAX_FILE_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB` | Default (100) | Helm `env` (if override needed) | `100` |
  1079→| `CLOUD_TASKS_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `kd-lab-464110` |
  1080→| `CLOUD_TASKS_QUEUE` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `rag-ingestion-poc` |
  1081→| `CLOUD_TASKS_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `europe-west1` |
  1082→| `CLOUD_TASKS_HANDLER_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` (per-env) | New kix ingestion URL |
  1083→| `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` | Default | Helm `env` | `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway` |
  1084→| `CHANNELHUB_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `https://api.konecta.cloud/one-channel-hub/prd` |
  1085→| `CHANNELHUB_LOGIN_URL` | Terraform | Helm `env` | `https://ch-prd.auth.eu-west-1.amazoncognito.com` |
  1086→| `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1087→| `CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1088→| `CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret | (secret) |
  1089→| `LOG_LEVEL` | Default (info) | Helm `env` | `info` (prod) / `debug` (dev) |
  1090→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | Cloud Build arg | CI build arg | `805102662749-...` |
  1091→| `NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT` | Cloud Build arg | CI build arg | `production` / `development` |
  1092→
  1093→**Build-time only** (not needed at runtime):
  1094→| Variable | Source |
  1095→|----------|--------|
  1096→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | GitHub Actions variable → `--build-arg` |
  1097→| `NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT` | GitHub Actions variable → `--build-arg` |
  1098→| `COMMIT_SHA` | `${{ github.sha }}` → `--build-arg` |
  1099→
  1100→---
  1101→
  1102→## Appendix B — Rollback Procedure
  1103→
  1104→### Automatic (ArgoCD)
  1105→
  1106→ArgoCD self-heals. To roll back:
  1107→
  1108→```bash
  1109→# Revert the image tag in the gitops repo
  1110→cd kix-platform-infrastructure
  1111→git revert HEAD
  1112→git push
  1113→# ArgoCD syncs the previous image tag automatically
  1114→```
  1115→
  1116→### Manual (kubectl)
  1117→
  1118→```bash
  1119→# Roll back to previous ReplicaSet
  1120→kubectl rollout undo deployment/kix-usecase-iq-portal -n dev-api-services
  1121→
  1122→# Verify
  1123→kubectl rollout status deployment/kix-usecase-iq-portal -n dev-api-services
  1124→```
  1125→
  1126→### Fall back to Cloud Run
  1127→
  1128→If the entire kix deployment is problematic:
  1129→
  1130→```bash
  1131→# Re-enable Cloud Run
  1132→gcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \
  1133→  --region=europe-west1 \
  1134→  --max-instances=10
  1135→
  1136→# Point DNS back (if changed)
  1137→# Update iq-portal.app.konecta.io → Cloud Run URL
  1138→```
  1139→
  1140→Since nothing is production and Cloud Run will remain available during migration, this is low risk.
  1141→

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-tic/CLAUDE.md",
  "old_string": "## Common Investigation Patterns",
  "new_string": "## Backstage Integration\n\nEvery service migrated to kix-infrastructure must include Backstage artifacts for the internal developer portal.\n\n### Required Files (every repo)\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `catalog-info.yaml` | Service descriptor — registers the service in the Backstage Software Catalog |\n| `mkdocs.yml` | TechDocs configuration — required for Backstage to render documentation |\n| `docs/index.md` | Service documentation — rendered in the Backstage \"Docs\" tab |\n\n### catalog-info.yaml Template\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: backstage.io/v1alpha1\nkind: Component\nmetadata:\n  name: <service-name>           # e.g., kix-mcp-gateway\n  description: <one-liner>\n  annotations:\n    github.com/project-slug: <org>/<service-name>\n    argocd/app-name: <service-name>\n    backstage.io/kubernetes-id: <service-name>\n    backstage.io/kubernetes-namespace: dev-<domain>,qa-<domain>,prod-<domain>\n    backstage.io/techdocs-ref: dir:.\n    sonarqube.org/project-key: <service-name>\n  tags:\n    - <language>       # python, nodejs\n    - <framework>      # fastapi, express, nuxt, fastmcp\n    - cloud-sql        # only if service uses Cloud SQL\n  links:\n    - url: https://<service>.app.konecta.io\n      title: Production\n      icon: dashboard\nspec:\n  type: service\n  lifecycle: production\n  owner: group:<team-name>\n  system: kix-platform\n  dependsOn:                     # list GCP resources and other services\n    - resource:<resource-name>\n    - component:<dependency>\n```\n\n### mkdocs.yml Template\n\n```yaml\nsite_name: <Service Display Name>\nnav:\n  - Home: index.md\nplugins:\n  - techdocs-core\n```\n\n### TechDocs docs/index.md\n\nInclude at minimum:\n- Service description and architecture pattern\n- API documentation link (if applicable)\n- Runbook: health check paths, log commands, database connection info\n\n### Backstage Plugins Active\n\n| Plugin | What It Shows |\n|--------|--------------|\n| Software Catalog | Service metadata, ownership, dependencies |\n| TechDocs | Rendered docs from `docs/` directory |\n| ArgoCD | Deployment status, sync state, revision history |\n| Kubernetes | Running pods, events, resource usage |\n| GitHub Actions | CI pipeline runs, build status |\n| SonarQube | Code quality, coverage, security hotspots |\n| Dependency-Track | SCA vulnerability tracking |\n\n### Onboarding New Services via Backstage\n\n**Preferred path**: Use Backstage Software Templates (Scaffolder) at `https://backstage.konecta.io/create` to create new service repos. Templates are available for: `python-api`, `nodejs-api`, `mcp-server`, `nuxt-frontend`.\n\n**Fallback**: Create manually from `kix-platform-service-template` and add `catalog-info.yaml` + `docs/` by hand. Backstage discovers new repos within 30 minutes via org-wide catalog scanning.\n\n---\n\n## Common Investigation Patterns"
}
```
